#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 A52 PRECODE: actual native S5 load + SQLite single writer + STOP race stress.
This is an intentionally bounded *experiment*, not UNO production code.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import signal
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

VERSION = 'UNO_R41_A52_S5_STOP_CONCURRENCY_STRESS_V1'
MIB = 1024 * 1024
BATTERY_MIN = 40
BATTERY_TEMP_MAX = 375  # 37.5 C, battery sensor only
MEMAVAIL_MIN_KB = 400000


def nint(p):
    try: return int(Path(p).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError, ValueError): return None


def avail_kb():
    try:
        for line in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines():
            if line.startswith('MemAvailable:'): return int(line.split()[1])
    except (OSError, ValueError, IndexError): pass
    return None


def telemetry():
    return (nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),
            nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'), avail_kb())


def safety(strict=False):
    b, t, m = telemetry()
    if strict and (b is None or t is None or not 150 <= t <= 800 or m is None):
        return 'required_safety_telemetry_missing_or_invalid'
    if b is not None and b < BATTERY_MIN: return 'battery_below_40_percent'
    if t is not None and 150 <= t <= 800 and t >= BATTERY_TEMP_MAX: return 'battery_sensor_ge_37_5_C'
    if m is not None and m < MEMAVAIL_MIN_KB: return 'MemAvailable_below_400000_kB'
    return None


def open_db(db):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db), isolation_level=None, timeout=3.0)
    con.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=3000')
    con.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    con.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return con


def init_db(db):
    c = open_db(db)
    try:
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE trials(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, epoch INTEGER NOT NULL, stopped INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE receipts(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY, trial INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE used(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY)')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE p_steps(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY)')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE memory(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY)')
    finally: c.close()


def child_controller(db, ctrl, jobs):
    c = None
    history = {}
    try:
        c = open_db(db)
        while True:
            # High-priority control lane, independently of bounded data queue.
            if ctrl.poll(0):
                msg = ctrl.recv()
                cmd = msg[0]
                if cmd == 'START':
                    tid = msg[1]
                    c.execute('INSERT INTO trials(id,epoch,stopped) VALUES(?,1,0)', (tid,))
                    history[tid] = []
                    ctrl.send(('STARTED', tid, time.monotonic_ns()))
                elif cmd == 'STOP':
                    tid, sent = msg[1:3]
                    recv = time.monotonic_ns()
                    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                    try:
                        c.execute('UPDATE trials SET stopped=1, epoch=epoch+1 WHERE id=? AND stopped=0', (tid,))
                        c.execute('COMMIT')
                    except BaseException:
                        if c.in_transaction: c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                        raise
                    latch = time.monotonic_ns()
                    ctrl.send(('STOP_ACK', tid, sent, recv, latch))
                elif cmd == 'QUERY':
                    tid = msg[1]
                    state = c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM trials WHERE id=?', (tid,)).fetchone()
                    tickets = [r[0] for r in c.execute('SELECT ticket FROM receipts WHERE trial=?', (tid,))]
                    credit = len(tickets)
                    other = [sum(c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM '+tbl+' WHERE ticket=?', (ticket,)).fetchone() is not None
                                 for ticket in tickets) for tbl in ('used','p_steps','memory')]
                    ctrl.send(('QUERY_OK', tid, state, credit, other, tuple(history.get(tid, ()))))
                elif cmd == 'CLOSE':
                    ctrl.send(('CLOSED',)); return
                else: raise RuntimeError('bad_control_message_'+str(cmd))
                continue
            if not jobs.poll(0.005):
                continue
            try:
                job = jobs.recv()
            except EOFError:
                return
            if not isinstance(job, tuple) or len(job) != 3 or job[0] != 'TICKET':
                raise RuntimeError('unexpected_data_message')
            _, tid, ticket = job
            # All state-changing database ops, including synthetic P credit, use one writer.
            c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            try:
                state = c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM trials WHERE id=?', (tid,)).fetchone()
                if state is None or state[0] != 1 or state[1] != 0:
                    c.execute('ROLLBACK'); continue
                if c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM used WHERE ticket=?', (ticket,)).fetchone():
                    c.execute('ROLLBACK'); continue
                c.execute('INSERT INTO receipts VALUES (?,?)', (ticket, tid))
                for tbl in ('used','p_steps','memory'):
                    c.execute('INSERT INTO '+tbl+' VALUES (?)', (ticket,))
                # Last cooperative priority probe: a later STOP can still race with COMMIT.
                if ctrl.poll(0):
                    c.execute('ROLLBACK'); continue
                c.execute('COMMIT')
                history.setdefault(tid, []).append(time.monotonic_ns())
            except BaseException:
                if c.in_transaction: c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                raise
    except BaseException as ex:
        try: ctrl.send(('CHILD_ERROR', type(ex).__name__, str(ex)[:160]))
        except (OSError, BrokenPipeError): pass
    finally:
        if c is not None: c.close()
        ctrl.close()


def memory_hold(mib, quit_event, ready):
    try:
        buf = bytearray(mib * MIB)
        for i in range(0, len(buf), 4096): buf[i] = (i // 4096) & 255
        ready.put(('READY', os.getpid(), mib), timeout=2)
        while not quit_event.wait(0.15):
            # touch a small number of pages so memory remains resident
            for i in range(0, len(buf), 4096 * 128): buf[i] ^= 1
    except BaseException as e:
        try: ready.put(('ERROR', str(e)[:160]), timeout=1)
        except Exception: pass


def s5_worker(binary, quit_event, result_queue):
    trials = 0
    gmac = []
    problems = []
    while not quit_event.is_set():
        try:
            run = subprocess.run([binary, 'rank', 'full', '3.0'],
                                 capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=6.0)
            if run.returncode != 0 or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS', run.stdout) or 'MEASURE events=' not in run.stdout:
                problems.append('exit='+str(run.returncode)+' '+(run.stderr or run.stdout)[-100:])
                break
            found = re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)', run.stdout)
            if found: gmac.append(float(found.group(1)))
            trials += 1
        except BaseException as e:
            problems.append(type(e).__name__+':'+str(e)[:100]);break
    try: result_queue.put((os.getpid(), trials, min(gmac) if gmac else None,
                          sum(gmac)/len(gmac) if gmac else None, problems), timeout=3)
    except Exception: pass


def binary_on_device(log):
    lab = Path.home() / 'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'
    exe = lab / 'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if exe.is_file() and os.access(exe, os.X_OK):
        return str(exe)
    src = lab / 'uno_r41_s5_operator.c'
    if src.is_file():
        log('BUILD native S5 from prior experiment source='+str(src))
        try:
            p = subprocess.run(['clang','-std=c11','-O3','-fno-fast-math',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],
                               capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=35)
        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as e:
            raise RuntimeError('cannot_build_native_S5_'+str(e))
        if p.returncode: raise RuntimeError('clang_failed: '+p.stderr[-300:])
        return str(exe)
    raise RuntimeError('Native S5 benchmark not found. Run existing UNO_A52_R41_F_OPERATOR_BENCH.sh first; it creates '+str(src))


def req(ch, m, expected, timeout=8.0):
    ch.send(m)
    if not ch.poll(timeout): raise RuntimeError('controller_timeout_'+str(expected))
    reply = ch.recv()
    if reply[0] == 'CHILD_ERROR': raise RuntimeError(str(reply))
    if reply[0] != expected: raise RuntimeError('unexpected_reply_'+str(reply))
    return reply


def percentile(values, pct):
    xs = sorted(values)
    if not xs: return 0
    return xs[max(0, int((len(xs) * pct + 99) // 100) - 1)]


def stage(name, workers, hold_mib, seconds, ctx, binary, home, log, serial, strict_safety):
    db = home / (name+'.sqlite3')
    init_db(db)
    job_reader, job_writer = ctx.Pipe(duplex=False)
    parent, child = ctx.Pipe(duplex=True)
    proc = ctx.Process(target=child_controller, args=(str(db), child, job_reader), name='UNO_LAB_CONTROLLER')
    quit_event = ctx.Event()
    results = ctx.Queue()
    mem_ready = ctx.Queue()
    mem_proc = None
    burns = []
    proc.start(); child.close(); job_reader.close()
    try:
        if hold_mib:
            mem_proc = ctx.Process(target=memory_hold, args=(hold_mib, quit_event, mem_ready))
            mem_proc.start()
            msg = mem_ready.get(timeout=5.0)
            if msg[0] != 'READY': raise RuntimeError('memory_holder_'+str(msg))
            log('MEMORY_PRESSURE allocated_touched_MiB=%d pid=%d' % (hold_mib,msg[1]))
        for _ in range(workers):
            p=ctx.Process(target=s5_worker,args=(binary,quit_event,results))
            p.start();burns.append(p)
        log('STAGE_BEGIN name=%s s5_workers=%d resident_MiB=%d min_duration_s=%d' %
            (name,workers,hold_mib,seconds))
        started=time.monotonic()
        ack=[];receive=[];latch=[]; window=0; pre=0; post_ack=0; poststop_changes=0; anomalies=[]
        trials=0; submissions=0
        until=started + seconds
        target_trials = max(8, round(seconds * 3))
        while time.monotonic() < until and trials < target_trials:
            cycle_begin = time.monotonic()
            stop_reason=safety(strict_safety)
            if stop_reason:
                log('SAFETY_ABORT stage=%s reason=%s' % (name,stop_reason))
                raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+stop_reason)
            tid=serial+trials
            req(parent,('START',tid),'STARTED')
            # A full queue of concurrent work is intended: STOP must bypass the data queue.
            for i in range(32):
                job_writer.send(('TICKET',tid,'t%06d_%04d' % (tid,i)))
                submissions += 1
            # Deliberately vary interleaving so STOP can arrive during SQLite COMMIT.
            time.sleep((trials % 7) * 0.00029)
            stop_sent=time.monotonic_ns()
            reply=req(parent,('STOP',tid,stop_sent),'STOP_ACK')
            t_ack=time.monotonic_ns()
            _,got_id,sent_ts,recv_ts,latch_ts=reply
            if got_id!=tid or sent_ts!=stop_sent:raise RuntimeError('wrong_stop_ACK')
            ack.append((t_ack-stop_sent)/1e6)
            receive.append((recv_ts-stop_sent)/1e6)
            latch.append((latch_ts-stop_sent)/1e6)
            # Verify post-STOP new work cannot advance this trial's synthetic P / receipts.
            before=req(parent,('QUERY',tid),'QUERY_OK')
            _,_,state,credits,other,events=before
            if state != (2,1): anomalies.append('not_stopped_'+str(tid))
            if any(x != credits for x in other): anomalies.append('atomicity_'+str(tid))
            pre+=credits
            window+=sum(stop_sent < t <= latch_ts for t in events)
            post_ack+=sum(t > t_ack for t in events)
            for x in range(4):
                job_writer.send(('TICKET',tid,'late_%06d_%d' % (tid,x)))
            # Let the old queued tickets drain while next trial is prepared.
            time.sleep(0.012)
            after=req(parent,('QUERY',tid),'QUERY_OK')
            if after[3] != credits:
                poststop_changes += 1
                anomalies.append('post_stop_commit_'+str(tid))
            if after[2] != (2,1): anomalies.append('stop_not_durable_'+str(tid))
            trials+=1
            if trials % 20 == 0:
                b,t,m=telemetry()
                log('PROGRESS stage=%s trials=%d ack_max_ms=%.3f race_window_commits=%d battery=%s temp_deciC=%s avail_kB=%s' %
                    (name,trials,max(ack),window,b,t,m))
            # Spread STOP races over the entire native S5 load interval.
            time.sleep(max(0.0, seconds / target_trials - (time.monotonic() - cycle_begin)))
        while time.monotonic() < until:
            why = safety(strict_safety)
            if why: raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+why)
            time.sleep(0.20)
        # Ensure final latched trial remains intact after the controller is force-killed.
        proc.kill();proc.join(timeout=3)
        if proc.is_alive(): raise RuntimeError('controller_not_terminated')
        con = open_db(db)
        try:
            integrity=con.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
            total=con.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM receipts').fetchone()[0]
            n_p=con.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM p_steps').fetchone()[0]
            n_mem=con.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM memory').fetchone()[0]
            n_used=con.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM used').fetchone()[0]
            last=con.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM trials WHERE id=?',(serial+trials-1,)).fetchone()
            ticket_sets=[{r[0] for r in con.execute('SELECT ticket FROM '+table)} for table in ('receipts','used','p_steps','memory')]
            ticket_sets_match=all(ts==ticket_sets[0] for ts in ticket_sets)
        finally:con.close()
        if not (integrity=='ok' and total==n_p==n_mem==n_used and ticket_sets_match and last==(2,1)):
            anomalies.append('crash_recovery_integrity_or_atomicity')
        # Check that real S5 native processes actually computed and validated.
        quit_event.set()
        for p in burns:
            p.join(timeout=4.7)
            if p.is_alive():
                p.kill();p.join(timeout=2)
                anomalies.append('S5_worker_forced_kill_'+str(p.pid))
        got_reports=[]
        for _ in burns:
            try:
                got_reports.append(results.get(timeout=1.5))
            except queue.Empty:
                anomalies.append('S5_worker_missing_report')
        for pid, runs, mn, avg, problems in got_reports:
            log('S5_WORKER pid=%d native_3s_runs=%d min_GMAC_s=%s mean_GMAC_s=%s errors=%s' %
                (pid,runs,mn,avg,problems))
            if runs < 1 or problems:
                anomalies.append('S5_native_execution_problem_'+str(pid))
        if post_ack:
            anomalies.append('commit_after_STOP_ACK_'+str(post_ack))
        log('STOP_STATS stage=%s trials=%d submitted=%d committed_pre_or_race=%d '
            'ack_p50_ms=%.3f ack_p95_nearest_rank_ms=%.3f ack_max_ms=%.3f '
            'receive_max_ms=%.3f latch_max_ms=%.3f '
            'COMMIT_DONE_AFTER_STOP_REQUEST_BEFORE_LATCH=%d '
            'COMMIT_DONE_AFTER_ACK=%d post_stop_count_change=%d db_integrity=%s atomic_counts=%d/%d/%d/%d' %
            (name,trials,submissions,pre,percentile(ack,50),percentile(ack,95),max(ack),
             max(receive),max(latch),window,post_ack,poststop_changes,integrity,total,n_p,n_mem,n_used))
        if anomalies:
            log('STAGE_FAIL stage=%s anomalous=%s' % (name,','.join(anomalies[:15])))
            return False, window
        log('STAGE_PASS stage=%s synthetic_post_latch_commits=0 persisted_stop_after_crash=YES '
            'stop_request_race_window=%d (not_a_proven_strict_STOP_policy)' % (name,window))
        return True, window
    finally:
        quit_event.set()
        for p in burns:
            p.join(timeout=4.5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(timeout=2)
        if mem_proc is not None:
            mem_proc.join(timeout=2)
            if mem_proc.is_alive():mem_proc.kill();mem_proc.join(timeout=1)
        if proc.is_alive():
            try:req(parent,('CLOSE',),'CLOSED',timeout=1)
            except (OSError,EOFError,RuntimeError):pass
            proc.join(timeout=1)
            if proc.is_alive():proc.kill();proc.join(timeout=1)
        parent.close();job_writer.close()
        results.cancel_join_thread();results.close()
        mem_ready.cancel_join_thread();mem_ready.close()


def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Bounded S5 + STOP concurrency stress (NOT production)')
    p.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='Local smoke test only; not a stress result')
    args=p.parse_args()
    downloads=Path.home()/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=Path.home()
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/('UNO_A52_S5_STOP_CONCURRENCY_STRESS_R41_'+stamp+'.txt')
    fp=report.open('x',encoding='utf-8')
    def log(s):
        print(s,flush=True)
        fp.write(s+'\n');fp.flush()
    try:
        log('UNO / UNOVYRA R4.1 -- REAL NATIVE S5 UNDER STOP+SQLITE CONCURRENCY '+VERSION)
        log('Experimental C256R32 native FP32 S5 + parallel CPU load, 32-ticket queue, one SQLite writer, STOP priority lane, memory pressure, crash-readback.')
        log('NOT: true Android STOP, user auth, Android actions, real P weights, hard real-time guarantee, production code.')
        log('Strict distinction: STOP sent != STOP received != STOP latched; commits in between are RACE_WINDOW observations, not automatic protocol violations.')
        log('SAFETY: battery>=40%; battery temp<37.5C; MemAvailable>=400000 kB; max four native S5 processes + 64 MiB holder.')
        log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
        b,t,m=telemetry();log('PRECHECK battery=%s temp_deciC=%s MemAvailable_kB=%s' % (b,t,m))
        strict_safety=not args.quick
        why=safety(strict_safety)
        if why:
            log('RESULT SKIPPED_SAFETY '+why);return 3
        binary=binary_on_device(log)
        log('NATIVE_BINARY '+binary)
        home=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_s5_stop_concurrency_lab'/stamp
        home.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False,mode=0o700)
        ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
        configs=[('IDLE',0,0,2),('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,4),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,5)] if args.quick else [
            ('IDLE',0,0,8),('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,20),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,28)]
        good=True
        total_race_windows=0
        for i,(name,cores,mib,dur) in enumerate(configs):
            if safety(strict_safety):
                log('RESULT ABORT_SAFETY before_stage='+name+' reason='+str(safety(strict_safety)))
                return 3
            ok, race_count=stage(name,cores,mib,dur,ctx,binary,home,log,(i+1)*100000,strict_safety)
            total_race_windows+=race_count
            good=good and ok
            b,t,m=telemetry();log('STAGE_TELEMETRY name=%s battery=%s temp_deciC=%s MemAvailable_kB=%s' % (name,b,t,m))
            if not ok:break
        result='FAIL_INVARIANT' if not good else ('PASS_WITH_STOP_REQUEST_RACE_WINDOW_FINDINGS' if total_race_windows else 'PASS_NO_RACE_WINDOW_OBSERVED')
        log('RESULT '+result+' stop_request_window_commits='+str(total_race_windows))
        log('NOT_CLEARED: no strict guarantee that the first user STOP request precedes all subsequent durable commits.')
        log('LIMIT: no power-cycle durability, no real OS dispatch, no model trained; high CPU work is synthetic C256R32 S5 with repeatable fake weights.')
        log('LIMIT: 32 queued per trial, timing distribution limited to one run; scheduled CPU/core affinity, SoC die temperature, joules not measured.')
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 0 if good else 2
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log('RESULT STOPPED_BY_USER')
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 130
    except BaseException as ex:
        log('RESULT ERROR '+type(ex).__name__+' '+str(ex)[:240])
        log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1])
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 2
    finally:fp.close()


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNOVYRA R4.1 precode experiment. Real ARM native S5 load during directed STOP/COMMIT races.
No production UNO, Android dispatch, real P learning or owner authentication.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

VERSION = 'UNO_R41_A52_S5_STOP_PRECOMMIT_RACE_STRESS_V2'
MiB = 1024 * 1024
SAFETY_BATTERY_MIN = 40
SAFETY_BATTERY_TEMP_MAX = 375  # deciC: battery sensor, NOT die temperature
SAFETY_MEMAVAILABLE_MIN = 400000  # kB


def readint(path):
    try:
        return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def memavail():
    try:
        for line in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines():
            if line.startswith('MemAvailable:'):
                return int(line.split()[1])
    except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
        pass
    return None


def telemetry():
    return (readint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),
            readint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'), memavail())


def safety(allow_missing):
    b, t, m = telemetry()
    if not allow_missing and (b is None or t is None or not 150 <= t <= 800 or m is None):
        return 'MISSING_OR_INVALID_BATTERY_OR_MEMORY_SENSOR'
    if b is not None and b < SAFETY_BATTERY_MIN:
        return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t is not None and 150 <= t <= 800 and t >= SAFETY_BATTERY_TEMP_MAX:
        return 'BATTERY_SENSOR_GE_37_5_C'
    if m is not None and m < SAFETY_MEMAVAILABLE_MIN:
        return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None


def dbopen(db):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db), timeout=3.0, isolation_level=None)
    con.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=3000')
    con.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    con.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return con


def dbinit(db):
    con = dbopen(db)
    try:
        con.execute('CREATE TABLE state (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, epoch INTEGER NOT NULL, stopped INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        con.execute('INSERT INTO state VALUES(1,1,0)')
        for table in ('receipts', 'memory', 'plasticity'):
            con.execute('CREATE TABLE ' + table + '(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY)')
    finally:
        con.close()


def dbread(db):
    con = dbopen(db)
    try:
        state = tuple(con.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        rows = [set(r[0] for r in con.execute('SELECT ticket FROM ' + t))
                for t in ('receipts', 'memory', 'plasticity')]
        integrity = con.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
        return {'state': state, 'rows': len(rows[0]),
                'atomic': rows[0] == rows[1] == rows[2], 'integrity': integrity}
    finally:
        con.close()


def controller(db, mode, work, stop, intent):
    """One SQLite writer. Deliberate barriers expose distinct STOP-check/COMMIT orderings."""
    c = None
    try:
        c = dbopen(db)
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        current = c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if current != (1, 0):
            raise RuntimeError('controller_invalid_start_state')
        for table in ('receipts', 'memory', 'plasticity'):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO ' + table + ' VALUES (?)', ('single_synthetic_receipt',))
        precheck_ns = None
        if mode in ('NAIVE_POST_CHECK', 'RECHECK_POST_CHECK', 'COMMIT_FIRST'):
            if intent.value:
                raise RuntimeError('intent_set_before_precheck')
            precheck_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        if mode == 'COMMIT_FIRST':
            commit_begin = time.monotonic_ns()
            c.execute('COMMIT')
            commit_done = time.monotonic_ns()
            work.send(('COMMIT_READY', precheck_ns, commit_begin, commit_done))
        else:
            work.send(('TX_STAGED', precheck_ns, time.monotonic_ns()))
            if not work.poll(8.0):
                raise TimeoutError('release_barrier_timeout')
            if work.recv() != ('RELEASE',):
                raise RuntimeError('bad_barrier_release')
            # Intent is a shared-memory STOP request set in the test parent BEFORE RELEASE.
            # A post-check recheck reduces the window but cannot prove its elimination.
            if mode in ('PRE_GATE', 'RECHECK_POST_CHECK') and intent.value:
                decision = 'ROLLBACK_STOP_INTENT'
                lastcheck = time.monotonic_ns()
                c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                commit_begin = commit_done = None
            else:
                decision = 'COMMIT'
                lastcheck = precheck_ns if mode == 'NAIVE_POST_CHECK' else time.monotonic_ns()
                commit_begin = time.monotonic_ns()
                c.execute('COMMIT')
                commit_done = time.monotonic_ns()
            work.send(('TX_DONE', decision, lastcheck, commit_begin, commit_done))
        # STOP is received through independent IPC; durable latch is a separate SQLite commit.
        if not stop.poll(8.0):
            raise TimeoutError('stop_ipc_timeout')
        item = stop.recv()
        if not (len(item) == 2 and item[0] == 'STOP'):
            raise RuntimeError('bad_stop_ipc')
        recv_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        c.execute('UPDATE state SET epoch=2, stopped=1 WHERE id=1')
        c.execute('COMMIT')
        latch_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        stop.send(('STOP_ACK', item[1], recv_ns, latch_ns))
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:
            if c is not None and c.in_transaction:
                c.execute('ROLLBACK')
            work.send(('CONTROLLER_ERROR', type(ex).__name__, str(ex)[:120]))
        except (OSError, BrokenPipeError):
            pass
    finally:
        if c is not None:
            c.close()
        work.close()
        stop.close()


def resident_holder(mib, stop_event, ready):
    try:
        block = bytearray(mib * MiB)
        for i in range(0, len(block), 4096):
            block[i] = i // 4096 & 255
        ready.put(('READY', os.getpid(), mib), timeout=3)
        while not stop_event.wait(0.2):
            for i in range(0, len(block), 4096 * 64):
                block[i] ^= 1
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:
            ready.put(('ERROR', str(ex)[:100]), timeout=1)
        except Exception:
            pass


def native_s5_worker(binary, stop_event, outcomes):
    runs, values, errors = 0, [], []
    while not stop_event.is_set():
        try:
            p = subprocess.run([binary, 'rank', 'full', '1.0'],
                               capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=4.0)
            if p.returncode != 0 or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS', p.stdout):
                errors.append('S5_invalid:' + (p.stdout + p.stderr)[-130:])
                break
            m = re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)', p.stdout)
            if not m:
                errors.append('S5_no_throughput')
                break
            values.append(float(m.group(1)))
            runs += 1
        except BaseException as ex:
            errors.append(type(ex).__name__ + ':' + str(ex)[:100])
            break
    try:
        outcomes.put((os.getpid(), runs, min(values) if values else None,
                      sum(values)/len(values) if values else None, errors), timeout=2)
    except Exception:
        pass


def check_binary(log):
    folder = Path.home() / 'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'
    exe = folder / 'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not (exe.is_file() and os.access(exe, os.X_OK)):
        source = folder / 'uno_r41_s5_operator.c'
        if not source.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError('Missing native S5: first run UNO_A52_R41_F_OPERATOR_BENCH.sh')
        p = subprocess.run(['clang', '-O3', '-fno-fast-math', '-std=c11', str(source), '-lm', '-o', str(exe)],
                           text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=40)
        if p.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError('native_clang_failed: ' + p.stderr[-200:])
        log('NATIVE_REBUILT_FROM_PREVIOUS_LAB_SOURCE')
    p = subprocess.run([str(exe), 'rank', 'prefix2', '0.3'],
                       text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=4)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS', p.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('native_S5_validation_failed')
    return str(exe)


def recv(conn, expected, timeout=6.0):
    if not conn.poll(timeout):
        raise TimeoutError('IPC_no_reply_' + expected)
    msg = conn.recv()
    if msg[0] == 'CONTROLLER_ERROR':
        raise RuntimeError(str(msg))
    if msg[0] != expected:
        raise RuntimeError('IPC_expected_' + expected + '_got_' + str(msg))
    return msg


def run_case(ctx, db, mode, log, kill_before_latch=False):
    dbinit(db)
    work_parent, work_child = ctx.Pipe()
    ctrl_parent, ctrl_child = ctx.Pipe()
    intent = ctx.RawValue('i', 0)
    p = ctx.Process(target=controller, args=(str(db), mode, work_child, ctrl_child, intent))
    p.start()
    work_child.close(); ctrl_child.close()
    requested = None
    try:
        if mode == 'COMMIT_FIRST':
            msg = recv(work_parent, 'COMMIT_READY')
            committed_ns = msg[3]
            requested = time.monotonic_ns()
            intent.value = 1
            ctrl_parent.send(('STOP', requested))
            tx_result = 'COMMIT_BEFORE_STOP'
        else:
            ready = recv(work_parent, 'TX_STAGED')
            requested = time.monotonic_ns()
            intent.value = 1
            ctrl_parent.send(('STOP', requested))
            if kill_before_latch:
                # Controller is intentionally held IN AN OPEN TRANSACTION.
                # Kill before it can persist the STOP. Volatile intent is NOT durable.
                p.kill()
                p.join(timeout=3)
                if p.is_alive():
                    raise RuntimeError('crash_child_not_terminated')
                observed = dbread(db)
                finding = observed['state'] == (1, 0) and observed['rows'] == 0 and observed['integrity'] == 'ok'
                return {'mode': 'CRASH_BEFORE_LATCH', 'status': 'RACE_FINDING_VOLATILE_STOP_LOST' if finding else 'UNEXPECTED',
                        'ack_ms': None, 'gap_ms': None, 'committed_after_request': 0, 'data': observed,
                        'expected': finding}
            # Coordinated release intentionally places STOP in the last-check/COMMIT gap.
            work_parent.send(('RELEASE',))
            outcome = recv(work_parent, 'TX_DONE')
            tx_result, checked_ns, begin_ns, committed_ns = outcome[1:]
        ack = recv(ctrl_parent, 'STOP_ACK')
        now = time.monotonic_ns()
        if ack[1] != requested:
            raise RuntimeError('STOP_ACK_timestamp_mismatch')
        received_ns, latched_ns = ack[2:]
        p.join(timeout=2)
        if p.is_alive():
            raise RuntimeError('controller_stuck_after_stop')
        data = dbread(db)
        committed_after_request = int(committed_ns is not None and committed_ns > requested)
        committed_after_ack = int(committed_ns is not None and committed_ns > now)
        expected_rows = 0 if mode in ('PRE_GATE', 'RECHECK_POST_CHECK') else 1
        expected = (data['state'] == (2, 1) and data['rows'] == expected_rows
                    and data['atomic'] and data['integrity'] == 'ok' and not committed_after_ack)
        status = ('OBSERVED_COMMIT_AFTER_STOP_REQUEST' if mode == 'NAIVE_POST_CHECK' and committed_after_request else
                  'GUARDED_ROLLBACK' if mode in ('PRE_GATE', 'RECHECK_POST_CHECK') and data['rows'] == 0 else
                  'PRIOR_COMMIT_RETAINED' if mode == 'COMMIT_FIRST' and committed_ns < requested else 'UNEXPECTED')
        if not expected or status == 'UNEXPECTED':
            expected = False
            status = 'FAIL_INVARIANT'
        return {'mode': mode, 'status': status, 'expected': expected,
                'ack_ms': (now-requested)/1e6,
                'receive_ms': (received_ns-requested)/1e6,
                'latch_ms': (latched_ns-requested)/1e6,
                'gap_ms': (committed_ns-requested)/1e6 if committed_ns else None,
                'committed_after_request': committed_after_request,
                'data': data, 'commit_before_stop': mode == 'COMMIT_FIRST' and committed_ns < requested,
                'decision': tx_result}
    finally:
        if p.is_alive():
            p.kill()
            p.join(timeout=2)
        work_parent.close(); ctrl_parent.close()


def fmt(v):
    return '-' if v is None else '%.3f' % v


def percent_nearest(nums, q):
    if not nums:
        return None
    values = sorted(nums)
    import math
    return values[max(0, math.ceil(q/100 * len(values))-1)]


def stress_stage(name, nworkers, memory_mib, seconds, repetitions, binary, home, ctx, log, quick):
    stop_event = ctx.Event()
    result_q, ready_q = ctx.Queue(), ctx.Queue()
    workers = []
    holder = None
    started = time.monotonic()
    findings, guarded_fails, results = 0, 0, []
    try:
        if memory_mib:
            holder = ctx.Process(target=resident_holder, args=(memory_mib, stop_event, ready_q))
            holder.start()
            info = ready_q.get(timeout=5)
            if info[0] != 'READY':
                raise RuntimeError('memory_holder_failed_' + str(info))
            log('RAM_PRESSURE_STAGE allocated_touched_MiB=%d pid=%d' % (memory_mib, info[1]))
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            p = ctx.Process(target=native_s5_worker, args=(binary, stop_event, result_q))
            p.start(); workers.append(p)
        log('STAGE_BEGIN name=%s native_S5_workers=%d held_ram_MiB=%d min_duration_s=%d' %
            (name, nworkers, memory_mib, seconds))
        # S5 jobs are active for ALL trials. There is NO zero-worker phase.
        modes = ('PRE_GATE', 'NAIVE_POST_CHECK', 'RECHECK_POST_CHECK', 'COMMIT_FIRST')
        case_no = 0
        for rep in range(1, repetitions+1):
            for mode in modes:
                issue = safety(quick)
                if issue:
                    raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_' + issue)
                case_no += 1
                db = home / ('%s_%03d_%s.sqlite3' % (name, case_no, mode))
                out = run_case(ctx, db, mode, log)
                results.append(out)
                if out['status'] == 'OBSERVED_COMMIT_AFTER_STOP_REQUEST':
                    findings += 1
                if not out['expected']:
                    guarded_fails += 1
                log('CASE stage=%s rep=%d mode=%s status=%s ack_ms=%s commit_minus_request_ms=%s rows=%d stopped=%d atomic=%s' %
                    (name, rep, mode, out['status'], fmt(out['ack_ms']), fmt(out['gap_ms']),
                     out['data']['rows'], out['data']['state'][1], out['data']['atomic']))
                # Short spacing varies actual scheduling under continuous S5 pressure.
                time.sleep((case_no % 3) * 0.004)
        issue = safety(quick)
        if issue:
            raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_' + issue)
        crash = run_case(ctx, home / (name + '_CRASH_BEFORE_STOP_LATCH.sqlite3'),
                         'PRE_GATE', log, kill_before_latch=True)
        results.append(crash)
        if not crash['expected']:
            guarded_fails += 1
        log('CRASH_FINDING stage=%s status=%s rows=%d persisted_stop=%d crash_rollback=%s' %
            (name, crash['status'], crash['data']['rows'], crash['data']['state'][1], crash['data']['integrity']))
        while time.monotonic() < started+seconds:
            issue = safety(quick)
            if issue:
                raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_' + issue)
            time.sleep(0.2)
        acks = [x['ack_ms'] for x in results if x.get('ack_ms') is not None]
        latch = [x.get('latch_ms') for x in results if x.get('latch_ms') is not None]
        log('STOP_TIMING stage=%s samples=%d ack_median_ms=%s ack_p95_nearest_rank_ms=%s ack_max_ms=%s latch_max_ms=%s' %
            (name, len(acks), fmt(percent_nearest(acks,50)),fmt(percent_nearest(acks,95)),fmt(max(acks)),fmt(max(latch))))
        log('PROTOCOL_OBSERVATIONS stage=%s cases=%d exposed_post_check_commits=%d strict_user_request_stop_NOT_PROVEN=YES volatile_crash_stop_lost=%s invariant_failures=%d' %
            (name,len(results),findings,crash['status']=='RACE_FINDING_VOLATILE_STOP_LOST',guarded_fails))
    finally:
        stop_event.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(timeout=2.5)
            if p.is_alive():
                p.kill();p.join(timeout=1)
        if holder is not None:
            holder.join(timeout=2)
            if holder.is_alive():
                holder.kill();holder.join(timeout=1)
        found=[]
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                found.append(result_q.get(timeout=1.0))
            except queue.Empty:
                log('S5_WORKER_MISSING_RESULT stage='+name)
                guarded_fails += 1
        for pid,runs,mn,avg,errs in found:
            log('S5_WORKER stage=%s pid=%d native_runs=%d min_GMAC_s=%s mean_GMAC_s=%s errors=%s' %
                (name,pid,runs,mn,avg,errs))
            if runs < 1 or errs:
                guarded_fails += 1
        result_q.cancel_join_thread();result_q.close()
        ready_q.cancel_join_thread();ready_q.close()
    if guarded_fails:
        log('STAGE_FAIL name=%s failures=%d' % (name,guarded_fails))
        return False, findings
    log('STAGE_COMPLETE name=%s expected_fixture_behaviors=PASS observed_vulnerable_window=%d' % (name,findings))
    return True, findings


def main():
    arg = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    arg.add_argument('--quick', action='store_true', help='Smoke test on developer Linux; NOT the A52 stress result')
    a=arg.parse_args()
    dest = Path.home()/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not dest.is_dir() or not os.access(dest,os.W_OK):
        dest=Path.home()
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report = dest/('UNO_A52_R41_STOP_PRECOMMIT_NATIVE_STRESS_'+stamp+'.txt')
    f=report.open('x',encoding='utf-8')
    def log(s):
        print(s,flush=True)
        f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
    try:
        log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- NATIVE S5 LOADED STOP / PRECOMMIT RACE + CRASH LAB '+VERSION)
        log('Actual native C256R32 FP32 S5 computes on Android CPU; Python orchestrates IPC/SQLite and timestamps.')
        log('No idle phase: 2 S5 workers / 32 MiB, then 4 S5 workers / 64 MiB for ALL directed race cases.')
        log('TEST: STOP before precommit gate; STOP after last check before COMMIT; final recheck; COMMIT before STOP; crash before durable STOP latch.')
        log('WARNING: deliberately vulnerable NAIVE_POST_CHECK mode is a NEGATIVE CONTROL; finding is expected, NOT proof production UNO is broken.')
        log('No Android actions, permission changes, user authentication, training, model weights, or real user STOP. OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
        log('SAFETY: battery>=40%, battery sensor<37.5C, MemAvailable>=400000 kB; Ctrl+C terminates test workers.')
        b,t,m=telemetry();log('PRECHECK battery=%s temp_deciC=%s memavailable_kB=%s' % (b,t,m))
        issue=safety(a.quick)
        if issue:
            log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue)
            return 3
        exe=check_binary(log)
        log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_BINARY '+exe)
        root=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_stop_precommit_stress_lab'/stamp
        root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False,mode=0o700)
        ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
        plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,4,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,5,2)] if a.quick else [
            ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,14,5),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,18,5)]
        ok_all=True; total_findings=0
        for name,n,ram,duration,reps in plans:
            issue=safety(a.quick)
            if issue:
                log('RESULT SAFETY_ABORT '+issue)
                return 3
            ok,count=stress_stage(name,n,ram,duration,reps,exe,root,ctx,log,a.quick)
            ok_all=ok_all and ok
            total_findings += count
            b,t,m=telemetry()
            log('STAGE_TELEMETRY stage=%s battery=%s temp_deciC=%s MemAvailable_kB=%s' % (name,b,t,m))
            if not ok:
                break
        log('RESULT '+('PASS_WITH_REPRODUCED_RACE_AND_CRASH_GAPS' if ok_all else 'FAIL_INVARIANT')+
            ' intentionally_vulnerable_commits_after_stop_request='+str(total_findings))
        log('UNRESOLVED strict user STOP request-to-commit guarantee; durable external stop intent across pre-latch controller crash.')
        log('LIMIT This tests deliberately staged interleavings under real ARM S5 compute, NOT probabilistic discovery of all races, Android control or a STOP SLA.')
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 0 if ok_all else 2
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPTED')
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 130
    except BaseException as ex:
        log('RESULT ERROR '+type(ex).__name__+' '+str(ex)[:220])
        log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1])
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 2
    finally:
        f.close()

if __name__=='__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

<<SOURCE_END V02 UNO_A52_R41_S5_STOP_PRECOMMIT_RACE_STRESS_V2.py>>

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 experimental (not production): durable STOP vs receipt commit ordering.
Runs under 2/4 *native* FP32 C256R32 S5 workers on A52 in Termux.
No Android capabilities, actual owner STOP, external dispatch, neural learning, or release claim.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as datetime
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
import queue
import re
import signal
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback
from pathlib import Path

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_S5_DURABLE_STOP_ORDER_STRESS_V3'
MIB=1024*1024

def read_int(path):
    try: return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (ValueError, OSError): return None

def mem_avail():
    try:
        for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines():
            if s.startswith('MemAvailable:'): return int(s.split()[1])
    except (OSError, ValueError, IndexError): pass
    return None

def metrics():
    return read_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'), read_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'), mem_avail()

def safety(quick):
    b,t,m=metrics()
    if quick: return None
    if b is None or t is None or m is None or t<100 or t>800: return 'REQUIRED_SENSOR_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40: return 'BATTERY_LT_40'
    if t>=375: return 'BATTERY_GE_37_5_C'
    if m<400000: return 'MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def connect(path):
    db=sqlite3.connect(str(path), timeout=10.0, isolation_level=None)
    db.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=10000')
    db.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return db

def initialize(path):
    c=connect(path)
    try:
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE gate (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id=1), epoch INTEGER NOT NULL, stopped INTEGER NOT NULL, stop_ns INTEGER)')
        c.execute('INSERT INTO gate VALUES (1,1,0,NULL)')
        for name in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
            c.execute('CREATE TABLE '+name+'(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY, epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    finally: c.close()

def inspect(path):
    c=connect(path)
    try:
        state=tuple(c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM gate WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        records=[set(c.execute('SELECT ticket,epoch FROM '+name).fetchall()) for name in ('receipts','memory','plasticity')]
        return {'state':state, 'rows':len(records[0]), 'atomic':records[0]==records[1]==records[2],
                'integrity':c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]}
    finally: c.close()

def commit_once(path,ticket='T1',epoch=1):
    c=connect(path)
    try:
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        e,stop=c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM gate WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if stop or epoch!=e:
            c.execute('ROLLBACK');return 'STOPPED_OR_STALE', None
        if c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM receipts WHERE ticket=?',(ticket,)).fetchone():
            c.execute('ROLLBACK');return 'DUPLICATE',None
        for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO '+tab+' VALUES (?,?)',(ticket,epoch))
        c.execute('COMMIT')
        return 'COMMITTED',time.monotonic_ns()
    finally:
        if c.in_transaction: c.execute('ROLLBACK')
        c.close()

def stop_actor(path,channel,mode):
    c=None
    try:
        c=connect(path)
        channel.send(('READY',os.getpid()))
        if not channel.poll(12): raise TimeoutError('STOP_REQUEST_TIMEOUT')
        item=channel.recv()
        if item[0]!='STOP': raise ValueError('UNEXPECTED_STOP_MESSAGE')
        received=time.monotonic_ns()
        channel.send(('RECEIVED',received))
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        c.execute('UPDATE gate SET epoch=2,stopped=1,stop_ns=? WHERE id=1',(received,))
        if mode=='HOLD':
            channel.send(('HELD',time.monotonic_ns()))
            if not channel.poll(12): raise TimeoutError('STOP_RELEASE_TIMEOUT')
            if channel.recv()!=('RELEASE',): raise ValueError('STOP_RELEASE_BAD')
        c.execute('COMMIT')
        durable=time.monotonic_ns()
        channel.send(('ACK',durable))
        if mode=='ACK_THEN_WAIT':
            # Parent deliberately kills this still-live process after ACK.
            if not channel.poll(15): raise TimeoutError('PARENT_DID_NOT_CRASH_STOP_ACTOR')
            channel.recv()
    except BaseException as ex:
        try: channel.send(('ERROR',type(ex).__name__,str(ex)[:120]))
        except Exception: pass
    finally:
        if c is not None:
            try:
                if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                c.close()
            except Exception:pass
        channel.close()

def held_commit_actor(path,crash_after_commit,channel):
    c=None
    try:
        c=connect(path)
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        e,stop=c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped FROM gate WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if e!=1 or stop: raise RuntimeError('EXPECTED_UNSTOPPED')
        for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO '+tab+' VALUES (?,?)',('T1',1))
        channel.send(('HELD',os.getpid()))
        if not channel.poll(12): raise TimeoutError('COMMIT_RELEASE_TIMEOUT')
        if channel.recv()!=('RELEASE',): raise RuntimeError('COMMIT_RELEASE_BAD')
        c.execute('COMMIT')
        committed=time.monotonic_ns()
        if crash_after_commit:
            # Deliberate crash after durable local commit and before any user-level ACK.
            os._exit(42)
        channel.send(('COMMITTED',committed))
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:channel.send(('ERROR',type(ex).__name__,str(ex)[:120]))
        except Exception:pass
    finally:
        if c is not None:
            try:
                if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                c.close()
            except Exception:pass
        channel.close()

def commit_actor(path,channel,ticket='T1'):
    try:
        channel.send(('TRYING',os.getpid()))
        out,ns=commit_once(path,ticket)
        channel.send(('RESULT',out,ns))
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:channel.send(('ERROR',type(ex).__name__,str(ex)[:120]))
        except Exception:pass
    finally:channel.close()

def get(c,expected,timeout=12):
    if not c.poll(timeout):raise TimeoutError('IPC_WAIT_'+expected)
    v=c.recv()
    if v[0]=='ERROR':raise RuntimeError(str(v))
    if v[0]!=expected:raise RuntimeError('EXPECTED_'+expected+'_GOT_'+str(v))
    return v

def make_process(ctx,target,args):
    parent,child=ctx.Pipe(duplex=True)
    p=ctx.Process(target=target,args=(*args,child))
    p.start();child.close()
    return p,parent

def _stop_process(ctx,db,mode):
    parent,child=ctx.Pipe(duplex=True)
    p=ctx.Process(target=stop_actor,args=(str(db),child,mode))
    p.start();child.close()
    get(parent,'READY')
    return p,parent

def join_checked(p,expected_exit=0):
    p.join(4)
    if p.is_alive():raise TimeoutError('CHILD_DID_NOT_EXIT')
    if p.exitcode!=expected_exit:raise RuntimeError('CHILD_EXIT_'+str(p.exitcode))

def release_process(p):
    if p.is_alive():p.kill()
    p.join(2)

def tested_case(ctx,db,mode):
    initialize(db)
    actors=[]
    times={}
    try:
        if mode=='STOP_LATCH_FIRST':
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'DIRECT');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1]
            times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            join_checked(stop)
            out,ns=commit_once(db)
            expected=out=='STOPPED_OR_STALE' and ns is None
            label='STOP_BLOCKED_FUTURE_COMMIT'
        elif mode=='COMMIT_FIRST':
            out,times['commit']=commit_once(db)
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'DIRECT');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1]
            times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            join_checked(stop)
            expected=out=='COMMITTED' and times['commit']<times['request']
            label='PAST_COMMIT_RETAINED'
        elif mode=='STOP_LOCK_FIRST':
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'HOLD');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1];get(ch,'HELD')
            cp,cc=make_process(ctx,commit_actor,(str(db),));actors.append((cp,cc))
            get(cc,'TRYING')
            time.sleep(.012)  # force competing writer to wait on gate lock
            ch.send(('RELEASE',))
            times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            out=get(cc,'RESULT');times['commit']=out[2]
            join_checked(stop);join_checked(cp)
            expected=out[1]=='STOPPED_OR_STALE' and times['commit'] is None
            label='STOP_TX_WINS_DB_ORDER'
        elif mode=='WRITER_LOCK_FIRST':
            cp,cc=make_process(ctx,held_commit_actor,(str(db),False));actors.append((cp,cc))
            get(cc,'HELD')
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'DIRECT');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1]
            time.sleep(.012)  # writer deliberately holds BEGIN IMMEDIATE lock across STOP request
            cc.send(('RELEASE',))
            times['commit']=get(cc,'COMMITTED')[1]
            times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            join_checked(cp);join_checked(stop)
            expected=times['request']<times['commit']<times['latch']
            label='REQUEST_BEFORE_COMMIT_BUT_DB_ORDER_COMMIT_FIRST'
        elif mode=='CRASH_AFTER_STOP_ACK':
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'ACK_THEN_WAIT');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1];times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            if not stop.is_alive(): raise RuntimeError('STOP_ACTOR_EXITED_BEFORE_FORCED_CRASH')
            stop.kill();stop.join(3)
            if stop.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('STOP_ACTOR_NOT_KILLED_AFTER_ACK')
            # ACK already durable; a new controller must enforce it without volatile RAM.
            out,ns=commit_once(db)
            expected=out=='STOPPED_OR_STALE'
            label='DURABLE_ACK_SURVIVES_NEW_CONTROLLER'
        elif mode=='CRASH_PRE_STOP_ACK':
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'HOLD');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1];get(ch,'HELD')
            stop.kill();stop.join(3)
            if stop.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('STOP_PROCESS_STUCK')
            prior=inspect(db)
            # This negative control demonstrates the consequence: after process death,
            # a *new* ticket writer can commit because the unacknowledged STOP vanished.
            replay_status,_=commit_once(db,'T_AFTER_UNACKED_CRASH')
            expected=prior['state']==(1,0) and prior['rows']==0 and replay_status=='COMMITTED'
            label='UNACKED_STOP_LOST_AND_RESTART_COMMIT_ALLOWED'
        elif mode=='REPLAY_QUEUE_32':
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'DIRECT');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1];times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            join_checked(stop)
            out=[commit_once(db,'T'+str(i))[0] for i in range(32)]
            expected=all(s=='STOPPED_OR_STALE' for s in out)
            label='ALL_32_POST_STOP_DENIED'
        elif mode=='COMMIT_NO_ACK_DEDUP':
            cp,cc=make_process(ctx,held_commit_actor,(str(db),True));actors.append((cp,cc))
            get(cc,'HELD');cc.send(('RELEASE',))
            join_checked(cp,42)
            pre=inspect(db)
            replay,ticket=commit_once(db,'T1')
            stop,ch=_stop_process(ctx,db,'DIRECT');actors.append((stop,ch))
            times['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('STOP',))
            times['receive']=get(ch,'RECEIVED')[1];times['latch']=get(ch,'ACK')[1]
            join_checked(stop)
            expected=pre['rows']==1 and replay=='DUPLICATE' and ticket is None
            label='DURABLE_COMMIT_REPLAY_DEDUP'
        else: raise ValueError('UNKNOWN_CASE_'+mode)
        data=inspect(db)
        expected=expected and data['integrity']=='ok' and data['atomic']
        if mode in ('STOP_LATCH_FIRST','STOP_LOCK_FIRST','CRASH_AFTER_STOP_ACK','REPLAY_QUEUE_32'):
            expected=expected and data['rows']==0
        else:
            expected=expected and data['rows']==1
        if mode!='CRASH_PRE_STOP_ACK':expected=expected and data['state']==(2,1)
        return {'case':mode,'status':'PASS' if expected else 'FAIL','label':label,'data':data,
                'request_to_receive_ms':(times['receive']-times['request'])/1e6 if 'receive' in times else None,
                'request_to_latch_ms':(times['latch']-times['request'])/1e6 if 'latch' in times else None,
                'commit_minus_request_ms':(times['commit']-times['request'])/1e6 if times.get('commit') is not None and 'request' in times else None,
                'negative_control':mode in ('WRITER_LOCK_FIRST','CRASH_PRE_STOP_ACK')}
    finally:
        for p,c in actors:
            release_process(p)
            c.close()

def native_worker(binary,event,result):
    values=[];errs=[]
    while not event.is_set():
        try:
            p=subprocess.run([binary,'rank','full','0.75'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=4)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):
                errs.append('S5_BAD_EXIT_OR_CONTRACT');break
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not m:errs.append('S5_MISSING_RATE');break
            values.append(float(m.group(1)))
        except BaseException as ex:
            errs.append(type(ex).__name__+':'+str(ex)[:90]);break
    try:result.put((os.getpid(),len(values),min(values) if values else None, sum(values)/len(values) if values else None,errs),timeout=2)
    except Exception:pass

def hold_ram(mib,event,ready):
    try:
        block=bytearray(mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(block),4096):block[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=2)
        while not event.wait(.3):
            for i in range(0,len(block),4096*64):block[i]^=1
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(ex)),timeout=1)
        except Exception:pass

def check_binary():
    path=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not (path.is_file() and os.access(path,os.X_OK)):
        raise RuntimeError('MISSING_NATIVE_S5: run earlier UNO_A52_R41_F_OPERATOR_BENCH.sh')
    p=subprocess.run([str(path),'rank','prefix2','0.3'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
    if p.returncode or 'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT' not in p.stdout or 'status=PASS' not in p.stdout:
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_PROBE_FAILED')
    return str(path)

def fmt(x):return '-' if x is None else ('%.3f'%x)

def stage(name,n,ram,duration,repetitions,binary,root,ctx,log,quick):
    event=ctx.Event();workers=[];res=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();holder=None
    began=time.monotonic();allresults=[];errs=0
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=hold_ram,args=(ram,event,ready));holder.start()
        msg=ready.get(timeout=5)
        if msg[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_NOT_READY_'+str(msg))
        for _ in range(n):
            p=ctx.Process(target=native_worker,args=(binary,event,res));p.start();workers.append(p)
        log('STAGE_BEGIN name=%s native_S5=%d held_ram_MiB=%d duration_floor_s=%d' %(name,n,ram,duration))
        modes=('STOP_LATCH_FIRST','COMMIT_FIRST','STOP_LOCK_FIRST','WRITER_LOCK_FIRST',
               'CRASH_AFTER_STOP_ACK','CRASH_PRE_STOP_ACK','REPLAY_QUEUE_32','COMMIT_NO_ACK_DEDUP')
        for rep in range(1,repetitions+1):
            for mode in modes:
                issue=safety(quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                db=root/('%s_%02d_%s.sqlite3'%(name,rep,mode))
                out=tested_case(ctx,db,mode)
                allresults.append(out)
                if out['status']!='PASS':errs+=1
                log('CASE stage=%s rep=%d mode=%s status=%s rows=%d stopped=%d atomic=%s request_to_receive_ms=%s request_to_latch_ms=%s commit_minus_request_ms=%s meaning=%s' %(
                    name,rep,mode,out['status'],out['data']['rows'],out['data']['state'][1],out['data']['atomic'],
                    fmt(out['request_to_receive_ms']),fmt(out['request_to_latch_ms']),fmt(out['commit_minus_request_ms']),out['label']))
        while time.monotonic()<began+duration:
            issue=safety(quick)
            if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
            time.sleep(.2)
    finally:
        event.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(3)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(1)
        if holder is not None:
            holder.join(2)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(1)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,minimum,mean,problems=res.get(timeout=1)
                log('S5_WORKER stage=%s pid=%d native_runs=%d min_GMAC_s=%s mean_GMAC_s=%s errors=%s'%(
                    name,pid,runs,minimum,mean,problems))
                if runs<1 or problems:errs+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                log('S5_WORKER_MISSING_RESULT stage='+name);errs+=1
        res.cancel_join_thread();res.close();ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
    acks=[x['request_to_latch_ms'] for x in allresults if x['request_to_latch_ms'] is not None]
    import statistics
    acks.sort()
    med=statistics.median(acks) if acks else None
    maxack=max(acks) if acks else None
    import math
    p95=acks[math.ceil(.95*len(acks))-1] if acks else None
    finding_request=sum(1 for x in allresults if x['case']=='WRITER_LOCK_FIRST' and x['commit_minus_request_ms'] is not None and x['commit_minus_request_ms']>0)
    finding_crash=sum(1 for x in allresults if x['case']=='CRASH_PRE_STOP_ACK' and x['data']['state']==(1,0))
    log('STAGE_SUMMARY stage=%s cases=%d failures=%d stop_ack_n=%d ack_median_ms=%s ack_p95_nearest_rank_ms=%s ack_max_ms=%s request_precedes_commit_findings=%d pre_ack_crash_loses_stop=%d' %(
        name,len(allresults),errs,len(acks),fmt(med),fmt(p95),fmt(maxack),finding_request,finding_crash))
    return errs==0,finding_request,finding_crash

def main():
    arg=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    arg.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='Developer smoke test, not full A52 measurement')
    opts=arg.parse_args()
    target=Path.home()/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not target.is_dir() or not os.access(target,os.W_OK):target=Path.home()
    stamp=datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=target/('UNO_A52_R41_DURABLE_STOP_ORDER_STRESS_'+stamp+'.txt')
    out=report.open('x',encoding='utf-8')
    def log(s):
        print(s,flush=True);out.write(s+'\n');out.flush()
    try:
        log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- DURABLE STOP / COMMIT SERIALIZATION UNDER NATIVE S5 STRESS '+VERSION)
        log('On A52: native ARM FP32 S5 workers run on phone CPU; Python orchestrates SQLite and IPC; synthetic fixtures.')
        log('No idle stage: 2 workers +32MiB and 4 workers +64MiB. All directed cases run under load.')
        log('SQLite state and T/L/P fixture share ONE WAL FULL database; separate stop registrar and ticket executor contend for BEGIN IMMEDIATE.')
        log('STOP REQUEST, STOP RECEIVED, DURABLE STOP ACK and COMMIT are different instants.')
        log('Negative controls: WRITE_LOCK_FIRST commit after STOP request; CRASH_PRE_STOP_ACK loses unacknowledged STOP.')
        log('No genuine Android dispatch or user authorization, native receiver or trained P; no production guarantee.')
        log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
        b,t,m=metrics();log('PRECHECK battery=%s temp_deciC=%s MemAvailable_kB=%s'%(b,t,m))
        issue=safety(opts.quick)
        if issue:
            log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue);return 3
        binary=check_binary();log('NATIVE_S5 '+binary)
        root=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_durable_stop_order_lab'/stamp
        root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False,mode=0o700)
        ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
        plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,4,1),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,5,1)] if opts.quick else [
            ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,16,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,20,2)]
        overall=True;findings=0;crashes=0
        def stage_watchdog(signum, frame):
            raise TimeoutError('STAGE_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT')
        old_handler=signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM,stage_watchdog)
        try:
            for name,n,ram,seconds,reps in plans:
                issue=safety(opts.quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                signal.alarm(100 if n==2 else 140)
                try:
                    ok,request_findings,crash_findings=stage(name,n,ram,seconds,reps,binary,root,ctx,log,opts.quick)
                finally:
                    signal.alarm(0)
                overall=overall and ok;findings+=request_findings;crashes+=crash_findings
                b,t,m=metrics();log('STAGE_TELEMETRY name=%s battery=%s temp_deciC=%s MemAvailable_kB=%s'%(name,b,t,m))
                if not ok:break
        finally:
            signal.alarm(0)
            signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM,old_handler)
        log('RESULT '+('PASS_EXPECTED_SQLITE_SERIAL_ORDER_WITH_KNOWN_REQUEST_GAPS' if overall else 'FAIL_INVARIANT')+
            ' stop_requested_before_commit_cases='+str(findings)+' pre_ack_crash_lost_stop_cases='+str(crashes))
        log('UNRESOLVED: strict user-press STOP before all future commits; reliable durable acceptance of STOP before controller crash; real Android capability isolation.')
        log('LIMIT: ordering is for SQLite synthetic transactions only; not external OS actions or hard real-time response; 2 repetitions per stage cannot define SLA.')
        log('REPORT '+str(report))
        return 0 if overall else 2
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPT');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
    except BaseException as ex:
        log('RESULT ERROR '+type(ex).__name__+' '+str(ex)[:250]);log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2
    finally:out.close()

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())
