#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 V4 PRECODE laboratory: independent durable STOP ingress and synthetic commit guard.
Under native S5 C256R32 CPU load on an ARM Android phone. NOT a production security model.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, datetime, fcntl, math, multiprocessing as mp, os, queue, re
import signal, sqlite3, statistics, subprocess, sys, time, traceback
from pathlib import Path

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_S5_STOP_INGRESS_DURABILITY_STRESS_V4'
MiB=1048576

def read_i(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:
        m=next(int(s.split()[1]) for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):m=None
    return (read_i('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),read_i('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),m)

def safety(quick):
    if quick:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if b is None or t is None or m is None or not 100<=t<=800:return 'REQUIRED_BATTERY_OR_MEMORY_SENSOR_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_LT_40_PCT'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_GE_37_5_C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def db(path):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),timeout=7.0,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=7000')
    c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return c

def init_case(root):
    root.mkdir(mode=0o700,parents=True,exist_ok=False)
    (root/'gate.lock').touch(mode=0o600)
    s=db(root/'stop.sqlite3')
    s.execute('CREATE TABLE stop_latch(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,epoch INTEGER NOT NULL,stopped INTEGER NOT NULL,seq INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    s.execute('INSERT INTO stop_latch VALUES(1,1,0,0)');s.close()
    w=db(root/'work.sqlite3')
    for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
        w.execute('CREATE TABLE '+tab+'(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY,epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    w.close()

def get_state(root):
    c=db(root/'stop.sqlite3')
    try:return tuple(c.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped,seq FROM stop_latch WHERE id=1').fetchone())
    finally:c.close()

def inspect(root):
    s=db(root/'stop.sqlite3');w=db(root/'work.sqlite3')
    try:
        state=tuple(s.execute('SELECT epoch,stopped,seq FROM stop_latch WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        sets=[set(w.execute('SELECT ticket,epoch FROM '+tab).fetchall()) for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity')]
        return {'state':state,'rows':len(sets[0]),'atomic':sets[0]==sets[1]==sets[2],
                'db_ok':s.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]=='ok' and w.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]=='ok'}
    finally:s.close();w.close()

def guarded_commit(root,ticket='T1'):
    with (root/'gate.lock').open('rb') as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        try:
            s=db(root/'stop.sqlite3');w=db(root/'work.sqlite3')
            try:
                if s.execute('SELECT stopped FROM stop_latch WHERE id=1').fetchone()[0]:return 'STOPPED',None
                w.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                if w.execute('SELECT 1 FROM receipts WHERE ticket=?',(ticket,)).fetchone():
                    w.execute('ROLLBACK');return 'DUPLICATE',None
                for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
                    w.execute('INSERT INTO '+tab+' VALUES (?,1)',(ticket,))
                w.execute('COMMIT')
                return 'COMMITTED',time.monotonic_ns()
            finally:
                if w.in_transaction:w.execute('ROLLBACK')
                w.close();s.close()
        finally:fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_UN)

def stop_ingress(root,mode,ch):
    """Separate process; STOP is durable only after SQLite FULL COMMIT and ACK.
    The shared advisory file lock orders all participating lab committers and STOP acceptance.
    """
    c=None
    try:
        c=db(root/'stop.sqlite3')
        ch.send(('READY',os.getpid()))
        if not ch.poll(10):raise TimeoutError('NO_STOP_REQUEST')
        if ch.recv()!=('STOP',):raise RuntimeError('BAD_STOP_REQUEST')
        receive_ns=time.monotonic_ns();ch.send(('RECEIVED',receive_ns))
        if mode=='WAIT_BEFORE_ACCEPT':
            ch.send(('PARKED',))
            if not ch.poll(12):raise TimeoutError('WAIT_RELEASE_STOP')
            if ch.recv()!=('RELEASE',):raise RuntimeError('WRONG_RELEASE')
        with (root/'gate.lock').open('rb') as lock:
            fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            try:
                if mode=='HOLD_LOCK':
                    ch.send(('LOCKED',))
                    if not ch.poll(12):raise TimeoutError('WAIT_LOCK_RELEASE')
                    if ch.recv()!=('RELEASE',):raise RuntimeError('WRONG_LOCK_RELEASE')
                c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                c.execute('UPDATE stop_latch SET stopped=1,epoch=2,seq=seq+1 WHERE id=1')
                c.execute('COMMIT')
                latch_ns=time.monotonic_ns()
            finally:fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_UN)
        ch.send(('ACK_DURABLE',latch_ns))
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:ch.send(('ERROR',type(ex).__name__,str(ex)[:130]))
        except Exception:pass
    finally:
        if c:
            try:
                if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                c.close()
            except Exception:pass
        ch.close()

def work_actor(root,mode,ch):
    """Fixture worker holds global lock and work transaction to force exact interleavings."""
    w=None
    try:
        with (root/'gate.lock').open('rb') as lock:
            fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            try:
                state=get_state(root)
                if state[1]:ch.send(('BLOCKED',));return
                w=db(root/'work.sqlite3');w.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
                    w.execute('INSERT INTO '+tab+' VALUES (?,1)',('T1',))
                ch.send(('HELD',os.getpid()))
                if not ch.poll(12):raise TimeoutError('WORKER_RELEASE_TIMEOUT')
                if ch.recv()!=('RELEASE',):raise RuntimeError('INVALID_WORKER_RELEASE')
                if mode=='CRASH_UNCOMMITTED':os._exit(33)
                w.execute('COMMIT')
                ch.send(('COMMITTED',time.monotonic_ns()))
            finally:fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:ch.send(('ERROR',type(ex).__name__,str(ex)[:130]))
        except Exception:pass
    finally:
        if w:
            try:
                if w.in_transaction:w.execute('ROLLBACK')
                w.close()
            except Exception:pass
        ch.close()

def writer_try_actor(root,ch):
    try:
        ch.send(('TRYING',time.monotonic_ns()))
        outcome,ns=guarded_commit(root)
        ch.send(('RESULT',outcome,ns))
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:ch.send(('ERROR',type(ex).__name__,str(ex)[:130]))
        except Exception:pass
    finally:ch.close()

def dummy_controller(ch):
    try:
        ch.send(('READY',os.getpid()))
        while True:time.sleep(.1)
    finally:ch.close()

def start(ctx,target,*args):
    a,b=ctx.Pipe(duplex=True)
    p=ctx.Process(target=target,args=(*args,b))
    p.start();b.close()
    return p,a

def get(ch,expected,timeout=12):
    if not ch.poll(timeout):raise TimeoutError('WAIT_'+expected)
    v=ch.recv()
    if v[0]=='ERROR':raise RuntimeError('CHILD_ERROR_'+str(v))
    if v[0]!=expected:raise RuntimeError('EXPECTED_'+expected+'_GOT_'+str(v))
    return v

def join(p,exitcode=0):
    p.join(4)
    if p.is_alive():raise TimeoutError('CHILD_DID_NOT_EXIT')
    if p.exitcode!=exitcode:raise RuntimeError('BAD_CHILD_EXIT_'+str(p.exitcode))

def cleanup(actors):
    for p,ch in actors:
        try:
            if p.is_alive():p.kill()
            p.join(2)
        except Exception:pass
        try:ch.close()
        except Exception:pass

def case(ctx,root,mode):
    init_case(root);actors=[];t={};expected=False;label=''
    try:
        if mode=='ACK_FIRST_BLOCKS':
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'DIRECT');actors.append((ip,ic))
            get(ic,'READY');t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',))
            t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1];t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1];join(ip)
            out,ns=guarded_commit(root)
            expected=out=='STOPPED' and ns is None
            label='POST_ACK_COMMIT_BLOCKED'
        elif mode=='COMMIT_FIRST_RETAINED':
            out,t['commit']=guarded_commit(root)
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'DIRECT');actors.append((ip,ic))
            get(ic,'READY');t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',))
            t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1];t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1];join(ip)
            expected=out=='COMMITTED' and t['commit']<t['request']
            label='PAST_COMMIT_RETAINED'
        elif mode=='STOP_LOCK_FIRST':
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'HOLD_LOCK');actors.append((ip,ic))
            get(ic,'READY');t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',))
            t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1];get(ic,'LOCKED')
            wp,wc=start(ctx,writer_try_actor,root);actors.append((wp,wc))
            get(wc,'TRYING');time.sleep(.012);ic.send(('RELEASE',))
            t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1]
            status=get(wc,'RESULT');join(ip);join(wp)
            expected=status[1]=='STOPPED' and status[2] is None
            label='STOP_LOCK_PRECEDES_WORK_COMMIT'
        elif mode=='WORK_LOCK_FIRST':
            wp,wc=start(ctx,work_actor,root,'NORMAL');actors.append((wp,wc));get(wc,'HELD')
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'DIRECT');actors.append((ip,ic));get(ic,'READY')
            t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',));t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1]
            time.sleep(.012);wc.send(('RELEASE',));t['commit']=get(wc,'COMMITTED')[1]
            t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1];join(wp);join(ip)
            expected=t['request']<t['commit']<t['ack']
            label='KNOWN_REQUEST_TO_ACK_WINDOW'
        elif mode=='CTRL_CRASH_PRE_ACK_INGRESS_SURVIVES':
            wp,wc=start(ctx,work_actor,root,'NORMAL');actors.append((wp,wc));get(wc,'HELD')
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'DIRECT');actors.append((ip,ic));get(ic,'READY')
            t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',));t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1]
            # Work/controller process dies while holding the transaction lock. Ingress remains alive.
            wp.kill();join(wp,-9)
            t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1];join(ip)
            out,ns=guarded_commit(root,'T_AFTER_CTRL_CRASH')
            expected=out=='STOPPED' and ns is None
            label='INDEPENDENT_STOP_PERSISTS_AFTER_CONTROLLER_CRASH'
        elif mode=='CTRL_CRASH_POST_ACK':
            controller,cc=start(ctx,dummy_controller);actors.append((controller,cc));get(cc,'READY')
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'DIRECT');actors.append((ip,ic));get(ic,'READY')
            t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',))
            t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1];t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1];join(ip)
            controller.kill();join(controller,-9)
            out,_=guarded_commit(root,'T_AFTER_ACK_AND_CRASH')
            expected=out=='STOPPED'
            label='DURABLE_ACK_SURVIVES_CONTROLLER_CRASH'
        elif mode=='INGRESS_CRASH_PRE_ACK_NEGATIVE':
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'WAIT_BEFORE_ACCEPT');actors.append((ip,ic))
            get(ic,'READY');t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',))
            t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1];get(ic,'PARKED')
            ip.kill();join(ip,-9)
            out,t['commit']=guarded_commit(root,'T_AFTER_INGRESS_CRASH')
            expected=out=='COMMITTED'
            label='UNACKNOWLEDGED_STOP_CAN_BE_LOST_WHEN_INGRESS_CRASHES'
        elif mode=='POST_ACK_QUEUE_32':
            ip,ic=start(ctx,stop_ingress,root,'DIRECT');actors.append((ip,ic));get(ic,'READY')
            t['request']=time.monotonic_ns();ic.send(('STOP',))
            t['receive']=get(ic,'RECEIVED')[1];t['ack']=get(ic,'ACK_DURABLE')[1];join(ip)
            outcomes=[guarded_commit(root,'Q'+str(i))[0] for i in range(32)]
            expected=outcomes.count('STOPPED')==32
            label='ALL_32_POST_ACK_DENIED'
        else:raise RuntimeError('NO_CASE_'+mode)
        result=inspect(root)
        expected=expected and result['db_ok'] and result['atomic']
        if mode in ('COMMIT_FIRST_RETAINED','WORK_LOCK_FIRST','INGRESS_CRASH_PRE_ACK_NEGATIVE'):
            expected=expected and result['rows']==1
        else:expected=expected and result['rows']==0
        if mode=='INGRESS_CRASH_PRE_ACK_NEGATIVE':expected=expected and result['state']==(1,0,0)
        else:expected=expected and result['state']==(2,1,1)
        return {'mode':mode,'status':'PASS' if expected else 'FAIL','label':label,
                'rows':result['rows'],'state':result['state'],'atomic':result['atomic'],'integrity':result['db_ok'],
                'request_ack_ms':(t['ack']-t['request'])/1e6 if 'ack' in t else None,
                'receive_ms':(t['receive']-t['request'])/1e6 if 'receive' in t else None,
                'commit_request_ms':(t['commit']-t['request'])/1e6 if t.get('commit') is not None and 'request' in t else None}
    finally:cleanup(actors)

def native_worker(binary,quit,results):
    rates=[];errs=[]
    while not quit.is_set():
        try:
            p=subprocess.run([binary,'rank','full','0.70'],text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=5)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):
                errs.append('NATIVE_S5_BAD_EXIT_OR_CONTRACTION');break
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not m:errs.append('MISSING_GMAC');break
            rates.append(float(m.group(1)))
        except BaseException as ex:errs.append(type(ex).__name__+':'+str(ex)[:80]);break
    try:results.put((os.getpid(),len(rates),min(rates) if rates else None,statistics.mean(rates) if rates else None,errs),timeout=2)
    except Exception:pass

def hold_mem(amount,quit,ready):
    try:
        a=bytearray(amount*MiB)
        for i in range(0,len(a),4096):a[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=2)
        while not quit.wait(.2):
            for i in range(0,len(a),262144):a[i]^=1
    except BaseException as ex:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',repr(ex)),timeout=2)
        except Exception:pass

def native_binary():
    p=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_NOT_FOUND: Run earlier F operator benchmark')
    x=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
    if x.returncode!=0 or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',x.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_SELF_CHECK_FAILED')
    return str(p)

def fmt(x):return '-' if x is None else f'{x:.3f}'

def stage(ctx,root,log,quick,name,n,ram,duration,repeats,binary):
    stop=ctx.Event();out=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None
    results=[];failures=0;begin=time.monotonic()
    modes=('ACK_FIRST_BLOCKS','COMMIT_FIRST_RETAINED','STOP_LOCK_FIRST','WORK_LOCK_FIRST',
           'CTRL_CRASH_PRE_ACK_INGRESS_SURVIVES','CTRL_CRASH_POST_ACK',
           'INGRESS_CRASH_PRE_ACK_NEGATIVE','POST_ACK_QUEUE_32')
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=hold_mem,args=(ram,stop,ready));holder.start()
        msg=ready.get(timeout=5)
        if msg[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('MEM_HOLDER_BAD_'+str(msg))
        for _ in range(n):
            p=ctx.Process(target=native_worker,args=(binary,stop,out));p.start();workers.append(p)
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} native_S5_workers={n} touched_ram_MiB={ram} duration_floor_s={duration}')
        for repeat in range(1,repeats+1):
            for mode in modes:
                issue=safety(quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                place=root/f'{name}_{repeat:02}_{mode}'
                r=case(ctx,place,mode);results.append(r)
                if r['status']!='PASS':failures+=1
                log('CASE stage=%s rep=%d mode=%s status=%s rows=%d stopped=%d atomic=%s integrity=%s request_ack_ms=%s receive_ms=%s commit_minus_request_ms=%s meaning=%s'%(
                    name,repeat,mode,r['status'],r['rows'],r['state'][1],r['atomic'],r['integrity'],fmt(r['request_ack_ms']),
                    fmt(r['receive_ms']),fmt(r['commit_request_ms']),r['label']))
        while time.monotonic()<begin+duration:
            issue=safety(quick)
            if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
            time.sleep(.20)
    finally:
        stop.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(4)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(1)
        if holder is not None:
            holder.join(2)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(1)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mn,avg,problems=out.get(timeout=1)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} min_GMAC_s={mn} mean_GMAC_s={avg} errors={problems}')
                if runs<1 or problems:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                log('ERROR MISSING_S5_WORKER_RESULT stage='+name);failures+=1
        out.cancel_join_thread();out.close();ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
    acks=sorted(v['request_ack_ms'] for v in results if v['request_ack_ms'] is not None)
    p95=acks[math.ceil(.95*len(acks))-1] if acks else None
    late=sum(v['mode']=='WORK_LOCK_FIRST' and v['commit_request_ms'] is not None and v['commit_request_ms']>0 for v in results)
    saved=sum(v['mode']=='CTRL_CRASH_PRE_ACK_INGRESS_SURVIVES' and v['state']==(2,1,1) for v in results)
    lost=sum(v['mode']=='INGRESS_CRASH_PRE_ACK_NEGATIVE' and v['state']==(1,0,0) for v in results)
    log('STAGE_SUMMARY stage=%s cases=%d failures=%d durable_ack_samples=%d ack_median_ms=%s ack_p95_nearest_rank_ms=%s ack_max_ms=%s pre_ack_ctrl_crash_saved=%d ingress_pre_ack_crash_lost=%d request_before_commit_windows=%d'%(
        name,len(results),failures,len(acks),fmt(statistics.median(acks) if acks else None),fmt(p95),fmt(max(acks) if acks else None),saved,lost,late))
    return failures==0,saved,lost,late

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='Linux developer smoke test; no phone conclusions')
    opt=p.parse_args()
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/('UNO_A52_R41_S5_STOP_INGRESS_DURABILITY_V4_'+stamp+'.txt')
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA R4.1 -- SEPARATE DURABLE STOP INGRESS UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('Native C256R32 S5 workers on Android CPU + 32/64MiB RAM; Python orchestrates processes, SQLite WAL FULL and advisory flock.')
            log('One independent STOP ingress journal, separate from work DB. Shared lock serializes STOP acceptance and participating commits.')
            log('STOP request != receive != durable ACK. Physical user press, Android actions and hard real-time are NOT tested.')
            log('Negative controls: pre-ACK ingress crash loses STOP; writer already holding gate may commit after STOP request and before ACK.')
            log('No idle stage, root, APK, Android dispatch, permission escalation, model learning, trained weights or system changes.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            issue=safety(opt.quick)
            if issue:log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue);return 3
            binary=native_binary();log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_S5 '+binary)
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_stop_ingress_durability_v4'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,4,1),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,5,1)] if opt.quick else [
                ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,18,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,24,2)]
            saved=lost=late=0;okay=True
            def timeout_handler(_sig,_frame):raise TimeoutError('STAGE_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT')
            old=signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM,timeout_handler)
            try:
                for name,n,ram,duration,reps in plans:
                    issue=safety(opt.quick)
                    if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                    signal.alarm(100 if n==2 else 150)
                    try:o,sv,ls,la=stage(ctx,root,log,opt.quick,name,n,ram,duration,reps,binary)
                    finally:signal.alarm(0)
                    saved+=sv;lost+=ls;late+=la;okay=okay and o
                    b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
                    if not o:break
            finally:
                signal.alarm(0);signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM,old)
            log('RESULT '+('PASS_LAB_DURABLE_INGRESS_WITH_DOCUMENTED_GAPS' if okay else 'FAIL_INVARIANT')+
                f' controller_crash_pre_ack_stop_survived={saved} ingress_crash_pre_ack_stop_lost={lost} request_before_commit_windows={late}')
            log('LIMIT: ACK is durable only after separate SQLite FULL COMMIT; request before ACK can lose on ingress failure.')
            log('LIMIT: shared fcntl lock is ADVISORY, not Android permission boundary; all writers must cooperate. No power-cut test.')
            log('LIMIT: commit before STOP ACK remains possible if writer locked first; no guarantee from user physical press, no external OS action rollback.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report));return 0 if okay else 2
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPT');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as ex:
            log('RESULT ERROR '+type(ex).__name__+' '+str(ex)[:220]);log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 V5: fail-closed restart + ingress watchdog laboratory, under native S5 load.
This is PRECODE experiment, not an implementation of real Android STOP or owner authentication.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, datetime, math, multiprocessing as mp, os, queue, re, signal
import sqlite3, statistics, subprocess, sys, time, traceback
from pathlib import Path

VERSION = 'UNO_R41_A52_S5_FAILCLOSED_RESTART_WATCHDOG_STRESS_V5'
MiB = 1048576
MODES = (
    'COLD_START_DENIES', 'LAB_ARM_COMMIT', 'CTRL_CRASH_RESTART_DISARMS',
    'PREACK_INGRESS_CRASH_RESTART_DISARMS', 'DURABLE_STOP_RESTART_DENIES',
    'STALE_EPOCH_AFTER_REARM', 'DUPLICATE_AFTER_REARM',
    'WATCHDOG_INGRESS_LOSS', 'WATCHDOG_PREACK_STOP_LOSS',
    'NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG',
)

def read_i(path):
    try: return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError, ValueError): return None

def telemetry():
    try: mem = next(int(x.split()[1]) for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
    except (OSError, ValueError, StopIteration, IndexError): mem = None
    return read_i('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'), read_i('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'), mem

def safety(quick):
    if quick:return None
    batt,temp,mem=telemetry()
    if batt is None or temp is None or mem is None or not 100 <= temp <= 800: return 'REQUIRED_SENSOR_UNAVAILABLE'
    if batt < 40: return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40_PCT'
    if temp >= 375: return 'BATTERY_AT_OR_ABOVE_37_5_C'
    if mem < 400000: return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def db(root):
    conn=sqlite3.connect(str(root/'lab.sqlite3'), timeout=7, isolation_level=None)
    conn.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=7000')
    conn.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return conn

def init_case(root):
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False, mode=0o700)
    con=db(root)
    try:
        con.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        con.execute('CREATE TABLE control(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, epoch INTEGER NOT NULL, armed INTEGER NOT NULL, stopped INTEGER NOT NULL, ingress_ok INTEGER NOT NULL, seq INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        con.execute('INSERT INTO control VALUES(1,1,0,0,0,0)')
        for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
            con.execute(f'CREATE TABLE {tab}(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY,epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    finally:con.close()

def state(root):
    con=db(root)
    try:return tuple(con.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped,ingress_ok,seq FROM control WHERE id=1').fetchone())
    finally:con.close()

def transact(root,op):
    con=db(root)
    try:
        con.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        s=tuple(con.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped,ingress_ok,seq FROM control WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        if op=='NEW_BOOT':
            con.execute('UPDATE control SET epoch=epoch+1,armed=0,ingress_ok=0 WHERE id=1')
            result=('BOOT_DISARMED',s[0]+1)
        elif op=='LAB_INGRESS_READY':
            if s[2]:result=('STOP_LATCHED',s[0])
            else:
                con.execute('UPDATE control SET ingress_ok=1 WHERE id=1')
                result=('LAB_INGRESS_READY',s[0])
        elif op=='LAB_ARM':
            if s[2] or not s[3]:result=('ARM_DENIED',s[0])
            else:
                con.execute('UPDATE control SET armed=1 WHERE id=1')
                result=('LAB_ARMED',s[0])
        elif op=='DURABLE_STOP':
            if not s[2]:con.execute('UPDATE control SET stopped=1,armed=0,ingress_ok=0,epoch=epoch+1,seq=seq+1 WHERE id=1')
            result=('DURABLE_STOP',s[0]+(0 if s[2] else 1))
        else:raise ValueError('UNSUPPORTED_OP')
        con.execute('COMMIT');return result
    finally:
        if con.in_transaction:con.execute('ROLLBACK')
        con.close()

def guarded_commit(root,ticket,epoch):
    con=db(root)
    try:
        con.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        s=tuple(con.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped,ingress_ok,seq FROM control WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        if s[2]:ans='STOPPED'
        elif epoch!=s[0]:ans='STALE_EPOCH'
        elif not s[1]:ans='DISARMED'
        elif not s[3]:ans='NO_INGRESS'
        elif con.execute('SELECT 1 FROM receipts WHERE ticket=?',(ticket,)).fetchone():ans='DUPLICATE'
        else:
            for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
                con.execute(f'INSERT INTO {tab}(ticket,epoch) VALUES(?,?)',(ticket,epoch))
            ans='COMMITTED'
        con.execute('COMMIT')
        return ans,time.monotonic_ns()
    finally:
        if con.in_transaction:con.execute('ROLLBACK')
        con.close()

def inspect(root):
    con=db(root)
    try:
        s=tuple(con.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped,ingress_ok,seq FROM control WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        sets=[set(con.execute('SELECT ticket,epoch FROM '+tab).fetchall()) for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity')]
        return s,len(sets[0]),(sets[0]==sets[1]==sets[2]),con.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]=='ok'
    finally:con.close()

def send(ch,msg):
    try:ch.send(msg)
    except (BrokenPipeError,EOFError,OSError):pass

def ingress_actor(heartbeat,ch):
    try:
        heartbeat.value=time.monotonic_ns();send(ch,('READY',os.getpid()))
        while True:
            heartbeat.value=time.monotonic_ns()
            if ch.poll(.02):
                cmd=ch.recv()
                if cmd=='STOP':
                    send(ch,('STOP_RECEIVED',time.monotonic_ns()))
                    # Deliberately wait: parent will simulate ingress crash BEFORE durable STOP.
                    while True:time.sleep(.05)
                if cmd=='QUIT':break
    except (EOFError,ConnectionResetError):pass
    finally:ch.close()

def watchdog_actor(root,heartbeat,timeout_s,ch):
    try:
        send(ch,('READY',os.getpid()))
        start=time.monotonic()
        while True:
            if time.monotonic()-start>6:send(ch,('ERROR','WATCHDOG_NO_INGRESS_LOSS'));return
            age=(time.monotonic_ns()-heartbeat.value)/1e9 if heartbeat.value else 0
            if age>timeout_s:
                t_detect=time.monotonic_ns()
                # Fail-closed durable latch (same SQLite transaction ordering as commits).
                transact(root,'DURABLE_STOP')
                t_latch=time.monotonic_ns()
                send(ch,('LATCHED',t_detect,t_latch,age*1000));return
            time.sleep(.015)
    except BaseException as exc:send(ch,('ERROR',type(exc).__name__,str(exc)[:100]))
    finally:ch.close()

def dummy_controller(ch):
    send(ch,('READY',os.getpid()))
    while True:time.sleep(.05)

def blocked_writer(root,ch):
    con=None
    try:
        con=db(root);con.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        s=tuple(con.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped,ingress_ok,seq FROM control WHERE id=1').fetchone())
        if not(s[1] and s[3] and not s[2]):raise RuntimeError('WRITER_NOT_ARMED')
        for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
            con.execute(f'INSERT INTO {tab}(ticket,epoch) VALUES(?,?)',('INFLIGHT',s[0]))
        send(ch,('HELD',time.monotonic_ns()))
        if not ch.poll(4):raise TimeoutError('WRITER_NOT_RELEASED')
        if ch.recv()!='RELEASE':raise RuntimeError('WRITER_BAD_RELEASE')
        con.execute('COMMIT')
        send(ch,('COMMITTED',time.monotonic_ns()))
    except BaseException as exc:send(ch,('ERROR',type(exc).__name__,str(exc)[:120]))
    finally:
        if con is not None:
            if con.in_transaction:con.execute('ROLLBACK')
            con.close()
        ch.close()

def start(ctx,target,*args):
    parent,child=ctx.Pipe()
    proc=ctx.Process(target=target,args=(*args,child))
    proc.start();child.close()
    return proc,parent

def recv(ch,expected,timeout=8):
    if not ch.poll(timeout):raise TimeoutError(f'WAIT_{expected}')
    msg=ch.recv()
    if msg[0]!=expected:raise RuntimeError(f'EXPECTED_{expected}_GOT_{msg}')
    return msg

def kill_and_reap(proc):
    if proc.is_alive():proc.kill()
    proc.join(3)
    if proc.is_alive():raise TimeoutError('PROCESS_NOT_KILLED')

def cleanup(actors):
    for proc,ch in reversed(actors):
        try:kill_and_reap(proc)
        except Exception:pass
        try:ch.close()
        except Exception:pass

def run_case(ctx,root,mode):
    init_case(root)
    actors=[];start_ns=time.monotonic_ns();out='';expect=False;watch_ms=None
    try:
        if mode=='COLD_START_DENIES':
            out=guarded_commit(root,'T0',1)[0]
            expect=out=='DISARMED'
        elif mode=='LAB_ARM_COMMIT':
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');arm=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')
            out=guarded_commit(root,'T1',arm[1])[0]
            expect=arm[0]=='LAB_ARMED' and out=='COMMITTED'
        elif mode=='CTRL_CRASH_RESTART_DISARMS':
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');arm=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')
            p,ch=start(ctx,dummy_controller);actors.append((p,ch));recv(ch,'READY');kill_and_reap(p)
            boot=transact(root,'NEW_BOOT')
            out=guarded_commit(root,'T1',boot[1])[0]
            expect=out=='DISARMED' and state(root)[2]==0
        elif mode=='PREACK_INGRESS_CRASH_RESTART_DISARMS':
            beat=ctx.Value('Q',0,lock=False);ip,ic=start(ctx,ingress_actor,beat);actors.append((ip,ic));recv(ic,'READY')
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');transact(root,'LAB_ARM')
            ic.send('STOP');recv(ic,'STOP_RECEIVED');kill_and_reap(ip)
            boot=transact(root,'NEW_BOOT')
            out=guarded_commit(root,'T1',boot[1])[0]
            expect=out=='DISARMED' and state(root)[2]==0
        elif mode=='DURABLE_STOP_RESTART_DENIES':
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');transact(root,'LAB_ARM')
            transact(root,'DURABLE_STOP');boot=transact(root,'NEW_BOOT')
            arm=transact(root,'LAB_ARM');out=guarded_commit(root,'T1',boot[1])[0]
            expect=out=='STOPPED' and arm[0]=='ARM_DENIED'
        elif mode=='STALE_EPOCH_AFTER_REARM':
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');old=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')[1]
            transact(root,'NEW_BOOT');transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');fresh=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')[1]
            old_result=guarded_commit(root,'T1',old)[0];out=guarded_commit(root,'T1',fresh)[0]
            expect=old_result=='STALE_EPOCH' and out=='COMMITTED'
            out='old='+old_result+',new='+out
        elif mode=='DUPLICATE_AFTER_REARM':
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');old=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')[1]
            first=guarded_commit(root,'T1',old)[0]
            transact(root,'NEW_BOOT');transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');fresh=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')[1]
            out=guarded_commit(root,'T1',fresh)[0]
            expect=first=='COMMITTED' and out=='DUPLICATE'
        elif mode in ('WATCHDOG_INGRESS_LOSS','WATCHDOG_PREACK_STOP_LOSS','NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG'):
            beat=ctx.Value('Q',0,lock=False)
            ip,ic=start(ctx,ingress_actor,beat);actors.append((ip,ic));recv(ic,'READY')
            transact(root,'LAB_INGRESS_READY');arm=transact(root,'LAB_ARM')
            wp,wc=start(ctx,watchdog_actor,root,beat,.25);actors.append((wp,wc));recv(wc,'READY')
            tx_t=None;kill_time=None
            if mode=='NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG':
                tp,tc=start(ctx,blocked_writer,root);actors.append((tp,tc));recv(tc,'HELD')
            if mode in ('WATCHDOG_PREACK_STOP_LOSS','NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG'):
                ic.send('STOP');recv(ic,'STOP_RECEIVED')
            kill_time=time.monotonic_ns();kill_and_reap(ip)
            if mode=='NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG':
                # A writer already inside its SQLite transaction can win before watchdog STOP.
                time.sleep(.32)
                tc.send('RELEASE');tx_t=recv(tc,'COMMITTED')[1]
            seen=recv(wc,'LATCHED',timeout=8)
            latch_time=seen[2];watch_ms=(latch_time-kill_time)/1e6
            out=guarded_commit(root,'T2',arm[1])[0]
            if mode=='NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG':
                expect=(tx_t is not None and tx_t>kill_time and tx_t<=latch_time and out=='STOPPED')
            else:expect=(out=='STOPPED')
            out+=f',kill_to_latch_ms={watch_ms:.3f}'
        else:raise ValueError('UNSUPPORTED_MODE')
        st,n,atomic,integrity=inspect(root)
        if mode=='NEGATIVE_HELD_TX_BLOCKS_WATCHDOG':expect=expect and n==1 and st[2]==1
        if mode=='WATCHDOG_INGRESS_LOSS' or mode=='WATCHDOG_PREACK_STOP_LOSS':expect=expect and n==0 and st[2]==1
        if not atomic or not integrity:expect=False
        return dict(mode=mode,status='PASS' if expect else 'FAIL',result=out,rows=n,stopped=st[2],armed=st[1],epoch=st[0],atomic=atomic,integrity=integrity,watch_ms=watch_ms,elapsed_ms=(time.monotonic_ns()-start_ns)/1e6)
    finally:cleanup(actors)

def native_worker(binary,quit,results):
    values=[];errors=[]
    while not quit.is_set():
        try:
            p=subprocess.run([binary,'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):
                errors.append('NATIVE_S5_EXEC_OR_CHECK_FAILED');break
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not m:errors.append('MISSING_RATE');break
            values.append(float(m.group(1)))
        except BaseException as exc:errors.append(type(exc).__name__+':'+str(exc)[:80]);break
    try:results.put((os.getpid(),len(values),min(values) if values else None,statistics.mean(values) if values else None,errors),timeout=2)
    except Exception:pass

def hold_mem(megabytes,quit,ready):
    try:
        area=bytearray(megabytes*MiB)
        for i in range(0,len(area),4096):area[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=2)
        while not quit.wait(.2):
            for i in range(0,len(area),262144):area[i]^=1
    except BaseException as exc:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(exc)[:100]),timeout=2)
        except Exception:pass

def native_binary(override=None):
    binary=Path(override) if override else Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not binary.is_file() or not os.access(binary,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_MISSING: run earlier S5 operator benchmark')
    p=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_SELFTEST_FAILED')
    return str(binary)

def stage(ctx,root,log,quick,name,n,ram,duration,repeats,binary):
    stop=ctx.Event();out=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None
    results=[];failures=0;started=time.monotonic();watch_latencies=[]
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=hold_mem,args=(ram,stop,ready));holder.start()
        msg=ready.get(timeout=5)
        if msg[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('MEM_HOLDER_FAILED '+str(msg))
        for _ in range(n):
            p=ctx.Process(target=native_worker,args=(binary,stop,out));p.start();workers.append(p)
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} native_S5_workers={n} touched_ram_MiB={ram} duration_floor_s={duration}')
        for rep in range(1,repeats+1):
            for mode in MODES:
                issue=safety(quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                try:r=run_case(ctx,root/f'{name}_{rep}_{mode}',mode)
                except Exception as exc:
                    failures+=1
                    log(f'CASE stage={name} rep={rep} mode={mode} status=ERROR error={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}')
                    continue
                results.append(r)
                if r['status']!='PASS':failures+=1
                if r['watch_ms'] is not None:watch_latencies.append(r['watch_ms'])
                log('CASE stage=%s rep=%d mode=%s status=%s outcome=%s rows=%d stopped=%d armed=%d epoch=%d atomic=%s integrity=%s elapsed_ms=%.3f'%(
                    name,rep,mode,r['status'],r['result'],r['rows'],r['stopped'],r['armed'],r['epoch'],r['atomic'],r['integrity'],r['elapsed_ms']))
        while time.monotonic()<started+duration:
            issue=safety(quick)
            if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
            time.sleep(.15)
    finally:
        stop.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(4)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(1)
        if holder is not None:
            holder.join(2)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(1)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mn,avg,errors=out.get(timeout=1)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} min_GMAC_s={mn} mean_GMAC_s={avg} errors={errors}')
                if runs<1 or errors:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                log('FAIL MISSING_WORKER_RESULT stage='+name);failures+=1
        out.cancel_join_thread();out.close();ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY name={name} cases={len(results)} expected={repeats*len(MODES)} failures={failures} watchdog_samples={len(watch_latencies)} kill_to_durable_stop_max_ms={max(watch_latencies) if watch_latencies else None}')
    return failures==0 and len(results)==repeats*len(MODES),results

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='LOCAL developer smoke test; NOT valid hardware evidence')
    parser.add_argument('--binary',help='LOCAL developer mock native S5 binary; omit on A52')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home

    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(text):print(text,flush=True);f.write(text+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- FAIL-CLOSED RESTART + INGRESS WATCHDOG UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('Native ARM C256R32 S5 on Android CPU; Python orchestrates synthetic controller SQLite WAL FULL and watchdog.')
            log('No idle phase: all checks run under 2 native S5 +32 MiB and 4 native S5 +64 MiB.')
            log('LAB_ARM is SYNTHETIC and is NOT owner authorization; no true Android actions or neural training.')
            log('Critical scope: restart begins DISARMED; watchdog can latch STOP after ingress loss, not instant at physical user press.')
            log('Negative control: an in-flight SQLite transaction can commit after ingress crash before watchdog STOP latch.')
            log('No root, ADB, USB, network, Android permission escalation, forced reboot, or production changes.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            issue=safety(args.quick)
            if issue:log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue);return 3
            binary=native_binary(args.binary);log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_S5 '+binary)
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_failclosed_watchdog_v5'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork');total=0;allpass=True
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,6,1),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,7,1)] if args.quick else [
                ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,24,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,30,2)]
            for name,n,ram,duration,repeats in plans:
                issue=safety(args.quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                passed,results=stage(ctx,root,log,args.quick,name,n,ram,duration,repeats,binary)
                allpass=allpass and passed;total+=len(results)
                b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
                if not passed:break
            log('RESULT '+('PASS_BOUNDED_FAILCLOSED_RESTART_AND_WATCHDOG' if allpass else 'FAIL_OR_INCOMPLETE')+f' directed_cases={total}')
            log('UNRESOLVED: controller or watchdog and ingress simultaneous crash while live; window before watchdog latches; strict STOP from physical press; real Android authorization and dispatch.')
            log('LIMIT: a restart is disarmed only in this isolated laboratory; LAB_ARM is not authenticated; no platform hardware durability or full UNO inference proof.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report))
            return 0 if allpass else 2
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPT');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as exc:
            log('RESULT ERROR '+type(exc).__name__+': '+str(exc)[:220])
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 V6 bounded PRECODE lab: simultaneous guard failure, vote replay, and same-UID bypass.
All staged cases run while native ARM S5 operator jobs occupy the phone CPU.
NOT a secure authorization protocol; synthetic votes and data; no Android control.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, datetime, multiprocessing as mp, os, queue, re, secrets, sqlite3
import statistics, subprocess, sys, time, traceback
from pathlib import Path

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_S5_DUAL_FAILURE_AND_BYPASS_STRESS_V6'
MiB=1048576
PERMIT_TTL_NS=750_000_000
MODES=(
 'COLD_BOOT_DENIED','BOTH_GUARDS_ALIVE_COMMIT',
 'INGRESS_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE','WATCHDOG_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE','BOTH_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE',
 'ONE_SHOT_VOTE_REPLAY_DENIED','VOTE_EXPIRED_DENIED',
 'NEGATIVE_BOTH_DIE_AFTER_VOTES','NEGATIVE_BOTH_DIE_WITH_OPEN_TX',
 'BOTH_DIE_RESTART_DISARMED','DURABLE_STOP_DUAL_CRASH_RESTART',
 'NEGATIVE_SAME_UID_DIRECT_DB_WRITE',
)
NEGATIVE={'NEGATIVE_BOTH_DIE_AFTER_VOTES','NEGATIVE_BOTH_DIE_WITH_OPEN_TX',
          'NEGATIVE_SAME_UID_DIRECT_DB_WRITE'}

def read_int(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (ValueError,OSError):return None

def telemetry():
    try: mem=next(int(s.split()[1]) for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
    except (OSError,StopIteration,IndexError,ValueError):mem=None
    return read_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),read_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),mem

def safety(quick):
    if quick:return None
    battery,temp,avail=telemetry()
    if battery is None or temp is None or avail is None or not 100 <= temp <= 800:return 'SENSORS_UNAVAILABLE'
    if battery < 40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40'
    if temp >= 375:return 'BATTERY_TEMPERATURE_AT_LEAST_37_5_C'
    if avail < 400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def connect(root):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(root/'synthetic_control.sqlite3'),timeout=7,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=7000')
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return c

def initialize(root):
    root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False,mode=0o700)
    c=connect(root)
    try:
        c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE state(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),epoch INTEGER NOT NULL,armed INTEGER NOT NULL,stopped INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        c.execute('INSERT INTO state VALUES(1,1,0,0)')
        for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
            c.execute(f'CREATE TABLE {tab}(ticket TEXT PRIMARY KEY,epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE used_votes(nonce TEXT PRIMARY KEY,ticket TEXT NOT NULL)')
    finally:c.close()

def transition(root,command):
    c=connect(root)
    try:
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        epoch,armed,stopped=c.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if command=='LAB_ARM_SYNTHETIC':
            out='STOPPED' if stopped else 'LAB_ARMED'
            if not stopped:c.execute('UPDATE state SET armed=1 WHERE id=1')
        elif command=='NEW_BOOT':
            c.execute('UPDATE state SET epoch=epoch+1,armed=0 WHERE id=1');out='BOOT_DISARMED'
        elif command=='STOP_DURABLE':
            c.execute('UPDATE state SET stopped=1,armed=0,epoch=epoch+1 WHERE id=1');out='STOP_DURABLE'
        else:raise ValueError('UNKNOWN_TRANSITION')
        c.execute('COMMIT');return out
    finally:
        if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
        c.close()

def put_record(c,ticket,epoch,nonce):
    for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
        c.execute(f'INSERT INTO {tab}(ticket,epoch) VALUES(?,?)',(ticket,epoch))
    c.execute('INSERT INTO used_votes(nonce,ticket) VALUES(?,?)',(nonce,ticket))

def commit(root,ticket,epoch,permit):
    """A deliberately cooperative lab writer. Cannot prevent same-UID noncooperative writes."""
    c=connect(root)
    try:
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        now=time.monotonic_ns()
        current,armed,stopped=c.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if stopped:out='STOPPED'
        elif not armed:out='DISARMED'
        elif epoch!=current:out='STALE_EPOCH'
        elif permit is None:out='NO_DUAL_VOTE'
        elif now-permit['issued_ns']<0 or now-permit['issued_ns']>PERMIT_TTL_NS:out='EXPIRED_VOTE'
        elif c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM used_votes WHERE nonce=?',(permit['nonce'],)).fetchone():out='VOTE_REPLAY'
        elif c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM receipts WHERE ticket=?',(ticket,)).fetchone():out='DUPLICATE_TICKET'
        else:
            put_record(c,ticket,epoch,permit['nonce']);out='COMMITTED'
        c.execute('COMMIT');return out,time.monotonic_ns()
    finally:
        if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
        c.close()

def inspect(root):
    c=connect(root)
    try:
        st=c.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        sets=[set(c.execute(f'SELECT ticket,epoch FROM {tab}').fetchall()) for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity')]
        used=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM used_votes').fetchone()[0]
        good=c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]=='ok'
        return st,len(sets[0]),sets[0]==sets[1]==sets[2] and len(sets[0])==used,good
    finally:c.close()

def safe_send(ch,value):
    try:ch.send(value)
    except (BrokenPipeError,EOFError,OSError):pass

def guard_actor(kind,ch):
    try:
        safe_send(ch,('READY',kind,os.getpid()))
        while True:
            if not ch.poll(.05):continue
            msg=ch.recv()
            if msg=='QUIT':return
            if not isinstance(msg,tuple) or len(msg)!=2 or msg[0]!='VOTE':continue
            safe_send(ch,('YES',kind,msg[1],time.monotonic_ns()))
    except (EOFError,ConnectionResetError,OSError):pass
    finally:ch.close()

def bypass_actor(root,ch):
    """Negative control: a process with the same OS UID ignores all lab gates."""
    try:
        c=connect(root)
        try:
            c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            ep,armed,stopped=c.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
            for tab in ('receipts','memory','plasticity'):
                c.execute(f'INSERT INTO {tab}(ticket,epoch) VALUES(?,?)',('DIRECT_BYPASS',ep))
            c.execute('INSERT INTO used_votes(nonce,ticket) VALUES(?,?)',('BYPASS_NO_VOTES','DIRECT_BYPASS'))
            c.execute('COMMIT')
            safe_send(ch,('BYPASS_WRITTEN',stopped,armed,os.getuid()))
        finally:
            if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
            c.close()
    except BaseException as exc:safe_send(ch,('ERROR',type(exc).__name__,str(exc)[:90]))
    finally:ch.close()

def begin_actor(ctx,fn,*args):
    par,child=ctx.Pipe()
    p=ctx.Process(target=fn,args=(*args,child));p.start();child.close()
    return p,par

def expect(ch,what,timeout=8):
    if not ch.poll(timeout):raise TimeoutError('WAIT_'+what)
    result=ch.recv()
    if result[0]!=what:raise RuntimeError(f'EXPECTED_{what}_GOT_{result}')
    return result

def kill_actor(p):
    if p.is_alive():p.kill()
    p.join(3)
    if p.is_alive():raise TimeoutError('CHILD_STILL_ALIVE')

def actors(ctx):
    ap,ac=begin_actor(ctx,guard_actor,'INGRESS');expect(ac,'READY')
    wp,wc=begin_actor(ctx,guard_actor,'WATCHDOG');expect(wc,'READY')
    return [(ap,ac),(wp,wc)]

def approve(pair):
    """Synthetic dual-vote protocol; votes are not cryptographically authenticated."""
    if any(not p.is_alive() for p,_ in pair):return None
    nonce=secrets.token_hex(12)
    for _,ch in pair:safe_send(ch,('VOTE',nonce))
    for i,(_,ch) in enumerate(pair):
        try:
            if not ch.poll(3):return None
            response=ch.recv()
        except (EOFError,OSError):return None
        if response[:3]!=('YES',('INGRESS','WATCHDOG')[i],nonce):return None
    return {'nonce':nonce,'issued_ns':time.monotonic_ns()}

def cleanup(pair):
    for p,ch in reversed(pair):
        try:kill_actor(p)
        except BaseException:pass
        try:ch.close()
        except BaseException:pass

def run_case(ctx,root,mode):
    initialize(root)
    stage_ns=time.monotonic_ns();group=[];extra='';result='';valid=False
    try:
        if mode=='COLD_BOOT_DENIED':
            result,_=commit(root,'T',1,None);valid=result=='DISARMED'
        elif mode=='NEGATIVE_SAME_UID_DIRECT_DB_WRITE':
            transition(root,'STOP_DURABLE')
            p,ch=begin_actor(ctx,bypass_actor,root);group.append((p,ch))
            observed=expect(ch,'BYPASS_WRITTEN');p.join(4)
            if p.is_alive():raise TimeoutError('BYPASS_ACTOR_TIMEOUT')
            result='SAME_UID_BYPASS_OBSERVED'
            extra=f'actor_stopped={observed[1]} actor_uid={observed[3]}'
            valid=observed[1]==1 and observed[2]==0 and os.getuid()==observed[3]
        else:
            group=actors(ctx);transition(root,'LAB_ARM_SYNTHETIC')
            if mode=='INGRESS_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE':kill_actor(group[0][0])
            if mode=='WATCHDOG_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE':kill_actor(group[1][0])
            if mode=='BOTH_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE':
                for p,_ in group:kill_actor(p)
            if mode in ('INGRESS_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE','WATCHDOG_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE','BOTH_DEAD_BEFORE_VOTE'):
                permit=approve(group)
                result,_=commit(root,'T',1,permit);valid=result=='NO_DUAL_VOTE'
            elif mode=='BOTH_GUARDS_ALIVE_COMMIT':
                permit=approve(group)
                result,_=commit(root,'T',1,permit);valid=result=='COMMITTED'
            elif mode=='ONE_SHOT_VOTE_REPLAY_DENIED':
                permit=approve(group);first,_=commit(root,'T1',1,permit)
                result,_=commit(root,'T2',1,permit)
                valid=first=='COMMITTED' and result=='VOTE_REPLAY'
                extra='first='+first
            elif mode=='VOTE_EXPIRED_DENIED':
                permit=approve(group)
                time.sleep(.88)
                result,_=commit(root,'T',1,permit);valid=result=='EXPIRED_VOTE'
            elif mode=='NEGATIVE_BOTH_DIE_AFTER_VOTES':
                permit=approve(group)
                if permit is None:raise RuntimeError('VOTE_NOT_READY')
                for p,_ in group:kill_actor(p)
                lastdeath=time.monotonic_ns()
                result,committed=commit(root,'T',1,permit)
                extra=f'commit_after_both_deaths_ms={(committed-lastdeath)/1e6:.3f}'
                valid=result=='COMMITTED' and committed>lastdeath
            elif mode=='NEGATIVE_BOTH_DIE_WITH_OPEN_TX':
                permit=approve(group)
                if permit is None:raise RuntimeError('VOTE_NOT_READY')
                c=connect(root)
                try:
                    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                    ep,armed,stopped=c.execute('SELECT epoch,armed,stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
                    if ep!=1 or not armed or stopped:raise RuntimeError('GATE_NOT_ARMED')
                    put_record(c,'INFLIGHT',ep,permit['nonce'])
                    for p,_ in group:kill_actor(p)
                    lastdeath=time.monotonic_ns()
                    c.execute('COMMIT');committed=time.monotonic_ns()
                    result='INFLIGHT_COMMITTED_AFTER_BOTH_DEAD'
                    extra=f'commit_after_both_deaths_ms={(committed-lastdeath)/1e6:.3f}'
                    valid=committed>lastdeath
                finally:
                    if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
                    c.close()
            elif mode=='BOTH_DIE_RESTART_DISARMED':
                permit=approve(group)
                for p,_ in group:kill_actor(p)
                transition(root,'NEW_BOOT')
                result,_=commit(root,'T',2,permit);valid=result=='DISARMED'
            elif mode=='DURABLE_STOP_DUAL_CRASH_RESTART':
                permit=approve(group)
                transition(root,'STOP_DURABLE')
                for p,_ in group:kill_actor(p)
                transition(root,'NEW_BOOT')
                arm=transition(root,'LAB_ARM_SYNTHETIC')
                result,_=commit(root,'T',3,permit)
                valid=arm=='STOPPED' and result=='STOPPED'
            else:raise ValueError('UNKNOWN_MODE')
        st,rows,atomic,integrity=inspect(root)
        if mode in NEGATIVE:
            valid=valid and rows==1 and atomic and integrity
        if mode in ('BOTH_GUARDS_ALIVE_COMMIT','ONE_SHOT_VOTE_REPLAY_DENIED'):
            valid=valid and rows==1
        if mode not in NEGATIVE and mode not in ('BOTH_GUARDS_ALIVE_COMMIT','ONE_SHOT_VOTE_REPLAY_DENIED'):
            valid=valid and rows==0
        valid=valid and atomic and integrity
        return dict(mode=mode,status='EXPECTED_BEHAVIOR_PASS' if valid else 'FAIL',outcome=result,
            rows=rows,epoch=st[0],armed=st[1],stopped=st[2],atomic=atomic,integrity=integrity,
            negative=mode in NEGATIVE,extra=extra,elapsed_ms=(time.monotonic_ns()-stage_ns)/1e6)
    finally:cleanup(group)

def native_worker(binary,stop,out):
    measurements=[];errors=[]
    while not stop.is_set():
        try:
            run=subprocess.run([binary,'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
            if run.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',run.stdout):
                errors.append('S5_NATIVE_CONTRACT_FAIL');break
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',run.stdout)
            if not m:errors.append('MISSING_F_GMAC_RATE');break
            measurements.append(float(m.group(1)))
        except BaseException as exc:errors.append(type(exc).__name__+':'+str(exc)[:60]);break
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(measurements),min(measurements) if measurements else None,
        statistics.mean(measurements) if measurements else None,errors),timeout=2)
    except BaseException:pass

def memory_holder(megabytes,stop,ready):
    try:
        buf=bytearray(megabytes*MiB)
        for i in range(0,len(buf),4096):buf[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=3)
        while not stop.wait(.2):
            for i in range(0,len(buf),262144):buf[i]^=1
    except BaseException as exc:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(exc)[:100]),timeout=1)
        except BaseException:pass

def native_binary(override=None):
    path=Path(override) if override else Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not path.is_file() or not os.access(path,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_BINARY_MISSING')
    run=subprocess.run([str(path),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
    if run.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',run.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_SELFTEST_FAILURE')
    return str(path)

def stage(ctx,root,log,quick,name,n,ram,duration,repeats,binary):
    stop=ctx.Event();res=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None
    cases=[];errors=0;began=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=memory_holder,args=(ram,stop,ready));holder.start()
        msg=ready.get(timeout=6)
        if msg[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED '+str(msg))
        for _ in range(n):
            p=ctx.Process(target=native_worker,args=(binary,stop,res));p.start();workers.append(p)
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} native_S5_workers={n} touched_RAM_MiB={ram} duration_floor_s={duration}')
        for repeat in range(1,repeats+1):
            for mode in MODES:
                issue=safety(quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                try:r=run_case(ctx,root/f'{name}_{repeat}_{mode}',mode)
                except BaseException as exc:
                    errors+=1;log(f'CASE stage={name} rep={repeat} mode={mode} status=ERROR exception={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}');continue
                cases.append(r)
                if r['status']!='EXPECTED_BEHAVIOR_PASS':errors+=1
                log(f"CASE stage={name} rep={repeat} mode={mode} status={r['status']} outcome={r['outcome']} "
                    f"rows={r['rows']} epoch={r['epoch']} armed={r['armed']} stopped={r['stopped']} "
                    f"atomic={r['atomic']} integrity={r['integrity']} negative_control={r['negative']} "
                    f"elapsed_ms={r['elapsed_ms']:.3f} {r['extra']}")
        while time.monotonic()<began+duration:
            issue=safety(quick)
            if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
            time.sleep(.15)
    finally:
        stop.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(4)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(2)
        if holder is not None:
            holder.join(2)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,minimum,mean,failures=res.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} min_GMAC_s={minimum} mean_GMAC_s={mean} errors={failures}')
                if runs<1 or failures:errors+=1
            except queue.Empty:errors+=1;log('S5_WORKER_FAILURE missing_result')
        res.cancel_join_thread();res.close();ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
    negative_count=sum(c['negative'] and c['status']=='EXPECTED_BEHAVIOR_PASS' for c in cases)
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={name} cases={len(cases)} expected={repeats*len(MODES)} '
        f'failures={errors} negative_controls_reproduced={negative_count} '
        f'restart_denials={sum(c["mode"]=="BOTH_DIE_RESTART_DISARMED" and c["status"]=="EXPECTED_BEHAVIOR_PASS" for c in cases)}')
    return errors==0 and len(cases)==repeats*len(MODES),cases

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='local smoke test ONLY, not phone evidence')
    p.add_argument('--binary',help='local S5 binary override; omit on phone')
    args=p.parse_args()
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_DUAL_FAILURE_BYPASS_STRESS_V6_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as file:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);file.write(s+'\n');file.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- DUAL GUARD CRASH, PERMIT REPLAY AND SAME-UID BYPASS UNDER REAL NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('Synthetic ingress + watchdog roles; two one-shot per-commit votes. Not cryptographic owner authentication.')
            log('All cases run while native ARM C256R32 FP32 S5 is computing on Android CPU (2 or 4 workers + 32/64MiB RAM).')
            log('Negative controls intentionally show: both guards dead AFTER approval can still be followed by COMMIT; same-UID DB writer can bypass app gates.')
            log('This is a PRECODE fixture, not a real trusted Android permission boundary, user STOP, production agent, or neural P training.')
            log('No Android actions, root, USB, ADB, network, reboot, or modification outside own Termux lab directory.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            issue=safety(args.quick)
            if issue:log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue);return 3
            binary=native_binary(args.binary);log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_S5 '+binary)
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_dual_guard_stress_v6'/stamp;root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,7,1),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,8,1)] if args.quick else [
                ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,24,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,30,2)]
            total=0;observed=0;allpass=True
            for name,n,ram,duration,reps in plans:
                issue=safety(args.quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                good,cases=stage(ctx,root,log,args.quick,name,n,ram,duration,reps,binary)
                total+=len(cases);observed+=sum(c['negative'] and c['status']=='EXPECTED_BEHAVIOR_PASS' for c in cases)
                allpass=allpass and good
                b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
                if not good:break
            log(f'RESULT {"EXPECTED_FINDINGS_REPRODUCED" if allpass else "FAIL_OR_INCOMPLETE"} cases={total} negative_control_findings={observed}')
            log('IMPORTANT: expected negative findings are NOT safety passes. Live actors before commit are NOT enough to prevent post-check commit after both die.')
            log('IMPORTANT: lab SQLite gates cannot enforce Android trust isolation against same-UID writers. Need process/UID/capability separation for real controller.')
            log('UNRESOLVED: user STOP physical press, platform-level isolation, power cut, already linearized Android effects, hard real-time SLA.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report))
            return 0 if allpass else 2
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPT');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as exc:
            log('RESULT ERROR '+type(exc).__name__+': '+str(exc)[:200]);log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())
