#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 V7 precode lab: typed IPC event backpressure under native S5 load.
Synthetic event queues, NOT a secure Android boundary, DSP/ASR, or a real neural implementation.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE=FALSE. No OS dispatch, root, privilege elevation or network.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, datetime, json, math, multiprocessing as mp, os, queue, re
import sqlite3, statistics, subprocess, sys, time, traceback
from pathlib import Path

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_EVENT_BACKPRESSURE_STRESS_V7'
MiB=1048576; CAP=65536; QUEUE_LIMIT=24; SENSOR_SLOTS=16; PCM_LIMIT=8
KINDS={'SENSOR','PCM','SCREEN','TASK','BARRIER'}

def read_int(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try: avail=next(int(s.split()[1]) for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
    except (OSError,ValueError,StopIteration,IndexError):avail=None
    return read_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),read_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),avail

def safety(quick):
    if quick:return None
    battery,temp,avail=telemetry()
    if None in (battery,temp,avail) or not 100<=temp<=800:return 'TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if battery<40:return 'LOW_BATTERY'
    if temp>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_AT_LEAST_37_5C'
    if avail<400000:return 'LOW_MEMAVAILABLE'
    return None

def strict_pairs(pairs):
    obj={}
    for k,v in pairs:
        if k in obj:raise ValueError('DUPLICATE_JSON_KEY')
        obj[k]=v
    return obj

def bad_constant(_):raise ValueError('NONFINITE_JSON')

def decode(raw):
    if not isinstance(raw,bytes) or len(raw)>CAP or len(raw)<2:raise ValueError('ENVELOPE_SIZE')
    obj=json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8',errors='strict'),object_pairs_hook=strict_pairs,parse_constant=bad_constant)
    if not isinstance(obj,dict) or set(obj)!={'v','kind','seq','data'}:raise ValueError('ENVELOPE_FIELDS')
    if type(obj['v']) is not int or obj['v']!=1:raise ValueError('VERSION')
    if obj['kind'] not in KINDS:raise ValueError('KIND_OR_CONTROL_IN_DATA')
    if type(obj['seq']) is not int or not 0<=obj['seq']<2**63:raise ValueError('SEQUENCE')
    data=obj['data']
    if not isinstance(data,dict):raise ValueError('PAYLOAD_NOT_OBJECT')
    kind=obj['kind']
    if kind=='SENSOR':
        if set(data)!={'channel','generation','value','missing','source','sample_ns'}:raise ValueError('SENSOR_FIELDS')
        if data['channel'] not in ('battery','temp','cpu6','memavailable','status','voltage'):raise ValueError('CHANNEL')
        if data['source']!='SYSFS_PROC_UNTRUSTED':raise ValueError('SOURCE')
        if type(data['generation']) is not int or not 0<=data['generation']<=1000000:raise ValueError('GENERATION')
        if type(data['sample_ns']) is not int or data['sample_ns']<0:raise ValueError('SAMPLE_TIME')
        if type(data['missing']) is not bool:raise ValueError('MISSING_FLAG')
        v=data['value']
        if data['missing'] and v is not None:raise ValueError('FABRICATED_PRESENT_VALUE')
        if not data['missing'] and (type(v) not in (int,float) or not math.isfinite(v)):raise ValueError('INVALID_READING')
    elif kind=='PCM':
        if set(data)!={'turn','frame','samples'} or not isinstance(data['turn'],str) or not 1<=len(data['turn'])<=24:raise ValueError('PCM_FIELDS')
        if type(data['frame']) is not int or not 0<=data['frame']<=10000 or data['samples']!=160:raise ValueError('PCM_FRAME')
    elif kind=='SCREEN':
        if set(data)!={'frame','sample_ns'} or type(data['frame']) is not int or type(data['sample_ns']) is not int:return_bad('SCREEN_FIELDS')
    elif kind=='TASK':
        if set(data)!={'ticket'} or not isinstance(data['ticket'],str) or not 1<=len(data['ticket'])<=64:raise ValueError('TASK_FIELDS')
    elif kind=='BARRIER':
        if set(data)!={'nonce'} or not isinstance(data['nonce'],str) or len(data['nonce'])>64:raise ValueError('BARRIER_FIELDS')
    return obj

def return_bad(s):raise ValueError(s)

def packet(kind,seq,data):return json.dumps({'v':1,'kind':kind,'seq':seq,'data':data},separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False).encode()

def sqlite_open(path):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),timeout=5,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL');c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000')
    return c

def start_state(path):
    c=sqlite_open(path)
    try:
        c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE state(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),stopped INTEGER NOT NULL,epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        c.execute('INSERT INTO state VALUES(1,0,1)')
    finally:c.close()

def check_stopped(path):
    c=sqlite_open(path)
    try:return c.execute('SELECT stopped,epoch FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone(),c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
    finally:c.close()

def controller(dataq,ctrl,barrierq,dbpath):
    st={'processed':0,'invalid':0,'after_stop_discarded':0,'sensor_slots':0,'sensor_coalesced':0,
        'pcm_incomplete':0,'screen_stale':0,'tasks_admitted':0,'tasks_terminal':0,
        'stop':0,'os_dispatch':0,'p_steps':0}
    sensors={};pcm={};tasks={};stopped=False
    c=sqlite_open(dbpath)
    try:
        ctrl.send(('READY',os.getpid()))
        while True:
            now=time.monotonic_ns()
            if ctrl.poll():
                cmd=ctrl.recv()
                if cmd=='QUIT':return
                if cmd=='STATS':
                    st['sensor_slots']=len(sensors)
                    ctrl.send(('STATS',dict(st),{k:v['seq'] for k,v in sensors.items()}))
                if cmd=='STOP':
                    receive_ns=time.monotonic_ns()
                    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                    c.execute('UPDATE state SET stopped=1,epoch=epoch+1 WHERE id=1')
                    c.execute('COMMIT')
                    stopped=True;st['stop']=1
                    for k,v in tasks.items():
                        if isinstance(v,int):tasks[k]='CANCELLED';st['tasks_terminal']+=1
                    ctrl.send(('STOP_ACK',receive_ns,time.monotonic_ns(),st['tasks_admitted'],st['tasks_terminal']))
            for ticket,v in list(tasks.items()):
                if isinstance(v,int) and now>=v:
                    tasks[ticket]='COMPLETED';st['tasks_terminal']+=1
            try:raw=dataq.get(timeout=.003)
            except queue.Empty:continue
            st['processed']+=1
            if stopped:
                st['after_stop_discarded']+=1
                continue
            try:o=decode(raw)
            except (ValueError,TypeError,UnicodeError,json.JSONDecodeError):
                st['invalid']+=1;continue
            k=o['kind'];d=o['data']
            if k=='SENSOR':
                key=(d['channel'],d['generation'])
                if key not in sensors and len(sensors)>=SENSOR_SLOTS:
                    st['invalid']+=1;continue
                prev=sensors.get(key)
                if prev is not None:
                    if o['seq']<=prev['seq']:st['invalid']+=1;continue
                    st['sensor_coalesced']+=1
                sensors[key]={'seq':o['seq'],'missing':d['missing'],'value':d['value']}
                st['sensor_slots']=len(sensors)
            elif k=='PCM':
                tid=d['turn'];obj=pcm.setdefault(tid,{'last':-1,'count':0,'incomplete':False})
                if d['frame']!=obj['last']+1:obj['incomplete']=True
                obj['last']=d['frame'];obj['count']+=1
                if obj['count']>PCM_LIMIT:obj['incomplete']=True
                st['pcm_incomplete']=sum(int(p['incomplete']) for p in pcm.values())
            elif k=='SCREEN':
                if now-d['sample_ns']>50_000_000:st['screen_stale']+=1
            elif k=='TASK':
                ticket=d['ticket']
                if ticket in tasks:st['invalid']+=1
                elif len(tasks)>=256:st['invalid']+=1
                else:
                    tasks[ticket]=now+90_000_000;st['tasks_admitted']+=1
            elif k=='BARRIER':
                barrierq.put((d['nonce'],dict(st)),timeout=2)
            time.sleep(.0004)
    finally:
        if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
        c.close();ctrl.close()

def worker(binary,stop,out):
    rates=[];errs=[]
    while not stop.is_set():
        try:
            run=subprocess.run([binary,'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
            if run.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',run.stdout):errs.append('S5_CONTRACT_FAILURE');break
            mat=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',run.stdout)
            if not mat:errs.append('MISSING_GMAC');break
            rates.append(float(mat.group(1)))
        except BaseException as ex:errs.append(type(ex).__name__);break
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(rates),min(rates) if rates else None,statistics.mean(rates) if rates else None,errs),timeout=2)
    except BaseException:pass

def memory_holder(mib,stop,ready):
    try:
        buf=bytearray(mib*MiB)
        for i in range(0,len(buf),4096):buf[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=3)
        while not stop.wait(.2):
            for i in range(0,len(buf),262144):buf[i]^=1
    except BaseException as exc:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(exc)[:80]),timeout=1)
        except BaseException:pass

def native_binary(override):
    b=Path(override) if override else Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not b.is_file() or not os.access(b,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('MISSING_S5_BINARY')
    r=subprocess.run([str(b),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=5)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):raise RuntimeError('S5_SELFTEST_FAIL')
    return str(b)

def probe_sensors():
    paths={'battery':'/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity',
       'temp':'/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp',
       'cpu6':'/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu6/cpufreq/scaling_cur_freq',
       'voltage':'/sys/class/power_supply/battery/voltage_now'}
    readings={k:read_int(p) for k,p in paths.items()}
    _,_,avail=telemetry();readings['memavailable']=avail
    # Encode an actually readable categorical charge-status as a lab-only enum.
    try: status_text=Path('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/status').read_text().strip()
    except OSError: status_text=''
    readings['status']={'Charging':1,'Full':2,'Not charging':0,'Discharging':-1}.get(status_text)
    return readings

def begin_broker(ctx,root):
    root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False,mode=0o700)
    db=root/'control.sqlite3';start_state(db)
    q=ctx.Queue(maxsize=QUEUE_LIMIT)
    par,child=ctx.Pipe(duplex=True);bar=ctx.Queue()
    proc=ctx.Process(target=controller,args=(q,child,bar,db));proc.start();child.close()
    if not par.poll(8):raise TimeoutError('BROKER_READY_TIMEOUT')
    if par.recv()[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('BROKER_READY_FAIL')
    return proc,q,par,bar,db

def close_broker(proc,q,ctrl,bar):
    if proc.is_alive():
        try:ctrl.send('QUIT')
        except BaseException:pass
    proc.join(2)
    if proc.is_alive():proc.kill();proc.join(2)
    ctrl.close();q.cancel_join_thread();q.close();bar.cancel_join_thread();bar.close()

def enqueue(q,raw,block=True):
    if block:q.put(raw,timeout=3);return True
    try:q.put_nowait(raw);return True
    except queue.Full:return False

def barrier(q,bar,n):
    enqueue(q,packet('BARRIER',n,{'nonce':str(n)}))
    target=str(n)
    until=time.monotonic()+6
    while time.monotonic()<until:
        try:uid,stats=bar.get(timeout=max(.05,until-time.monotonic()))
        except queue.Empty:break
        if uid==target:return stats
    raise TimeoutError('BARRIER_TIMEOUT')

def stop_and_stats(ctrl,db):
    sent_ns=time.monotonic_ns();ctrl.send('STOP')
    if not ctrl.poll(5):raise TimeoutError('STOP_ACK_TIMEOUT')
    ret=ctrl.recv();done_ns=time.monotonic_ns()
    if ret[0]!='STOP_ACK':raise RuntimeError('EXPECTED_STOP_ACK')
    ctrl.send('STATS')
    if not ctrl.poll(5):raise TimeoutError('STATS_TIMEOUT')
    st=ctrl.recv()
    if st[0]!='STATS':raise RuntimeError('STATS_INVALID')
    state,integrity=check_stopped(db)
    return {'ack_ms':(done_ns-sent_ns)/1e6,'receive_ms':(ret[1]-sent_ns)/1e6,
            'latch_ms':(ret[2]-sent_ns)/1e6,'state':state,'integrity':integrity,
            'stats':st[1],'sensor_seq':st[2]}

def exercise(ctx,root,mode,seqstart):
    proc,q,ctrl,bar,db=begin_broker(ctx,root)
    try:
        begin=time.monotonic_ns();seq=seqstart;dropped=0;readings=probe_sensors()
        def mk(kind,fields):
            nonlocal seq
            seq+=1;return packet(kind,seq,fields)
        if mode=='REAL_SENSOR_PCM_OVERFLOW':
            for ch,val in readings.items():
                enqueue(q,mk('SENSOR',{'channel':ch,'generation':1,'value':val,
                  'missing':val is None,'source':'SYSFS_PROC_UNTRUSTED','sample_ns':time.monotonic_ns()}))
            for i in range(12):
                cpu_val=read_int('/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu6/cpufreq/scaling_cur_freq')
                enqueue(q,mk('SENSOR',{'channel':'cpu6','generation':1,'value':cpu_val,
                    'missing':cpu_val is None,'source':'SYSFS_PROC_UNTRUSTED','sample_ns':time.monotonic_ns()}))
            for i in range(20):enqueue(q,mk('PCM',{'turn':'synthetic-turn','frame':i,'samples':160}))
            enqueue(q,mk('SCREEN',{'frame':1,'sample_ns':time.monotonic_ns()-200_000_000}))
            enqueue(q,mk('SCREEN',{'frame':2,'sample_ns':time.monotonic_ns()}))
            for i in range(10):enqueue(q,mk('TASK',{'ticket':f'T{i}'}))
            mid=barrier(q,bar,seq+1)
            stopped=stop_and_stats(ctrl,db)
            valid=(mid['sensor_slots']>=5 and mid['sensor_coalesced']>=10 and mid['pcm_incomplete']>=1
                   and mid['screen_stale']>=1 and mid['tasks_admitted']==10 and
                   stopped['stats']['tasks_admitted']==stopped['stats']['tasks_terminal'])
        elif mode=='INVALID_ENVELOPES':
            examples=[b'{}',b'{"v":1,"v":1,"kind":"TASK","seq":2,"data":{"ticket":"x"}}',
             b'{"v":1,"kind":"CONTROL","seq":3,"data":{"STOP":true}}',b'\xff',
             b'{"v":1,"kind":"SENSOR","seq":1,"data":{"channel":"battery","generation":1,"value":NaN,"missing":false,"source":"SYSFS_PROC_UNTRUSTED","sample_ns":5}}',
             b'X'*(CAP+1),mk('SENSOR',{'channel':'battery','generation':1,'value':0,'missing':True,
               'source':'SYSFS_PROC_UNTRUSTED','sample_ns':1}),
             mk('TASK',{'ticket':''}),mk('PCM',{'turn':'a','frame':-1,'samples':160}),
             mk('SCREEN',{'frame':1,'sample_ns':'invalid'})]
            for raw in examples:enqueue(q,raw)
            mid=barrier(q,bar,seq+1)
            stopped=stop_and_stats(ctrl,db)
            valid=(mid['invalid']==len(examples) and mid['tasks_admitted']==0 and stopped['stats']['os_dispatch']==0)
        elif mode=='CONTROL_PRIORITY_SATURATED':
            for i in range(8):enqueue(q,mk('TASK',{'ticket':f'PRIOR{i}'}))
            barrier(q,bar,seq+1)
            for i in range(1200):
                if not enqueue(q,mk('SENSOR',{'channel':'cpu6','generation':1,'value':652800,
                      'missing':False,'source':'SYSFS_PROC_UNTRUSTED','sample_ns':time.monotonic_ns()}),False):dropped+=1
            stopped=stop_and_stats(ctrl,db)
            before=stopped['stats']['tasks_admitted']
            for i in range(6):enqueue(q,mk('TASK',{'ticket':f'AFTER{i}'}))
            time.sleep(.10)
            ctrl.send('STATS')
            if not ctrl.poll(4):raise TimeoutError('POST_STOP_STATS')
            post=ctrl.recv()
            if post[0]!='STATS':raise RuntimeError('POST_STOP_BAD_REPLY')
            valid=(dropped>0 and stopped['stats']['tasks_admitted']==stopped['stats']['tasks_terminal']
                    and before==post[1]['tasks_admitted'] and stopped['stats']['os_dispatch']==0)
            mid=post[1]
        else:raise ValueError('UNKNOWN_MODE')
        dur=(time.monotonic_ns()-begin)/1e6
        valid=bool(valid and stopped['state'][0]==1 and stopped['integrity']=='ok' and
                  stopped['stats']['os_dispatch']==0 and stopped['stats']['p_steps']==0)
        return {'mode':mode,'status':'PASS' if valid else 'FAIL','elapsed_ms':dur,
           'real_readable':sum(v is not None for v in readings.values()),
           'ack_ms':stopped['ack_ms'],'latch_ms':stopped['latch_ms'],
           'dropped':dropped,'mid':mid,'final':stopped['stats'],'db':db,'proc':proc}
    finally:close_broker(proc,q,ctrl,bar)

def run_stage(ctx,root,log,quick,name,n,ram,duration,repeat,binary):
    event=ctx.Event();results=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None;errors=0;cases=[]
    begin=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=memory_holder,args=(ram,event,ready));holder.start()
        status=ready.get(timeout=7)
        if status[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('MEMORY_HOLDER_FAIL '+str(status))
        for _ in range(n):
            w=ctx.Process(target=worker,args=(binary,event,results));w.start();workers.append(w)
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} native_S5_workers={n} touched_RAM_MiB={ram} duration_floor_s={duration}')
        for rep in range(1,repeat+1):
            for mode in ('REAL_SENSOR_PCM_OVERFLOW','INVALID_ENVELOPES','CONTROL_PRIORITY_SATURATED'):
                issue=safety(quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                try:
                    out=exercise(ctx,root/f'{name}_{rep}_{mode}',mode,rep*100000)
                    # After a confirmed STOP, kill the controller and independently read its durable latch.
                    state,integrity=check_stopped(out['db'])
                    if state[0]!=1 or integrity!='ok':out['status']='FAIL'
                    cases.append(out)
                    if out['status']!='PASS':errors+=1
                    x=out['final'];y=out['mid']
                    log(f"CASE stage={name} rep={rep} mode={mode} status={out['status']} "
                        f"ack_ms={out['ack_ms']:.3f} latch_ms={out['latch_ms']:.3f} "
                        f"dropped_data={out['dropped']} real_sensor_fields={out['real_readable']}/6 invalid={x['invalid']} "
                        f"sensor_coalesced={x['sensor_coalesced']} pcm_incomplete={x['pcm_incomplete']} "
                        f"screen_stale={x['screen_stale']} tasks={x['tasks_admitted']}/{x['tasks_terminal']} "
                        f"after_stop_discarded={x['after_stop_discarded']} elapsed_ms={out['elapsed_ms']:.3f} "
                        f"crash_readback_stop={state[0]} sqlite_integrity={integrity}")
                except BaseException as ex:
                    errors+=1
                    log(f'CASE stage={name} rep={rep} mode={mode} status=ERROR exception={type(ex).__name__}:{str(ex)[:130]}')
        while time.monotonic()<begin+duration:
            issue=safety(quick)
            if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
            time.sleep(.15)
    finally:
        event.set()
        for w in workers:
            w.join(4)
            if w.is_alive():w.kill();w.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(2)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,minimum,mean,problems=results.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} min_GMAC_s={minimum} mean_GMAC_s={mean} errors={problems}')
                if runs==0 or problems:errors+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                errors+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} error=MISSING_RESULT')
        results.cancel_join_thread();results.close();ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
    ack=[x['ack_ms'] for x in cases]
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={name} cases={len(cases)} expected={3*repeat} failures={errors} '
        f'ack_max_ms={max(ack) if ack else None} real_sensor_probes={sum(x["mode"]=="REAL_SENSOR_PCM_OVERFLOW" for x in cases)}')
    return errors==0 and len(cases)==3*repeat,len(cases)

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='local smoke test only; NOT phone evidence')
    p.add_argument('--binary',help='S5 path override for local smoke test')
    args=p.parse_args()
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_NATIVE_EVENT_BACKPRESSURE_STRESS_V7_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- TYPED IPC / REAL SENSOR / PCM BACKPRESSURE UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('Separate priority STOP pipe versus bounded 24-item data queue. Synthetic PCM/SCREEN/TASK; SENSOR reads real A52 sysfs/proc.')
            log('STOP ack, post-STOP admission, missing-mask, canonical envelope parser, stale screen and incomplete PCM are checked under load.')
            log('All stages run native ARM C256R32 FP32 S5, with 2/4 worker processes plus 32/64 MiB RAM.')
            log('LIMIT: synthetic IPC queue, NOT Android Binder, real microphone/display, trained model, real owner consent or true OS STOP.')
            log('LIMIT: queue already receives Python bytes before envelope-size check; NOT a proof of allocation-before-parse protection.')
            log('No OS dispatch, root, USB, ADB, APK, network, permissions or production changes. OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            issue=safety(args.quick)
            if issue:log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue);return 3
            binary=native_binary(args.binary);log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_S5 '+binary)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork');root=home/'uno_a52_r41_native_event_backpressure_stress_v7'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,7,1),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,8,1)] if args.quick else [
                ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,24,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,30,2)]
            done=0;all_good=True
            for name,n,ram,duration,repeats in plans:
                issue=safety(args.quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                okay,count=run_stage(ctx,root,log,args.quick,name,n,ram,duration,repeats,binary)
                all_good=all_good and okay;done+=count
                b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY name={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
                if not okay:break
            log(f'RESULT {"PASS_BOUNDED_IPC_BACKPRESSURE_LAB" if all_good else "FAIL_OR_INCOMPLETE"} cases={done}')
            log('OPEN: real Binder/Android callbacks, OS capability separation, PCM VAD/CTC, device UX, hard real-time STOP, OS action readback.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report))
            return 0 if all_good else 2
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPT');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as ex:
            log('RESULT ERROR '+type(ex).__name__+': '+str(ex)[:190]);log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 S5-loaded sensor freshness/backpressure precode experiment V8.

Not production code, no Android actions, not an OS security boundary.
This is a directed comparison of a V7-style sequence-only store with a
new keyed, bounded, locally shared sensor register that survives DATA FIFO overflow.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE=FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import math
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
import queue
import re
import sqlite3
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback
from pathlib import Path

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_SENSOR_FRESHNESS_BACKPRESSURE_V8'
CHANNELS=('battery','temp','cpu6','memavailable','voltage','status')
PATHS={'battery':'/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity',
       'temp':'/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp',
       'cpu6':'/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu6/cpufreq/scaling_cur_freq',
       'voltage':'/sys/class/power_supply/battery/voltage_now'}
N=len(CHANNELS)
W=6; STOP_POS=N*W
MiB=1048576
CAP=24

def read_int(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:
        avail=next(int(x.split()[1]) for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
    except (OSError,ValueError,IndexError,StopIteration):avail=None
    return read_int(PATHS['battery']),read_int(PATHS['temp']),avail

def read_real():
    values={k:read_int(v) for k,v in PATHS.items()}
    values['memavailable']=telemetry()[2]
    try:status=Path('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/status').read_text().strip()
    except OSError:status=''
    values['status']={'Charging':1,'Full':2,'Not charging':0,'Discharging':-1}.get(status)
    return values

def safety(quick):
    if quick:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'REQUIRED_TELEMETRY_MISSING'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_AT_LEAST_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'AVAILABLE_RAM_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def initialize_slots(ctx):
    a=ctx.Array('q',N*W+1,lock=True)
    with a.get_lock():
        for i in range(N):
            a[W*i]=-1; a[W*i+1]=-1; a[W*i+2]=-1
    return a

def dump_slots(a):
    with a.get_lock():
        d={}
        for j,ch in enumerate(CHANNELS):
            b=W*j;d[ch]={'gen':a[b],'ns':a[b+1],'seq':a[b+2],
                         'value':a[b+3] if a[b+4] else None,
                         'present':bool(a[b+4]),'revision':a[b+5]}
        return d,bool(a[STOP_POS])

def publish(a,channel,gen,stamp,seq,value,missing=False):
    """Validation before shared write; status is explicit; no guessed thresholds."""
    if channel not in CHANNELS or type(gen) is not int or not 0<=gen<2**31 or \
       type(stamp) is not int or stamp<0 or type(seq) is not int or not 0<=seq<2**63 or \
       type(missing) is not bool or \
       (missing and value is not None) or \
       (not missing and (type(value) is not int or not -(2**62)<value<2**62)):
        return 'INVALID'
    # Test-only finite monotonic-clock horizon, NOT an approved SENSOR TTL.
    if stamp>time.monotonic_ns()+10_000_000:return 'FUTURE_REJECTED'
    idx=CHANNELS.index(channel)*W
    with a.get_lock():
        if a[STOP_POS]:return 'STOPPED'
        old=(a[idx],a[idx+1],a[idx+2])
        key=(gen,stamp,seq)
        if key<=old:return 'OLD_REJECTED'
        a[idx]=gen;a[idx+1]=stamp;a[idx+2]=seq
        a[idx+3]=0 if missing else value
        a[idx+4]=0 if missing else 1
        a[idx+5]+=1
        return 'UPDATED'

def open_db(path):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),timeout=4,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=4000')
    return c

def create_db(path):
    c=open_db(path)
    try:
        c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        c.execute('CREATE TABLE state (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), stopped INTEGER NOT NULL)')
        c.execute('INSERT INTO state VALUES(1,0)')
    finally:c.close()

def read_db(path):
    c=open_db(path)
    try:return c.execute('SELECT stopped FROM state WHERE id=1').fetchone()[0],c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
    finally:c.close()

def broker(a,dataq,ctrl,db):
    c=open_db(db);count=0;post=0
    try:
        ctrl.send(('READY',os.getpid()))
        while True:
            if ctrl.poll():
                cmd=ctrl.recv()
                if cmd=='QUIT':return
                if cmd=='STATS':ctrl.send(('STATS',count,post));continue
                if cmd=='STOP':
                    receive=time.monotonic_ns()
                    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                    c.execute('UPDATE state SET stopped=1 WHERE id=1')
                    c.execute('COMMIT')
                    # ACK follows durable SQLite commit. This is not atomic with
                    # the sensor register; request-to-ACK remains a known gap.
                    with a.get_lock():a[STOP_POS]=1
                    ctrl.send(('ACK',receive,time.monotonic_ns(),count,post))
                    continue
            try:_=dataq.get(timeout=.003)
            except queue.Empty:continue
            with a.get_lock(): stopped=bool(a[STOP_POS])
            if stopped:post+=1
            else:count+=1
            time.sleep(.0015)  # deliberately slow synthetic data consumer
    finally:
        if c.in_transaction:c.execute('ROLLBACK')
        c.close();ctrl.close()

def start_broker(ctx,root):
    root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
    a=initialize_slots(ctx)
    db=root/'stop.sqlite3';create_db(db)
    dataq=ctx.Queue(maxsize=CAP)
    parent,child=ctx.Pipe(duplex=True)
    p=ctx.Process(target=broker,args=(a,dataq,child,db));p.start();child.close()
    if not parent.poll(8):raise TimeoutError('CONTROLLER_NOT_READY')
    if parent.recv()[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('CONTROLLER_READY_INVALID')
    return p,a,db,dataq,parent

def end_broker(p,q,conn):
    if p.is_alive():
        try:conn.send('QUIT')
        except (OSError,EOFError):pass
    p.join(2)
    if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(2)
    conn.close();q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()

def stop_ack(conn):
    before=time.monotonic_ns();conn.send('STOP')
    if not conn.poll(5):raise TimeoutError('STOP_ACK_TIMEOUT')
    ret=conn.recv();after=time.monotonic_ns()
    if ret[0]!='ACK':raise RuntimeError('WRONG_STOP_ACK')
    return {'ack_ms':(after-before)/1e6,'receive_ms':(ret[1]-before)/1e6,
            'latch_ms':(ret[2]-before)/1e6}

def flood(q,count):
    rejected=0;accepted=0
    for i in range(count):
        try:q.put_nowait(b'PCM_SYNTHETIC_FRAME_OR_SCREEN'+str(i).encode());accepted+=1
        except queue.Full:rejected+=1
    return accepted,rejected

def real_seed(a):
    values=read_real();ts=time.monotonic_ns()
    for i,ch in enumerate(CHANNELS):
        value=values[ch]
        st=publish(a,ch,1,ts+i,1,value,value is None)
        if st!='UPDATED':raise AssertionError('FAILED_REAL_SENSOR_SEED')
    return values

def one_case(ctx,root,mode):
    p,a,db,q,ctrl=start_broker(ctx,root)
    try:
        real=real_seed(a)
        initial,_=dump_slots(a)
        ts=time.monotonic_ns()-2_000_000
        reasons=[];expected=None;negative=0;drops=0;offered=0
        ch='cpu6'
        if mode=='REORDERED_NEWEST_WINS':
            # Same source-generation, higher sequence but older SAMPLE timestamp:
            # V7-style seq-only replacement erroneously accepts the stale payload.
            assert publish(a,ch,2,ts,100,202600,False)=='UPDATED'
            oldstamp=ts-500_000_000
            verdict=publish(a,ch,2,oldstamp,101,111111,False)
            reasons.append(verdict)
            assert verdict=='OLD_REJECTED'
            v7_like={'seq':100,'time':ts,'value':202600}
            if 101>v7_like['seq']:
                v7_like={'seq':101,'time':oldstamp,'value':111111}
            negative=int(v7_like['time']<ts and v7_like['value']==111111)
            expected=(2,ts,100,202600,True)
        elif mode=='GENERATION_REPLAY_DENIED':
            assert publish(a,ch,2,ts,22,222222,False)=='UPDATED'
            reasons.append(publish(a,ch,1,ts+1,999999,777777,False))
            reasons.append(publish(a,ch,2,ts-1,999999,666666,False))
            assert reasons==['OLD_REJECTED','OLD_REJECTED']
            expected=(2,ts,22,222222,True)
        elif mode=='MISSING_NEWER_IS_NOT_ZERO':
            assert publish(a,ch,2,ts,10,0,False)=='UPDATED'
            assert publish(a,ch,2,ts+1,11,None,True)=='UPDATED'
            reasons.append(publish(a,ch,2,ts-1,12,938000,False))
            assert reasons==['OLD_REJECTED']
            expected=(2,ts+1,11,None,False)
        elif mode=='INVALID_AND_FUTURE_REJECT':
            assert publish(a,ch,2,ts,30,0,False)=='UPDATED'
            negatives=[(2,ts+1,31,float('nan'),False),
                       (2,ts+2,32,None,False),
                       (2,ts+3,33,0,True),
                       (2,ts+4,34,True,False),
                       (2,ts+5,-1,5,False),
                       (2,time.monotonic_ns()+2_000_000_000,36,5,False)]
            reasons=[publish(a,ch,gen,t,s,val,miss) for gen,t,s,val,miss in negatives]
            assert reasons==['INVALID']*5+['FUTURE_REJECTED'],reasons
            expected=(2,ts,30,0,True)
        elif mode=='QUEUE_OVERFLOW_SENSOR_LAST_WINS':
            # Saturate a bounded DATA FIFO. Sensors bypass it via six keyed slots.
            accepted,drops=flood(q,1800)
            offered=accepted+drops
            start=time.monotonic_ns()-3_000_000
            assert publish(a,ch,2,start,40,400000,False)=='UPDATED'
            for i in range(400):
                stamp=start+i*1000
                assert publish(a,ch,2,stamp,41+i,500000+i,False)=='UPDATED'
            expected=(2,start+399000,440,500399,True)
            assert publish(a,ch,2,start+398000,99999,123,False)=='OLD_REJECTED'
            negative=0  # overflow is a distinct finding, not a seq-only comparator finding
        else:raise ValueError('UNKNOWN_CASE')
        slots_before,stop_before=dump_slots(a)
        current=slots_before[ch]
        got=(current['gen'],current['ns'],current['seq'],current['value'],current['present'])
        fresh=(got==expected and not stop_before)
        # Other real sensor channels retain their original values, including
        # explicit missing masks. None are fabricated by this stress fixture.
        others_ok=all((slots_before[k]['value']==real[k] if real[k] is not None
                       else not slots_before[k]['present']) for k in CHANNELS if k!=ch)
        timing=stop_ack(ctrl)
        before_post,_=dump_slots(a)
        post_status=publish(a,ch,3,time.monotonic_ns(),10000000,1,False)
        after_post,stop_flag=dump_slots(a)
        dbstop,integrity=read_db(db)
        valid=bool(fresh and others_ok and post_status=='STOPPED' and
                   before_post==after_post and stop_flag and dbstop==1 and integrity=='ok' and
                   (mode!='REORDERED_NEWEST_WINS' or negative==1) and
                   (mode!='QUEUE_OVERFLOW_SENSOR_LAST_WINS' or (drops>0 and offered==1800)))
        return {'mode':mode,'pass':valid,'ack_ms':timing['ack_ms'],
                'latch_ms':timing['latch_ms'],'readable':sum(v is not None for v in real.values()),
                'fresh':fresh,'others':others_ok,'post':post_status,
                'dropped':drops,'offered':offered,'negative':negative,
                'mask':int(current['present']),'gen':current['gen'],
                'seq':current['seq'],'value':current['value'],
                'reasons':reasons,'integrity':integrity}
    finally:end_broker(p,q,ctrl)

def native_bin(override):
    path=Path(override) if override else Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not path.is_file() or not os.access(path,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_BINARY_MISSING')
    x=subprocess.run([str(path),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=6)
    if x.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',x.stdout):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_SELFTEST_FAILED')
    return str(path)

def native_worker(binary,ev,out,active):
    rates=[];errors=[]
    while not ev.is_set():
        try:
            p=subprocess.run([binary,'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=6)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):
                errors.append('BAD_NATIVE_S5_CONTRACT');break
            match=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not match:errors.append('NO_GMAC_METRIC');break
            rates.append(float(match.group(1)))
            if len(rates)==1:
                try:active.put(('ACTIVE',os.getpid()),timeout=2)
                except BaseException:errors.append('ACTIVE_SIGNAL_FAILED');break
        except BaseException as exc:errors.append(type(exc).__name__);break
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(rates),min(rates) if rates else None,
                 statistics.mean(rates) if rates else None,errors),timeout=2)
    except BaseException:pass

def memory_hold(meg,ev,ready):
    try:
        b=bytearray(meg*MiB)
        for i in range(0,len(b),4096):b[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=2)
        while not ev.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(b),262144):b[i]^=1
    except BaseException as e:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(e)[:90]),timeout=1)
        except BaseException:pass

def run_stage(ctx,root,log,quick,stage,num,ram,minimum,repeats,binary):
    cancel=ctx.Event();out=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();active=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None
    cases=[];fail=0;since=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=memory_hold,args=(ram,cancel,ready));holder.start()
        msg=ready.get(timeout=7)
        if msg[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('MEMORY_RESERVATION_FAILED '+str(msg))
        for i in range(num):
            p=ctx.Process(target=native_worker,args=(binary,cancel,out,active));p.start();workers.append(p)
        # Do not begin a directed case before ALL native S5 workers have
        # actually completed their first FP32 run on the device.
        until=time.monotonic()+25
        confirmed=set()
        while len(confirmed)<num:
            left=until-time.monotonic()
            if left<=0:raise TimeoutError('NATIVE_S5_LOAD_NOT_CONFIRMED')
            try:msg=active.get(timeout=left)
            except queue.Empty:raise TimeoutError('NATIVE_S5_LOAD_NOT_CONFIRMED')
            if msg[0]!='ACTIVE':raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_LOAD_INVALID_READY')
            confirmed.add(msg[1])
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={stage} workers={len(confirmed)}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={stage} native_s5_workers={num} held_ram_MiB={ram} min_duration_s={minimum}')
        modes=('REORDERED_NEWEST_WINS','GENERATION_REPLAY_DENIED','MISSING_NEWER_IS_NOT_ZERO',
               'INVALID_AND_FUTURE_REJECT','QUEUE_OVERFLOW_SENSOR_LAST_WINS')
        for rep in range(1,repeats+1):
            for mode in modes:
                err=safety(quick)
                if err:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+err)
                try:
                    result=one_case(ctx,root/f'{stage}_{rep}_{mode}',mode);cases.append(result)
                    if not result['pass']:fail+=1
                    log(f"CASE stage={stage} rep={rep} mode={mode} status={'PASS' if result['pass'] else 'FAIL'} "
                        f"freshness_verified={result['fresh']} real_sensor_fields={result['readable']}/6 "
                        f"gen={result['gen']} seq={result['seq']} value={result['value']} present_mask={result['mask']} "
                        f"dropped_data={result['dropped']}/{result['offered']} "
                        f"seq_only_negative_control={result['negative']} post_stop={result['post']} "
                        f"stop_ack_ms={result['ack_ms']:.3f} sqlite_integrity={result['integrity']}")
                except BaseException as e:
                    fail+=1;log(f'CASE stage={stage} rep={rep} mode={mode} status=ERROR reason={type(e).__name__}:{str(e)[:140]}')
        while time.monotonic()<since+minimum:
            err=safety(quick)
            if err:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+err)
            time.sleep(.12)
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        for p in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,low,mean,problems=out.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={stage} pid={pid} runs={runs} min_GMAC_s={low} mean_GMAC_s={mean} errors={problems}')
                if problems or not runs:fail+=1
            except queue.Empty:fail+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={stage} status=MISSING_RESULT')
        out.cancel_join_thread();out.close();ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close();active.cancel_join_thread();active.close()
    ack=[r['ack_ms'] for r in cases]
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY name={stage} cases={len(cases)} expected={5*repeats} '
        f'failures={fail} stop_ack_max_ms={max(ack) if ack else None} '
        f'data_fifo_drops={sum(r["dropped"] for r in cases)} '
        f'v7_seq_only_negative_reproduced={sum(r["negative"] for r in cases if r["mode"]=="REORDERED_NEWEST_WINS")}')
    return fail==0 and len(cases)==5*repeats,len(cases)

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='short smoke check only; NOT device evidence')
    parser.add_argument('--binary',help='native S5 path override for local smoke test')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_SENSOR_FRESHNESS_STRESS_V8_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as file:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);file.write(s+'\n');file.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- NATIVE S5 SENSOR LATEST-SAMPLE BACKPRESSURE STRESS '+VERSION)
            log('On A52: native ARM S5 CPU runs continuously per stage; real sysfs/proc sensors seed bounded six-slot shared register.')
            log('Directed V7-like seq-only negative control versus candidate generation/sample-time/seq latest-wins arbitration.')
            log('Separate 24-entry slow DATA FIFO and STOP IPC; DATA overflow cannot evict keyed sensor slots in this fixture.')
            log('STOP request != durable ACK. No Android Binder, OS dispatch, real microphone/screen, trained neural P, authenticated owner or production controller.')
            log('Fixed monotonic future-skew filter is synthetic; no production sensor TTL, dictionary, or hardware durability approved.')
            log('No root, ADB, USB, network, APK, permissions or system changes. OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            issue=safety(args.quick)
            if issue:log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+issue);return 3
            binary=native_bin(args.binary);log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_S5 '+binary)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_sensor_freshness_stress_v8'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,7,1),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,8,1)] if args.quick else [
                ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,24,2),('S5x4_RAM64',4,64,30,2)]
            total=0;passes=True
            for name,n,ram,minimum,repeats in plans:
                issue=safety(args.quick)
                if issue:raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_'+issue)
                good,num=run_stage(ctx,root,log,args.quick,name,n,ram,minimum,repeats,binary)
                total+=num;passes=passes and good
                b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY name={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
                if not good:break
            log(f'RESULT {"PASS_BOUNDED_LATEST_SAMPLE_FIXTURE" if passes else "FAIL_OR_INCOMPLETE"} cases={total}')
            log('LIMIT: source-generated fixture values synthetic; only seed readings are real. Same-UID shared memory is not a trusted Android boundary.')
            log('LIMIT: no guaranteed STOP-from-physical-press or power-cut durability; V7 seq-only outcome is a code-rule replica, not an independent V7 binary replay.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report))
            return 0 if passes else 2
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPTED');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as e:
            log('RESULT ERROR '+type(e).__name__+': '+str(e)[:180]);log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 PRECODE V9: true pre-compute S5 masks vs post-compute discards.

Runs only under bounded native ARM S5 load on the user's A52; builds a separate
synthetic C256R32 K=8 gating fixture with clang. No production agent code.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE=FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

V='UNO_R41_A52_S5_TRUE_PRECOMPUTE_MASK_GATE_STRESS_V9'
MIB=1048576
C_SOURCE=r'''
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <time.h>
#define D 256
#define B 32
#define U 128
#define R 32
#define NB 8
#define K 8
static float A2[NB][R][D],A1[NB][U][R],C2[NB][R][D],C1[NB][U][R],BB[NB][B][U];
static uint32_t rng=0x19acf57bu;
static volatile double late_sink=0, result_sink=0;
static uint32_t rand32(void){rng^=rng<<13;rng^=rng>>17;rng^=rng<<5;return rng;}
static float small(void){return ((float)(rand32()%20001)/10000.0f-1.0f)*.008f;}
static double seconds(void){struct timespec t;clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC,&t);return t.tv_sec+t.tv_nsec*1e-9;}
static void init(void){
 for(int j=0;j<NB;j++){
  for(int r=0;r<R;r++)for(int i=0;i<D;i++){A2[j][r][i]=small();C2[j][r][i]=small();}
  for(int u=0;u<U;u++)for(int r=0;r<R;r++){A1[j][u][r]=small();C1[j][u][r]=small();}
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)for(int u=0;u<U;u++)BB[j][b][u]=small();
 }
}
static void init_hz(float h[D],float z[D]){
 for(int i=0;i<D;i++){h[i]=.02f*(float)((i%17)-8);z[i]=.015f*(float)((i%11)-5);}
}
// mode 0 FULL: calculate and write all eight branches.
// mode 1 EARLY: skip the six inactive branches BEFORE any matrix products.
// mode 2 LATE: calculate all eight; throw away six already computed branches.
static void step(float h[D],const float z[D],int mode){
 float next[D],at[R],ct[R],u[U],v[U],rhs[B];
 memcpy(next,h,sizeof(float)*D);
 for(int j=0;j<NB;j++){
  if(mode==1 && j>=2)continue; // actual precompute gate
  for(int r=0;r<R;r++){
   float aa=0,cc=0;
   for(int i=0;i<D;i++){aa+=A2[j][r][i]*h[i];cc+=C2[j][r][i]*z[i];}
   at[r]=aa;ct[r]=cc;
  }
  for(int x=0;x<U;x++){
   float aa=0,cc=0;
   for(int r=0;r<R;r++){aa+=A1[j][x][r]*at[r];cc+=C1[j][x][r]*ct[r];}
   u[x]=tanhf(aa+cc);
  }
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++){
   float s=0;for(int x=0;x<U;x++)s+=BB[j][b][x]*u[x];
   rhs[b]=tanhf(s);
  }
  if(mode==2 && j>=2){late_sink+=(double)rhs[(j+3)%B];continue;}
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)next[j*B+b]=.5f*h[j*B+b]+.5f*rhs[b];
 }
 memcpy(h,next,sizeof(float)*D);
}
static void event(float h[D],const float z[D],int mode){for(int k=0;k<K;k++)step(h,z,mode);}
static int selftest(void){
 float early[D],late[D],original[D],z[D];init_hz(original,z);
 memcpy(early,original,sizeof(early));memcpy(late,original,sizeof(late));
 event(early,z,1);event(late,z,2);
 int same=memcmp(early,late,sizeof(early))==0,unchanged=1;
 for(int i=64;i<D;i++)if(early[i]!=original[i])unchanged=0;
 printf("FIXED_K_VERIFY K=%d masked_early_equals_late=%s inactive_unchanged=%s status=%s\n",K,same?"YES":"NO",unchanged?"YES":"NO",(same&&unchanged)?"PASS":"FAIL");
 return same&&unchanged;
}
int main(int argc,char**argv){
 if(argc!=3){fputs("usage: binary full|early|late seconds\n",stderr);return 2;}
 int mode=!strcmp(argv[1],"full")?0:(!strcmp(argv[1],"early")?1:(!strcmp(argv[1],"late")?2:-1));
 double duration=atof(argv[2]);if(mode<0||duration<.30||duration>2.00)return 2;
 init();if(!selftest())return 3;
 // Per branch: two rank products A and C, one B projection; K is FIXED.
 const unsigned long long macperbranch=2ull*(R*D+U*R)+(unsigned long long)B*U;
 const int computed=mode==1?2:8, updated=mode==0?8:2;
 const unsigned long long mac=(unsigned long long)computed*macperbranch*K;
 printf("LEDGER mode=%s K=%d active_blocks=%d computed_blocks=%d event_F_MAC=%llu\n",argv[1],K,updated,computed,mac);
 float h[D],z[D],orig[D];init_hz(orig,z);
 double t0=seconds(),elapsed=0,checksum=0;unsigned long long n=0;
 do{
  memcpy(h,orig,sizeof(h));h[n%D]+=1e-5f;
  event(h,z,mode);checksum+=h[(n*17ull)%D];n++;
  elapsed=seconds()-t0;
 }while(elapsed<duration&&n<3000000ull);
 result_sink=checksum;
 printf("MEASURE mode=%s events=%llu elapsed_s=%.4f avg_ms_per_event=%.6f F_GMAC_s=%.5f checksum=%.8f\n",argv[1],n,elapsed,1000*elapsed/n,(double)mac*n/elapsed/1e9,checksum);
 return 0;
}
'''

def nint(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telem():
    try:
        line=next(x for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:'))
        m=int(line.split()[1])
    except (OSError,ValueError,StopIteration,IndexError):m=None
    return nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),m

def safety(quick):
    if quick:return None
    b,t,m=telem()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_AT_LEAST_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'AVAILABLE_RAM_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

class SafetyAbort(RuntimeError):pass

def compile_fixture(root):
    clang=shutil.which('clang')
    if not clang:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_NOT_INSTALLED')
    src=root/'true_mask_gate.c';exe=root/'true_mask_gate'
    src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    p=subprocess.run([clang,'-O3','-std=c11','-Wall','-Wextra',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=30)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('C_COMPILE_FAIL '+p.stderr[-250:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def verify_background_binary():
    exe=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not exe.is_file() or not os.access(exe,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_MISSING')
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=8)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_SELFCHECK_FAILED')
    return exe

def background_worker(exe,cancel,out,ready):
    rates=[];errors=[]
    while not cancel.is_set():
        try:
            p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=7)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):
                errors.append('CONTRACT_FAIL');break
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not m:errors.append('GMAC_MISSING');break
            rates.append(float(m.group(1)))
            if len(rates)==1:ready.put(('ACTIVE',os.getpid()),timeout=3)
        except BaseException as e:errors.append(type(e).__name__);break
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(rates),min(rates) if rates else None,statistics.mean(rates) if rates else None,errors),timeout=2)
    except BaseException:pass

def ram_holder(mib,cancel,ready):
    try:
        data=bytearray(mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(data),4096):data[i]=1
        ready.put(('READY',os.getpid()),timeout=3)
        while not cancel.wait(.14):
            for i in range(0,len(data),262144):data[i]^=1
    except BaseException as e:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(e)[:90]),timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def sample(exe,mode,seconds):
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),mode,f'{seconds:.2f}'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=seconds+10)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('MASK_BINARY_EXIT '+str(p.returncode)+' '+p.stderr[-150:])
    if not re.search(r'FIXED_K_VERIFY[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('MASK_VERIFY_FAILED')
    ledger=re.search(r'LEDGER mode=(\w+) K=(\d+) active_blocks=(\d+) computed_blocks=(\d+) event_F_MAC=(\d+)',p.stdout)
    metric=re.search(r'MEASURE mode=(\w+) events=(\d+) elapsed_s=([0-9.]+) avg_ms_per_event=([0-9.]+) F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',p.stdout)
    if not ledger or not metric or ledger[1]!=mode or metric[1]!=mode:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_OUTPUT_PARSE_ERROR')
    mac=int(ledger[5]);comp=int(ledger[4]);active=int(ledger[3]);K=int(ledger[2]);
    expected=1835008 if mode!='early' else 458752
    if K!=8 or mac!=expected or comp!=(2 if mode=='early' else 8) or active!=(8 if mode=='full' else 2):
        raise RuntimeError('LEDGER_OR_MASK_MISMATCH')
    return {'mode':mode,'event_mac':mac,'events':int(metric[2]),'elapsed_s':float(metric[3]),'ms':float(metric[4]),'gmac':float(metric[5])}

def summarize(log,name,samples,failures,safety_reason,workers_ok):
    grouped={mode:[v['ms'] for v in samples if v['mode']==mode] for mode in ('early','late','full')}
    med={mode:(statistics.median(vals) if vals else None) for mode,vals in grouped.items()}
    ratio=med['late']/med['early'] if med['late'] and med['early'] else None
    ratio_full=med['full']/med['early'] if med['full'] and med['early'] else None
    incomplete=bool(safety_reason or failures or not workers_ok or any(len(v)<3 for v in grouped.values()))
    status='INCOMPLETE_SAFETY_ABORT' if safety_reason else ('FAIL' if incomplete else 'COMPLETE')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={name} status={status} measurements={len(samples)}/9 failures={failures} '
        f'early_median_ms={med["early"]} late_median_ms={med["late"]} full_median_ms={med["full"]} '
        f'late_over_early_time_ratio={ratio} full_over_early_time_ratio={ratio_full} '
        f'MAC_ratio_late_over_early=4.0 safety_reason={safety_reason or "-"}')
    return status=='COMPLETE',ratio

def run_stage(ctx,root,log,quick,name,count,mem_mib,min_duration,fixture,background):
    cancel=ctx.Event();out=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();hold_ready=ctx.Queue()
    processes=[];holder=None;samples=[];errors=0;reason=None;since=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=ram_holder,args=(mem_mib,cancel,hold_ready));holder.start()
        msg=hold_ready.get(timeout=8)
        if msg[0]!='READY':raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_NOT_READY '+str(msg))
        for _ in range(count):
            p=ctx.Process(target=background_worker,args=(background,cancel,out,ready));p.start();processes.append(p)
        confirmed=set();end=time.monotonic()+25
        while len(confirmed)<count:
            if time.monotonic()>end:raise TimeoutError('BACKGROUND_S5_NOT_ACTIVE')
            try:msg=ready.get(timeout=max(.1,end-time.monotonic()))
            except queue.Empty:raise TimeoutError('BACKGROUND_S5_NOT_ACTIVE')
            if msg[0]=='ACTIVE':confirmed.add(msg[1])
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={name} workers={len(confirmed)}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} native_s5_workers={count} resident_MiB={mem_mib} min_duration_s={min_duration} fixed_K=8')
        for rep in range(1,4):
            modes=('early','late','full') if rep%2 else ('late','full','early')
            for mode in modes:
                problem=safety(quick)
                if problem:raise SafetyAbort(problem)
                v=sample(fixture,mode,.40 if quick else .60)
                samples.append(v)
                log(f'MASK_MEASURE stage={name} rep={rep} mode={mode} K=8 blocks_computed={2 if mode=="early" else 8} '
                    f'event_F_MAC={v["event_mac"]} avg_ms={v["ms"]:.6f} events={v["events"]} '
                    f'F_GMAC_s={v["gmac"]:.5f} fixture_contract=PASS')
        while time.monotonic()-since<min_duration:
            problem=safety(quick)
            if problem:raise SafetyAbort(problem)
            time.sleep(.15)
    except SafetyAbort as e:
        reason=str(e)
        log(f'SAFETY_ABORT stage={name} reason={reason} detail=workers_will_be_stopped')
    except BaseException as e:
        errors+=1
        log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={name} type={type(e).__name__} detail={str(e)[:180]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for p in processes:
            p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        good=0
        for _ in processes:
            try:
                pid,runs,low,mean,problems=out.get(timeout=3)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} min_GMAC_s={low} mean_GMAC_s={mean} errors={problems}')
                if runs and not problems:good+=1
                else:errors+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                errors+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} status=RESULT_MISSING')
        for q in (out,ready,hold_ready):q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
    okay,ratio=summarize(log,name,samples,errors,reason,good==count)
    b,t,m=telem();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY name={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return okay,reason,ratio,len(samples)

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--quick',action='store_true',help='host smoke only; skips Android safety checks; not A52 evidence')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_NATIVE_S5_TRUE_MASK_GATE_V9_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        def log(msg):print(msg,flush=True);fp.write(msg+'\n');fp.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- NATIVE TRUE MASK GATE vs LATE DISCARD STRESS '+V)
            log('Synthetic C256R32 FP32, FIXED K=8: EARLY computes 2 blocks, LATE computes 8 but writes 2, FULL computes+writes 8.')
            log('Early and late are tested for identical state; late uses a volatile sink to prevent discarded work being optimized away.')
            log('2 and 4 existing native S5 load workers continuously active per stage; 32MiB held RAM per stage.')
            log('RAM hold x4 is 32MiB vs V8 64MiB to reduce memory pressure; 400000kB MemAvailable safety floor UNCHANGED.')
            log('NO real neural weights, learning, owner auth, OS dispatch, Android Binder, native STOP or production code.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telem();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            problem=safety(args.quick)
            if problem:log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP reason='+problem);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
            background=verify_background_binary();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_S5 '+str(background))
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_true_mask_gate_v9'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            fixture,source_hash=compile_fixture(root)
            log('COMPILED_NATIVE_FIXTURE '+str(fixture)+' source_sha256='+source_hash)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,7),('S5x4_RAM32',4,32,8)] if args.quick else [
                ('S5x2_RAM32',2,32,19),('S5x4_RAM32',4,32,23)]
            total=0;complete=0;ratios=[]
            for name,n,mem,duration in plans:
                problem=safety(args.quick)
                if problem:
                    log('RESULT SAFETY_ABORT_BEFORE_STAGE stage='+name+' reason='+problem)
                    log(f'PARTIAL_RESULT stages_complete={complete} measurements={total}')
                    log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
                done,reason,ratio,num=run_stage(ctx,root,log,args.quick,name,n,mem,duration,fixture,background)
                total+=num;complete+=int(done);ratios.append(ratio)
                if reason:
                    log(f'RESULT SAFETY_ABORT stages_complete={complete} measurements={total} reason={reason}')
                    log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
                if not done:
                    log(f'RESULT FAIL_OR_INCOMPLETE stages_complete={complete} measurements={total}')
                    log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_NATIVE_MASK_EXPERIMENT stages_complete={complete} measurements={total} ratios={ratios}')
            log('INTERPRETATION: timing is local compute comparison, NOT real neural quality or Android energy; mask selection is synthetic.')
            log('LIMIT: same K=8 comparison isolates masked branch computation; none of this establishes full UNO global stability.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report));return 0
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPTED');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as e:
            log('RESULT ERROR '+type(e).__name__+': '+str(e)[:200]);log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1])
            log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2
if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

<<SOURCE_END V09 UNO_A52_R41_NATIVE_S5_TRUE_MASK_GATE_STRESS_V9.py>>
