#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 PRECODE V10: native cyclic-prefix mask / K / MAC budget under actual S5 background load.

No production neural weights; synthetic deterministic low-rank C256R32 workload.
The lab budget is for F MAC only: not a bound on real elapsed time, overhead or energy.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_PREFIX_BUDGET_STRESS_V10'
MIB=1048576
BRANCH_MAC=28672
CONFIGS=[(1,1,3),(1,8,7),(2,4,1),(4,4,5),(4,8,2),(8,8,0)]
C_SOURCE=r'''
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <time.h>
#define D 256
#define B 32
#define NB 8
#define U 128
#define R 32
#define BM 28672ull
static float A2[NB][R][D], A1[NB][U][R], C2[NB][R][D], C1[NB][U][R], BB[NB][B][U];
static volatile double discard_sink=0, output_sink=0;
static uint32_t prng=0x3acd71b9u;
static uint32_t r32(void){prng^=prng<<13;prng^=prng>>17;prng^=prng<<5;return prng;}
static float sval(void){return ((float)(r32()%20001)/10000.f-1.f)*.008f;}
static double clk(clockid_t id){struct timespec ts;clock_gettime(id,&ts);return ts.tv_sec+ts.tv_nsec*1e-9;}
static void initialize(void){
 for(int j=0;j<NB;j++){
  for(int r=0;r<R;r++)for(int i=0;i<D;i++){A2[j][r][i]=sval();C2[j][r][i]=sval();}
  for(int x=0;x<U;x++)for(int r=0;r<R;r++){A1[j][x][r]=sval();C1[j][x][r]=sval();}
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)for(int x=0;x<U;x++)BB[j][b][x]=sval();
 }
}
static void init_hz(float h[D],float z[D]){
 for(int i=0;i<D;i++){h[i]=.02f*(float)((i%17)-8);z[i]=.015f*(float)((i%11)-5);}
}
static int selected(int j,int q,int turn){return (j-turn+NB)%NB<q;}
static unsigned long long do_step(float h[D],const float z[D],int q,int turn,int late){
 float next[D],a[R],c[R],u[U],v[B];unsigned long long done=0;
 memcpy(next,h,sizeof(next));
 for(int j=0;j<NB;j++){
  int active=selected(j,q,turn);
  if(!active&&!late)continue;
  done++;
  for(int r=0;r<R;r++){
   float sa=0,sc=0;
   for(int i=0;i<D;i++){sa+=A2[j][r][i]*h[i];sc+=C2[j][r][i]*z[i];}
   a[r]=sa;c[r]=sc;
  }
  for(int x=0;x<U;x++){
   float sa=0,sc=0;
   for(int r=0;r<R;r++){sa+=A1[j][x][r]*a[r];sc+=C1[j][x][r]*c[r];}
   u[x]=tanhf(sa+sc);
  }
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++){
   float sv=0;for(int x=0;x<U;x++)sv+=BB[j][b][x]*u[x];
   v[b]=tanhf(sv);
  }
  if(!active){discard_sink+=(double)v[j%B];continue;}
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)next[j*B+b]=.5f*h[j*B+b]+.5f*v[b];
 }
 memcpy(h,next,sizeof(next));return done;
}
static unsigned long long one_event(float h[D],const float z[D],int q,int K,int turn,int late){
 unsigned long long done=0;
 for(int k=0;k<K;k++)done+=do_step(h,z,q,turn,late);
 return done;
}
static int verify(int q,int K,int turn){
 float x[D],y[D],h[D],z[D];init_hz(h,z);
 memcpy(x,h,sizeof(x));memcpy(y,h,sizeof(y));
 unsigned long long a=one_event(x,z,q,K,turn,0),b=one_event(y,z,q,K,turn,1);
 int equal=memcmp(x,y,sizeof(x))==0,unchanged=1;
 for(int j=0;j<NB;j++)if(!selected(j,q,turn))
  for(int i=j*B;i<(j+1)*B;i++)if(x[i]!=h[i])unchanged=0;
 printf("CONTRACT equal_gate_late=%s inactive_unchanged=%s gate_blocks=%llu late_blocks=%llu status=%s\n",
  equal?"YES":"NO",unchanged?"YES":"NO",a,b,(equal&&unchanged&&a==(unsigned long long)q*K&&b==(unsigned long long)NB*K)?"PASS":"FAIL");
 return equal&&unchanged&&a==(unsigned long long)q*K&&b==(unsigned long long)NB*K;
}
int main(int argc,char**argv){
 if(argc!=7){fprintf(stderr,"usage: fixture gate|late q K turn mac_budget duration_s\n");return 2;}
 int late=!strcmp(argv[1],"late"),gate=!strcmp(argv[1],"gate");
 int q=atoi(argv[2]),K=atoi(argv[3]),turn=atoi(argv[4]);
 unsigned long long budget=strtoull(argv[5],NULL,10);
 double seconds=atof(argv[6]);
 if((!late&&!gate)||q<1||q>8||K<1||K>8||turn<0||turn>=8||seconds<.20||seconds>1.50)return 2;
 const unsigned long long required=(unsigned long long)(late?8:q)*K*BM;
 printf("REQUEST mode=%s q=%d K=%d turn=%d budget_mac=%llu required_mac=%llu\n",argv[1],q,K,turn,budget,required);
 if(required>budget){
  // Budget check precedes initialization and any F invocation.
  printf("BUDGET_ABORT=no_F_invocation admission=DENIED executed_F_MAC=0 state_intact=YES beta_updated=NO status=PASS\n");return 0;
 }
 initialize();
 if(!verify(q,K,turn))return 3;
 float h[D],z[D],orig[D];init_hz(orig,z);
 double wall_start=clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC),cpu_start=clk(CLOCK_PROCESS_CPUTIME_ID);
 unsigned long long n=0,counted=0;double checksum=0;
 do{
  memcpy(h,orig,sizeof(h));h[n%D]+=0.00001f;
  counted+=one_event(h,z,q,K,turn,late);
  checksum+=h[(n*31ull)%D];n++;
 }while(clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)-wall_start<seconds && n<3000000ull);
 double wall=clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)-wall_start,cpu=clk(CLOCK_PROCESS_CPUTIME_ID)-cpu_start;
 output_sink=checksum;
 unsigned long long executed=counted*BM,expected=n*required;
 printf("MEASURE mode=%s q=%d K=%d turn=%d events=%llu wall_ms_per_event=%.6f cpu_ms_per_event=%.6f total_F_MAC=%llu budget_per_event=%llu measured_blocks=%llu status=%s\n",
   argv[1],q,K,turn,n,wall*1000./n,cpu*1000./n,executed,budget,counted,executed==expected?"PASS":"FAIL");
 return executed==expected?0:4;
}
'''

def nint(p):
    try:return int(Path(p).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telem():
    try:
        m=int(next(s for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,ValueError,StopIteration,IndexError):m=None
    return nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),m

def safety(host_smoke):
    if host_smoke:return None
    b,t,m=telem()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'UNAVAILABLE_OR_INVALID_TELEMETRY'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_AT_LEAST_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def compile_native(root):
    exe=root/'budgeted_cyclic_mask';src=root/'budgeted_cyclic_mask.c'
    src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    clang=shutil.which('clang')
    if not clang:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_REQUIRED')
    p=subprocess.run([clang,'-O3','-std=c11','-Wall','-Wextra',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=35)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('C_COMPILATION_FAILED '+p.stderr[-350:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def background_check():
    bin=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not bin.is_file() or not os.access(bin,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_BASELINE_BINARY_MISSING')
    p=subprocess.run([str(bin),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=9)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_BASELINE_SELFTEST_FAILED')
    return bin

def bg_worker(bin,cancel,results,ready):
    rates=[];errs=[]
    try:
        while not cancel.is_set():
            p=subprocess.run([str(bin),'rank','full','0.70'],text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=9)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BASELINE_EXEC_FAIL')
            mt=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not mt:raise RuntimeError('GMAC_NOT_PRESENT')
            rates.append(float(mt[1]))
            if len(rates)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=2)
    except BaseException as e:errs.append(str(e)[:100])
    try:results.put((os.getpid(),len(rates),min(rates) if rates else None,statistics.mean(rates) if rates else None,errs),timeout=2)
    except BaseException:pass

def hold_ram(mib,cancel,ready):
    try:
        mem=bytearray(mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(mem),4096):mem[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=3)
        while not cancel.wait(.12):
            for i in range(0,len(mem),262144):mem[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def run_fixture(bin,mode,q,K,turn,budget,seconds):
    args=[str(bin),mode,str(q),str(K),str(turn),str(budget),f'{seconds:.2f}']
    p=subprocess.run(args,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=max(9,seconds+8))
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(f'FIXTURE_EXIT_{p.returncode} '+p.stdout[-200:]+p.stderr[-100:])
    if p.stdout.count('BUDGET_ABORT='):
        if 'admission=DENIED executed_F_MAC=0' not in p.stdout or 'status=PASS' not in p.stdout:raise RuntimeError('ABORT_MISMATCH')
        return {'abort':True,'mode':mode,'q':q,'K':K,'turn':turn}
    if not re.search(r'CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('GATE_DENSE_STATE_CONTRACT_FAIL')
    mm=re.search(r'MEASURE mode=(\w+) q=(\d+) K=(\d+) turn=(\d+) events=(\d+) wall_ms_per_event=([.\d]+) cpu_ms_per_event=([.\d]+) total_F_MAC=(\d+) budget_per_event=(\d+) measured_blocks=(\d+) status=(PASS|FAIL)',p.stdout)
    if not mm:raise RuntimeError('BAD_MEASURE_OUTPUT '+p.stdout[-200:])
    mode0,q0,k0,turn0,n,wall,cpu,total,budget0,blocks,status=mm.groups()
    n=int(n);total=int(total);blocks=int(blocks)
    expected_blocks=n*(8 if mode=='late' else q)*K
    if (mode0,q0,k0,turn0,status)!=(mode,str(q),str(K),str(turn),'PASS') or total!=expected_blocks*BRANCH_MAC or blocks!=expected_blocks or int(budget0)!=budget:
        raise RuntimeError('EXECUTED_LEDGER_MISMATCH')
    return {'abort':False,'mode':mode,'q':q,'K':K,'turn':turn,'events':n,'wall_ms':float(wall),'cpu_ms':float(cpu),'mac':total//n}

def stage(log,ctx,bin,background,name,workers,min_seconds,host_smoke,inject_abort=False):
    cancel=ctx.Event();results=ctx.Queue();ready=ctx.Queue();ram_ready=ctx.Queue()
    procs=[];holder=None;measurements=[];abort_count=0;failures=0;stop_reason=None;start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=hold_ram,args=(32,cancel,ram_ready));holder.start()
        if ram_ready.get(timeout=9) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(workers):
            p=ctx.Process(target=bg_worker,args=(background,cancel,results,ready));p.start();procs.append(p)
        active=set()
        for _ in range(workers):active.add(ready.get(timeout=22))
        if len(active)!=workers:raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_WORKER_COUNT_MISMATCH')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} S5_active={len(active)} resident_ram_MiB=32 synthetic_F=YES min_duration_s={min_seconds}')
        # The order is changed between repetitions; no zero-load phase.
        for rep in (1,2):
            configs=CONFIGS if rep==1 else tuple(reversed(CONFIGS))
            for q,K,turn in configs:
                if (err:=safety(host_smoke)):
                    stop_reason=err;break
                grant=q*K*BRANCH_MAC
                # A real gate is charged for actually executed branches.
                g=run_fixture(bin,'gate',q,K,turn,grant,.28 if host_smoke else .38)
                l=run_fixture(bin,'late',q,K,turn,8*K*BRANCH_MAC,.28 if host_smoke else .38)
                if g['abort'] or l['abort']:raise RuntimeError('UNEXPECTED_BUDGET_ABORT')
                measurements.extend((g,l))
                if inject_abort and len(measurements)>=6:
                    stop_reason='HOST_INJECTED_SAFETY_ABORT_AFTER_3_PAIRS'
                    log(f'SAFETY_ABORT stage={name} reason={stop_reason} cleanup=IN_PROGRESS')
                    break
                ratio=l['wall_ms']/g['wall_ms'] if g['wall_ms'] else float('nan')
                log(f'PAIR stage={name} rep={rep} q={q} K={K} turnSerial={turn} '
                    f'gate_MAC={g["mac"]} late_MAC={l["mac"]} '
                    f'gate_wall_ms={g["wall_ms"]:.6f} late_wall_ms={l["wall_ms"]:.6f} '
                    f'gate_cpu_ms={g["cpu_ms"]:.6f} late_cpu_ms={l["cpu_ms"]:.6f} '
                    f'wall_ratio={ratio:.3f} state_contract=PASS ledger=PASS')
                # Negative control: the same underfunded late compute must refuse before F invocation.
                if q<8 and K==8:
                    denied=run_fixture(bin,'late',q,K,turn,grant,.28)
                    if not denied['abort']:raise RuntimeError('UNDERBUDGET_LATE_WAS_ADMITTED')
                    abort_count+=1
                    log(f'BUDGET_ABORT_CHECK stage={name} rep={rep} q={q} K={K} '
                        f'budget_MAC={grant} late_required_MAC={8*K*BRANCH_MAC} '
                        'no_F_invocation=YES state_intact=YES result=PASS')
            if stop_reason:break
        # Impossible zero-budget prefix: must abstain, never invent q=0.
        if not stop_reason:
            for rep in range(2):
                x=run_fixture(bin,'gate',1,1,rep,0,.28)
                if not x['abort']:raise RuntimeError('EMPTY_FEASIBLE_SET_DID_NOT_ABORT')
                abort_count+=1
            log(f'EMPTY_FEASIBLE_SET stage={name} zero_budget_trials=2 result=BUDGET_ABORT no_F_invocation=YES')
            while time.monotonic()-start<min_seconds:
                if (err:=safety(host_smoke)):
                    stop_reason=err;break
                time.sleep(.12)
    except BaseException as e:
        failures+=1
        log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={name} type={type(e).__name__} message={str(e)[:220]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for p in procs:
            p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        done=0
        for _ in procs:
            try:
                pid,n,lo,avg,err=results.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={n} min_GMAC_s={lo} mean_GMAC_s={avg} errors={err}')
                if n>0 and not err:done+=1
                else:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                failures+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} RESULT_MISSING')
        for mq in (results,ready,ram_ready):mq.cancel_join_thread();mq.close()
    pairs=[(measurements[i],measurements[i+1]) for i in range(0,len(measurements)-1,2)]
    cpu_ratios=[l['cpu_ms']/g['cpu_ms'] for g,l in pairs if g['cpu_ms']>0]
    wall_ratios=[l['wall_ms']/g['wall_ms'] for g,l in pairs if g['wall_ms']>0]
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if stop_reason else ('COMPLETE' if failures==0 and done==workers and len(pairs)==12 and abort_count==6 else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY name={name} status={status} pairs={len(pairs)}/12 abort_checks={abort_count}/6 '
        f'background_workers_ok={done}/{workers} errors={failures} '
        f'median_wall_ratio={statistics.median(wall_ratios) if wall_ratios else None} '
        f'median_cpu_ratio={statistics.median(cpu_ratios) if cpu_ratios else None} '
        f'wall_ratio_min={min(wall_ratios) if wall_ratios else None} '
        f'wall_ratio_max={max(wall_ratios) if wall_ratios else None} safety_reason={stop_reason or "-"}')
    b,t,m=telem();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY name={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return status,len(pairs),abort_count

def main():
    import argparse
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='HOST ONLY: bypasses battery telemetry; NOT phone evidence')
    ap.add_argument('--host-abort-check',action='store_true',help='HOST ONLY: inject safety abort after 3 pairs; not for Android')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    if args.host_abort_check and not args.host_smoke:
        ap.error('--host-abort-check requires --host-smoke')
    home=Path.home();download=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not download.is_dir() or not os.access(download,os.W_OK):download=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=download/f'UNO_A52_R41_S5_PREFIX_BUDGET_V10_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);fp.write(s+'\n');fp.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- CYCLIC PREFIX MASK + MAC ADMISSION UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('R4.1 S6 candidate cyclic-prefix q and forced K in [1,8], not actual S6 learned logits/hazard or production policy.')
            log('Native synthetic C256R32 FP32; distinct measured CPU time vs elapsed wall time. Counted F-MAC excludes control, DSP, memory scan, cache effects and system overhead.')
            log('2 and 4 native S5 background workers, 32 MiB touched RAM; no zero-load stage. Fixed hardware safety guard: battery>=40%, battery temp<37.5C, MemAvailable>=400000 kB.')
            log('NOT real trained weights, model quality, energy, Android dispatcher, permissions, owner auth, production code, deadline SLA or neural learning.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telem();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            if err:=safety(args.host_smoke):
                log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP reason='+err);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
            background=background_check();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_S5 '+str(background))
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_prefix_budget_v10'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            bin,sha=compile_native(root)
            log(f'COMPILED_NATIVE_FIXTURE {bin} source_sha256={sha}')
            plans=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,3),('S5x4_RAM32',4,4)] if args.host_smoke else [('S5x2_RAM32',2,22),('S5x4_RAM32',4,28)]
            passed=0;count=0;denied=0
            for name,workers,floor in plans:
                if err:=safety(args.host_smoke):
                    log(f'RESULT SAFETY_ABORT_BEFORE_STAGE name={name} reason={err} partial_pairs={count}')
                    log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
                status,pairs,aborts=stage(log,mp.get_context('fork'),bin,background,name,workers,floor,args.host_smoke,args.host_abort_check)
                count+=pairs;denied+=aborts;passed+=status=='COMPLETE'
                if status!='COMPLETE':
                    log(f'RESULT {status} stages_complete={passed} pairs={count} budget_aborts={denied}')
                    log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3 if status=='SAFETY_ABORT' else 2
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_NATIVE_PREFIX_BUDGET_LAB stages={passed}/2 pairs={count}/24 budget_abort_checks={denied}/12')
            log('LIMIT: budget governs only counted synthetic F MAC; CPU and wall time are measured, not guaranteed, and no trained-model quality or platform security is shown.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report));return 0
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPTED');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:220]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2
if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 experimental S6 prefix sampler / synthetic hazard / F-only MAC feasibility.
Actual native C256R32 FP32 on Android CPU under 2/4 continuously active S5 background jobs.
No trained selector or hazard, no whole-turn budget, no production code.
OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import math
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback
from collections import Counter

VERSION = 'UNO_R41_A52_S6_SAMPLER_HAZARD_LOADED_V11'
BM = 28672
EPS = .05
TEMP = 1.0
SYNTHETIC_HAZARD_P = .35
MIB = 1048576
BUDGET_UNITS = (0,1,2,3,4,6,8,12,16,24,32,48,64)
C_SOURCE = '\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <math.h>\n#include <time.h>\n#define D 256\n#define B 32\n#define NB 8\n#define U 128\n#define R 32\n#define BM 28672ull\nstatic float A2[NB][R][D], A1[NB][U][R], C2[NB][R][D], C1[NB][U][R], BB[NB][B][U];\nstatic volatile double discard_sink=0, output_sink=0;\nstatic uint32_t prng=0x3acd71b9u;\nstatic uint32_t r32(void){prng^=prng<<13;prng^=prng>>17;prng^=prng<<5;return prng;}\nstatic float sval(void){return ((float)(r32()%20001)/10000.f-1.f)*.008f;}\nstatic double clk(clockid_t id){struct timespec ts;clock_gettime(id,&ts);return ts.tv_sec+ts.tv_nsec*1e-9;}\nstatic void initialize(void){\n for(int j=0;j<NB;j++){\n  for(int r=0;r<R;r++)for(int i=0;i<D;i++){A2[j][r][i]=sval();C2[j][r][i]=sval();}\n  for(int x=0;x<U;x++)for(int r=0;r<R;r++){A1[j][x][r]=sval();C1[j][x][r]=sval();}\n  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)for(int x=0;x<U;x++)BB[j][b][x]=sval();\n }\n}\nstatic void init_hz(float h[D],float z[D]){\n for(int i=0;i<D;i++){h[i]=.02f*(float)((i%17)-8);z[i]=.015f*(float)((i%11)-5);}\n}\nstatic int selected(int j,int q,int turn){return (j-turn+NB)%NB<q;}\nstatic unsigned long long do_step(float h[D],const float z[D],int q,int turn,int late){\n float next[D],a[R],c[R],u[U],v[B];unsigned long long done=0;\n memcpy(next,h,sizeof(next));\n for(int j=0;j<NB;j++){\n  int active=selected(j,q,turn);\n  if(!active&&!late)continue;\n  done++;\n  for(int r=0;r<R;r++){\n   float sa=0,sc=0;\n   for(int i=0;i<D;i++){sa+=A2[j][r][i]*h[i];sc+=C2[j][r][i]*z[i];}\n   a[r]=sa;c[r]=sc;\n  }\n  for(int x=0;x<U;x++){\n   float sa=0,sc=0;\n   for(int r=0;r<R;r++){sa+=A1[j][x][r]*a[r];sc+=C1[j][x][r]*c[r];}\n   u[x]=tanhf(sa+sc);\n  }\n  for(int b=0;b<B;b++){\n   float sv=0;for(int x=0;x<U;x++)sv+=BB[j][b][x]*u[x];\n   v[b]=tanhf(sv);\n  }\n  if(!active){discard_sink+=(double)v[j%B];continue;}\n  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)next[j*B+b]=.5f*h[j*B+b]+.5f*v[b];\n }\n memcpy(h,next,sizeof(next));return done;\n}\nstatic unsigned long long one_event(float h[D],const float z[D],int q,int K,int turn,int late){\n unsigned long long done=0;\n for(int k=0;k<K;k++)done+=do_step(h,z,q,turn,late);\n return done;\n}\nstatic int verify(int q,int K,int turn){\n float x[D],y[D],h[D],z[D];init_hz(h,z);\n memcpy(x,h,sizeof(x));memcpy(y,h,sizeof(y));\n unsigned long long a=one_event(x,z,q,K,turn,0),b=one_event(y,z,q,K,turn,1);\n int equal=memcmp(x,y,sizeof(x))==0,unchanged=1;\n for(int j=0;j<NB;j++)if(!selected(j,q,turn))\n  for(int i=j*B;i<(j+1)*B;i++)if(x[i]!=h[i])unchanged=0;\n printf("CONTRACT equal_gate_late=%s inactive_unchanged=%s gate_blocks=%llu late_blocks=%llu status=%s\\n",\n  equal?"YES":"NO",unchanged?"YES":"NO",a,b,(equal&&unchanged&&a==(unsigned long long)q*K&&b==(unsigned long long)NB*K)?"PASS":"FAIL");\n return equal&&unchanged&&a==(unsigned long long)q*K&&b==(unsigned long long)NB*K;\n}\nint main(int argc,char**argv){\n if(argc!=7){fprintf(stderr,"usage: fixture gate|late q K turn mac_budget duration_s\\n");return 2;}\n int late=!strcmp(argv[1],"late"),gate=!strcmp(argv[1],"gate");\n int q=atoi(argv[2]),K=atoi(argv[3]),turn=atoi(argv[4]);\n unsigned long long budget=strtoull(argv[5],NULL,10);\n double seconds=atof(argv[6]);\n if((!late&&!gate)||q<1||q>8||K<1||K>8||turn<0||turn>=8||seconds<.20||seconds>1.50)return 2;\n const unsigned long long required=(unsigned long long)(late?8:q)*K*BM;\n printf("REQUEST mode=%s q=%d K=%d turn=%d budget_mac=%llu required_mac=%llu\\n",argv[1],q,K,turn,budget,required);\n if(required>budget){\n  // Budget check precedes initialization and any F invocation.\n  printf("BUDGET_ABORT=no_F_invocation admission=DENIED executed_F_MAC=0 state_intact=YES beta_updated=NO status=PASS\\n");return 0;\n }\n initialize();\n if(!verify(q,K,turn))return 3;\n float h[D],z[D],orig[D];init_hz(orig,z);\n double wall_start=clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC),cpu_start=clk(CLOCK_PROCESS_CPUTIME_ID);\n unsigned long long n=0,counted=0;double checksum=0;\n do{\n  memcpy(h,orig,sizeof(h));h[n%D]+=0.00001f;\n  counted+=one_event(h,z,q,K,turn,late);\n  checksum+=h[(n*31ull)%D];n++;\n }while(clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)-wall_start<seconds && n<3000000ull);\n double wall=clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)-wall_start,cpu=clk(CLOCK_PROCESS_CPUTIME_ID)-cpu_start;\n output_sink=checksum;\n unsigned long long executed=counted*BM,expected=n*required;\n printf("MEASURE mode=%s q=%d K=%d turn=%d events=%llu wall_ms_per_event=%.6f cpu_ms_per_event=%.6f total_F_MAC=%llu budget_per_event=%llu measured_blocks=%llu status=%s\\n",\n   argv[1],q,K,turn,n,wall*1000./n,cpu*1000./n,executed,budget,counted,executed==expected?"PASS":"FAIL");\n return executed==expected?0:4;\n}\n'


class Xorshift32:
    def __init__(self,seed):
        assert 0<seed<2**32
        self.s=seed
        self.draws=0
    def random(self):
        x=self.s
        x^=(x<<13)&0xffffffff
        x^=x>>17
        x^=(x<<5)&0xffffffff
        self.s=x & 0xffffffff
        self.draws+=1
        return self.s/4294967296.0

def candidate_logits(turn):
    # Deterministic synthetic logits; NOT W_M inference and NOT trained.
    return tuple(.65*math.sin((j+1)*.7+turn*.11)+.35*math.cos(j*1.3) for j in range(8))

def s6_probs(feasible,turn):
    if not feasible:return ()
    logits=candidate_logits(turn)
    scores=[]
    for q in feasible:
        # sigma(j)=(j+turn mod 8) mod 8
        scores.append(sum(logits[(j+turn)%8] for j in range(q))/math.sqrt(q)/TEMP)
    mx=max(scores)
    exps=[math.exp(a-mx) for a in scores]
    den=sum(exps)
    p=tuple(EPS/len(feasible)+(1-EPS)*v/den for v in exps)
    if not math.isclose(sum(p),1.0,rel_tol=0,abs_tol=1e-12):raise AssertionError('PROB_SUM')
    if min(p)+1e-14<EPS/len(feasible):raise AssertionError('NO_FULL_SUPPORT')
    return p

def choose(budget_mac,turn,rng):
    feasible=tuple(range(1,min(8,budget_mac//BM)+1))
    if not feasible:
        return (0,0,(),(),'BUDGET_ABORT')
    probs=s6_probs(feasible,turn)
    draw=rng.random()
    mass=0.0;q=feasible[-1]
    for prefix,p in zip(feasible,probs):
        mass+=p
        if draw<mass:
            q=prefix;break
    max_steps=min(8,budget_mac//(q*BM))
    if max_steps<1:raise AssertionError('EMPTY_STEPS')
    # Pre-sampled synthetic hazard. It does NOT use intermediate h_k;
    # production would sample hazard after each completed step.
    K=max_steps
    for k in range(1,max_steps+1):
        if rng.random()<SYNTHETIC_HAZARD_P:
            K=k;break
    if not (1<=q<=8 and 1<=K<=8 and q*K*BM<=budget_mac):
        raise AssertionError('BUDGET_OVERRUN')
    return(q,K,feasible,probs,'ADMITTED')

def selftest():
    assert choose(0,0,Xorshift32(1))[-1]=='BUDGET_ABORT'
    assert choose(BM-1,0,Xorshift32(1))[-1]=='BUDGET_ABORT'
    for turn in range(17):
        for b in BUDGET_UNITS:
            a=choose(b*BM,turn,Xorshift32(0x56ee2091))
            z=choose(b*BM,turn,Xorshift32(0x56ee2091))
            assert a==z
            q,K,feasible,probs,status=a
            if b==0:assert status=='BUDGET_ABORT'
            else:
                assert q in feasible and q*K<=b
                assert len(probs)==len(feasible)
                assert math.isclose(sum(probs),1.0,abs_tol=1e-12)
    # Check epsilon mixing on a fixed, fully feasible scenario by an independent empirical draw.
    feasible=tuple(range(1,9));p=s6_probs(feasible,5)
    rng=Xorshift32(0x38f7d1ab);count=Counter()
    for _ in range(8000):
        u=rng.random();sm=0
        for q,z in zip(feasible,p):
            sm+=z
            if u<sm:count[q]+=1;break
    assert sum(count.values())==8000
    assert max(abs(count[q]/8000-prob) for q,prob in zip(feasible,p))<.035
    return 'SELFTEST_PASS feasibility=YES epsilon_softmax=YES PRNG_replay=YES distribution_8000=YES'

def nint(p):
    try:return int(Path(p).read_text().strip())
    except (ValueError,OSError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:m=int(next(s for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (ValueError,OSError,StopIteration,IndexError):m=None
    return nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),nint('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),m

def safety(host_smoke):
    if host_smoke:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in(b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'MISSING_OR_INVALID_HARDWARE_TELEMETRY'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_LT_40'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def compile_binary(root):
    src=root/'s6_synthetic_F.c';exe=root/'s6_synthetic_F'
    src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    clang=shutil.which('clang')
    if not clang:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_REQUIRED')
    p=subprocess.run([clang,'-O3','-std=c11','-Wall','-Wextra',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=40)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('COMPILATION_FAILURE '+p.stderr[-400:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def baseline_binary():
    exe=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not exe.exists() or not os.access(exe,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('BASELINE_S5_BINARY_MISSING')
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=9)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BASELINE_S5_SELFTEST_FAILED')
    return exe

def background_worker(exe,cancel,res,ready):
    arr=[];err=[]
    try:
        while not cancel.is_set():
            p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=9)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_ERROR')
            mt=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([.\d]+)',p.stdout)
            if not mt:raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_PERF_MISSING')
            arr.append(float(mt[1]))
            if len(arr)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=2)
    except BaseException as e:err.append(str(e)[:120])
    try:res.put((os.getpid(),len(arr),statistics.mean(arr) if arr else None,min(arr) if arr else None,err),timeout=2)
    except BaseException:pass

def ram_holder(size_mib,cancel,ready):
    try:
        data=bytearray(size_mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(data),4096):data[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=3)
        while not cancel.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(data),262144):data[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def native_execute(exe,q,K,turn,budget_mac,seconds):
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'gate',str(q),str(K),str(turn%8),str(budget_mac),f'{seconds:.2f}'],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(f'EXEC_FAIL_{p.returncode} '+p.stdout[-230:]+p.stderr[-100:])
    if 'CONTRACT equal_gate_late=YES inactive_unchanged=YES' not in p.stdout:raise RuntimeError('STATE_CONTRACT_FAIL')
    mt=re.search(r'MEASURE mode=gate q=(\d+) K=(\d+) turn=(\d+) events=(\d+) wall_ms_per_event=([.\d]+) cpu_ms_per_event=([.\d]+) total_F_MAC=(\d+) budget_per_event=(\d+) measured_blocks=(\d+) status=(PASS|FAIL)',p.stdout)
    if not mt:raise RuntimeError('MEASUREMENT_MISSING '+p.stdout[-120:])
    qq,kk,tt,ev,wall,cpu,total,bud,blocks,status=mt.groups()
    if (int(qq),int(kk),int(tt),int(bud),status)!=(q,K,turn%8,budget_mac,'PASS'):raise RuntimeError('PARAMETER_MISMATCH')
    if int(blocks)!=int(ev)*q*K or int(total)!=int(ev)*q*K*BM:raise RuntimeError('MAC_LEDGER_MISMATCH')
    return int(ev),float(wall),float(cpu)

def stage(log,ctx,exe,baseline,name,nworkers,stage_floor,host_smoke):
    cancel=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();res=ctx.Queue();rready=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None
    started=time.monotonic();observations=[];plans=[];blocked=0;failures=0;stop_reason=None;finished=0
    seed=0x71c35519+nworkers
    generator=Xorshift32(seed)
    ntrials=10 if host_smoke else 39
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=ram_holder,args=(32,cancel,rready));holder.start()
        if rready.get(timeout=8) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_START_FAIL')
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            w=ctx.Process(target=background_worker,args=(baseline,cancel,res,ready));w.start();workers.append(w)
        r={ready.get(timeout=24) for _ in workers}
        if len(r)!=nworkers:raise RuntimeError('S5_NOT_CONTINUOUSLY_ACTIVE')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={name} S5_background={nworkers} RAM_MiB=32 trials={ntrials} min_duration_s={stage_floor}')
        for idx in range(ntrials):
            if why:=safety(host_smoke):stop_reason=why;break
            units=BUDGET_UNITS[(idx*7+idx//len(BUDGET_UNITS))%len(BUDGET_UNITS)]
            # Ensure both zero budget and broad prefixes are exercised systematically.
            units=BUDGET_UNITS[idx%len(BUDGET_UNITS)] if idx%2==0 else units
            turn=idx
            before=generator.draws
            q,K,feasible,p,status=choose(units*BM,turn,generator)
            plans.append((units,turn,q,K,status,before,generator.draws))
            if status=='BUDGET_ABORT':
                blocked+=1
                log(f'ADMISSION stage={name} trial={idx} budget_F_MAC={units*BM} status=BUDGET_ABORT q=NONE K=NONE invoked_F=NO draws_used={generator.draws-before}')
                continue
            if q*K>units:raise RuntimeError('F_MAC_OVERRUN')
            ev,wall,cpu=native_execute(exe,q,K,turn,units*BM,.21 if host_smoke else .26)
            observations.append((wall,cpu,q,K,units))
            if idx%4==0 or idx==ntrials-1:
                log(f'S6_EXEC stage={name} trial={idx} turn={turn} budget_F_MAC={units*BM} feasible_prefixes={len(feasible)} '
                    f'selected_q={q} sampled_K={K} reserved_F_MAC={q*K*BM} events={ev} '
                    f'wall_ms_per_event={wall:.4f} cpu_ms_per_event={cpu:.4f} replay_draws={generator.draws-before} status=PASS')
        # Pure selector replay with the same logged seed, independent of executing F.
        rng=Xorshift32(seed)
        for units,turn,q,K,status,prev,post in plans:
            if prev!=rng.draws:raise RuntimeError('REPLAY_RNG_OFFSET_MISMATCH')
            a=choose(units*BM,turn,rng)
            if (q,K,status)!=(a[0],a[1],a[-1]) or rng.draws!=post:raise RuntimeError('SELECTOR_REPLAY_MISMATCH')
        log(f'REPLAY_CHECK stage={name} decisions={len(plans)} seed=0x{seed:08x} exact_match=YES random_draws={rng.draws}')
        while time.monotonic()-started<stage_floor:
            if why:=safety(host_smoke):stop_reason=why;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as e:
        failures+=1
        log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={name} error={type(e).__name__} detail={str(e)[:200]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for w in workers:
            w.join(5)
            if w.is_alive():w.kill();w.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,ran,avg,lo,err=res.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} native_runs={ran} mean_GMAC_s={avg} min_GMAC_s={lo} errors={err}')
                if ran>0 and not err:finished+=1
                else:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                failures+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} RESULT_MISSING')
        for qobj in (ready,res,rready):qobj.cancel_join_thread();qobj.close()
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if stop_reason else ('COMPLETE' if failures==0 and len(plans)==ntrials and finished==nworkers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={name} status={status} planned={len(plans)}/{ntrials} native_exec={len(observations)} '
        f'zero_feasible_aborts={blocked} workers_ok={finished}/{nworkers} failures={failures} '
        f'wall_median_ms={statistics.median(z[0] for z in observations) if observations else None} '
        f'wall_max_ms={max(z[0] for z in observations) if observations else None} '
        f'cpu_median_ms={statistics.median(z[1] for z in observations) if observations else None} '
        f'PRNG_replay={"YES" if failures==0 else "UNKNOWN"} safety_reason={stop_reason or "-"}')
    b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={name} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return status,len(observations),blocked

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='HOST-ONLY; never use this option on A52')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    root=Path.home();downloads=root/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=root
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_S6_SAMPLER_HAZARD_V11_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);fp.write(s+'\n');fp.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- R4.1 S6 SYNTHETIC EPSILON-SOFTMAX / HAZARD UNDER NATIVE ARM S5 '+VERSION)
            log('S6 trial: nonempty cyclic prefixes, epsilon=0.05 T=1, deterministic recorded PRNG, synthetic logits and presampled hazard=0.35; NOT W_M or neural hazard inference.')
            log('Feasibility is F-only MAC cap, NOT full turn admission: encoder, heads, time, memory, tokens and OS/thermal costs excluded.')
            log('Real native FP32 C256R32 work runs on Android CPU under 2/4 continuously active S5 workers plus touched 32MiB RAM. No idle stage.')
            log('NO: model training, production neural weights, Android permissions, real actions, owner authorization, Binder, hardware power-cut, global stability, quality proof or deadline guarantee.')
            log('SAFETY: battery>=40%; battery temperature<37.5C; MemAvailable>=400000 kB; script cleans up child processes on exit.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            if reason:=safety(args.host_smoke):
                log(f'RESULT SAFETY_SKIP reason={reason}');log(f'REPORT {report}');return 3
            log(selftest())
            baseline=baseline_binary();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_S5 '+str(baseline))
            work=root/'uno_a52_r41_s6_sampler_v11'/stamp
            work.mkdir(parents=True,mode=0o700,exist_ok=False)
            exe,sha=compile_binary(work);log(f'COMPILED_NATIVE_F {exe} source_sha256={sha}')
            specs=[('S5x2_RAM32',2,3),('S5x4_RAM32',4,4)] if args.host_smoke else [('S5x2_RAM32',2,24),('S5x4_RAM32',4,30)]
            total_exec=total_abort=complete=0
            for name,nworkers,floor in specs:
                if reason:=safety(args.host_smoke):
                    log(f'RESULT SAFETY_ABORT_BEFORE_STAGE name={name} reason={reason} completed={complete}')
                    log(f'REPORT {report}');return 3
                status,exec_n,abort_n=stage(log,mp.get_context('fork'),exe,baseline,name,nworkers,floor,args.host_smoke)
                total_exec+=exec_n;total_abort+=abort_n;complete+=status=='COMPLETE'
                if status!='COMPLETE':
                    log(f'RESULT {status} completed_stages={complete}/2 native_executions={total_exec} budget_aborts={total_abort}')
                    log(f'REPORT {report}');return 3 if status=='SAFETY_ABORT' else 2
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_S6_SAMPLER_LAB stages={complete}/2 native_executions={total_exec} budget_aborts={total_abort}')
            log('LIMIT: seeded synthetic selector with presampled K is not learned adaptive computation; it does not satisfy S6 full-resource feasibility or after-step state-dependent hazard.')
            log(f'REPORT {report}');return 0
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPTED');log(f'REPORT {report}');return 130
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:200]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log(f'REPORT {report}');return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

class Xorshift32:
    def __init__(self,seed):
        assert 0<seed<2**32
        self.s=seed;self.draws=0
    def random(self):
        x=self.s
        x^=(x<<13)&0xffffffff;x^=x>>17;x^=(x<<5)&0xffffffff
        self.s=x&0xffffffff;self.draws+=1
        return self.s/4294967296.0

def candidate_logits(turn):
    return tuple(.65*math.sin((j+1)*.7+turn*.11)+.35*math.cos(j*1.3) for j in range(8))

def probabilities(feasible,turn):
    l=candidate_logits(turn);scores=[sum(l[(j+turn)%8] for j in range(q))/math.sqrt(q) for q in feasible]
    mx=max(scores);es=[math.exp(x-mx) for x in scores];total=sum(es)
    out=[EPS/len(feasible)+(1-EPS)*p/total for p in es]
    assert math.isclose(sum(out),1.0,abs_tol=1e-12)
    return out

def select_prefix(budget,turn,rng):
    feasible=tuple(range(1,min(8,budget//BM)+1))
    if not feasible:return None,(),0
    probs=probabilities(feasible,turn);draw=rng.random();v=0.;q=feasible[-1]
    for prefix,p in zip(feasible,probs):
        v+=p
        if draw<v:q=prefix;break
    kcap=min(8,budget//(q*BM))
    assert kcap>=1
    return q,probs,kcap

def parse_int(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (ValueError,OSError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:mem=int(next(line for line in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):mem=None
    return parse_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),parse_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),mem

def safety(smoke):
    if smoke:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'INVALID_TELEMETRY'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def compile_native(root):
    clang=shutil.which('clang')
    if not clang:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_NOT_FOUND')
    src=root/'after_step_hazard.c';exe=root/'after_step_hazard'
    src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    result=subprocess.run([clang,'-O3','-std=c11','-Wall','-Wextra',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=50)
    if result.returncode:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_FAILED '+result.stderr[-800:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def background_binary():
    exe=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not exe.exists() or not os.access(exe,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_MISSING')
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=9)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_CONTRACT_FAIL')
    return exe

def worker(exe,cancel,ready,result):
    vals=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not cancel.is_set():
            p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=9)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BASELINE_F_ERROR')
            match=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([.\d]+)',p.stdout)
            if not match:raise RuntimeError('NO_BACKGROUND_MEASUREMENT')
            vals.append(float(match[1]))
            if len(vals)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=3)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:160])
    try:result.put((os.getpid(),len(vals),statistics.mean(vals) if vals else None,min(vals) if vals else None,errors),timeout=3)
    except BaseException:pass

def mem_holder(mib,cancel,ready):
    try:
        data=bytearray(mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(data),4096):data[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=4)
        while not cancel.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(data),262144):data[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def run_native(exe,mode,q,turn,budget,seed,dur=.20):
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),mode,str(q),str(turn%8),str(budget),str(seed),f'{dur:.2f}'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=14)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_EXIT '+str(p.returncode)+' '+p.stdout[-280:]+' '+p.stderr[-280:])
    return p.stdout

def get_val(line,name):
    m=re.search(r'\b'+re.escape(name)+r'=([^\s]+)',line)
    if not m:raise RuntimeError('FIELD_MISSING '+name+' '+line[:180])
    return m.group(1)

def selftest(exe):
    x=run_native(exe,'abort',0,0,0,9,.05)
    if 'BUDGET_ABORT' not in x or 'F_invocations=0' not in x or 'hazard_calls=0' not in x:raise RuntimeError('ZERO_BUDGET_FAIL')
    pair=run_native(exe,'pair',1,0,8*BM,9,.05)
    if 'STATE_CAUSAL_PAIR' not in pair or 'plus_K=1 minus_K=8' not in pair or 'status=PASS' not in pair:raise RuntimeError('CAUSE_PAIR_FAIL '+pair[-320:])
    for q,kcap in [(1,8),(3,4),(8,8)]:
        a=run_native(exe,'trace',q,2,q*kcap*BM,0x34a86c2e,.05)
        b=run_native(exe,'trace',q,2,q*kcap*BM,0x34a86c2e,.05)
        if a!=b:raise RuntimeError('TRACE_REPLAY_NOT_EXACT')
        line=next((l for l in a.splitlines() if l.startswith('TRACE ')),None)
        if not line or get_val(line,'status')!='PASS':raise RuntimeError('TRACE_CONTRACT')
    return pair.strip().splitlines()[-1]

def stage(log,ctx,exe,bg,name,nworkers,smoke):
    cancel=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();result=ctx.Queue();rready=ctx.Queue()
    workers=[];holder=None;start=time.monotonic();errors=0;reason=None;completed=0;aborts=0;first=0
    measurements=[];plans=[];tracechecks=0;pairchecks=0;goodworkers=0
    seed=0x1156e01f+nworkers;chooser=Xorshift32(seed)
    ntrials=6 if smoke else 24
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=mem_holder,args=(32,cancel,rready));holder.start()
        if rready.get(timeout=9) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            w=ctx.Process(target=worker,args=(bg,cancel,ready,result));w.start();workers.append(w)
        if len({ready.get(timeout=24) for _ in workers})!=nworkers:raise RuntimeError('WORKERS_NOT_READY')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN name={name} S5_workers={nworkers} RAM_MiB=32 trials={ntrials} no_idle_phase=YES')
        # Under load, prove same uniform sample has different K because F updated h differently.
        pair=run_native(exe,'pair',1,nworkers,8*BM,0x41a9,.05)
        if 'plus_K=1 minus_K=8' not in pair or 'status=PASS' not in pair:raise RuntimeError('STATE_DEPENDENCE_NOT_PROVEN')
        log('STATE_CONTROL stage='+name+' '+next(l for l in pair.splitlines() if l.startswith('STATE_CAUSAL_PAIR ')))
        pairchecks+=1
        for i in range(ntrials):
            if why:=safety(smoke):reason=why;break
            # Mix tight budgets, broad budgets, and empty budgets.
            units=BUDGET_UNITS[(i*5+i//3)%len(BUDGET_UNITS)]
            if i in (0,11,20):units=0
            turn=i
            before=chooser.draws
            q,p,kcap=select_prefix(units*BM,turn,chooser)
            plans.append((units,turn,q,kcap,before,chooser.draws))
            if q is None:
                aborts+=1
                out=run_native(exe,'abort',0,turn,0,0x54ed,.05)
                if 'BUDGET_ABORT' not in out or 'F_invocations=0' not in out:raise RuntimeError('ABORT_INVOKED_F')
                log(f'ADMISSION stage={name} trial={i} status=BUDGET_ABORT no_F=YES budget_F_MAC=0 rng_draws={chooser.draws-before}')
                continue
            budget=units*BM;native_seed=(0x63ac7181 + i*977 + nworkers*31)&0xffffffff
            if i%6==2:
                a=run_native(exe,'trace',q,turn,budget,native_seed,.05)
                b=run_native(exe,'trace',q,turn,budget,native_seed,.05)
                if a!=b:raise RuntimeError('STATEFUL_TRACE_REPLAY_MISMATCH')
                t=next(l for l in a.splitlines() if l.startswith('TRACE '))
                tracechecks+=1
                log('HAZARD_REPLAY stage='+name+' trial='+str(i)+' '+t)
            out=run_native(exe,'bench',q,turn,budget,native_seed,.20 if smoke else .26)
            if 'MASK_CONTRACT early_equals_late=YES inactive_preserved=YES' not in out:raise RuntimeError('MASK_STATE_CONTRACT_FAIL')
            line=next((l for l in out.splitlines() if l.startswith('BENCH ')),None)
            if not line or get_val(line,'status')!='PASS':raise RuntimeError('BENCH_UNAVAILABLE '+out[-200:])
            events=int(get_val(line,'events'));km=float(get_val(line,'K_mean'));kmin=int(get_val(line,'K_min'));kmax=int(get_val(line,'K_max'))
            calls=int(get_val(line,'hazard_calls'));blocks=int(get_val(line,'F_blocks'));mac=int(get_val(line,'total_F_MAC'))
            if not (1<=kmin<=km<=kmax<=kcap and blocks==q*calls and mac==blocks*BM and calls>=events):raise RuntimeError('AFTER_STEP_LEDGER_FAIL')
            if blocks>events*q*kcap or mac>events*budget:raise RuntimeError('HAZARD_MAC_BUDGET_OVERFLOW')
            wall=float(get_val(line,'avg_wall_ms'));cpu=float(get_val(line,'avg_cpu_ms'))
            measurements.append((wall,cpu,q,kcap,km))
            completed+=1
            if i%4==0 or i==ntrials-1:
                log(f'AFTER_STEP stage={name} trial={i} q={q} maxK={kcap} events={events} actual_mean_K={km:.3f} '
                    f'hazard_decisions_after_F={calls} actual_F_MAC={mac} max_F_MAC={events*budget} '
                    f'wall_ms_per_event={wall:.4f} cpu_ms_per_event={cpu:.4f} result=PASS')
        # Exact replay of PREFIX decisions (K is not sampled in Python).
        generator=Xorshift32(seed)
        for units,turn,q,kcap,before,after in plans:
            if generator.draws!=before:raise RuntimeError('PREFIX_RNG_OFFSET_FAIL')
            qq,_,kk=select_prefix(units*BM,turn,generator)
            if (q,kcap)!=(qq,kk) or generator.draws!=after:raise RuntimeError('PREFIX_REPLAY_MISMATCH')
        log(f'PREFIX_REPLAY stage={name} plans={len(plans)} exact=YES rng_draws={generator.draws}')
        floor=4 if smoke else (24 if nworkers==2 else 30)
        while time.monotonic()-start<floor:
            if why:=safety(smoke):reason=why;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as e:
        errors+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={name} {type(e).__name__} {str(e)[:250]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for w in workers:
            w.join(5)
            if w.is_alive():w.kill();w.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,low,errs=result.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} pid={pid} runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean} min_GMAC_s={low} errors={errs}')
                if runs>=1 and not errs:goodworkers+=1
                else:errors+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                errors+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={name} MISSING_REPORT')
        for qobj in (ready,result,rready):
            qobj.cancel_join_thread();qobj.close()
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if reason else ('COMPLETE' if errors==0 and len(plans)==ntrials and goodworkers==nworkers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={name} status={status} planned={len(plans)}/{ntrials} native_exec={completed} '
        f'budget_aborts={aborts} hazard_replays={tracechecks} state_causal_pairs={pairchecks} '
        f'workers_ok={goodworkers}/{nworkers} errors={errors} '
        f'median_wall_ms={statistics.median(v[0] for v in measurements) if measurements else None} '
        f'wall_max_ms={max(v[0] for v in measurements) if measurements else None} '
        f'safety_reason={reason or "-"}')
    log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={name} battery={telemetry()[0]} temp_deciC={telemetry()[1]} MemAvailable_kB={telemetry()[2]}')
    return status,completed,aborts,tracechecks

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser();ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='Host-only integration check; never use on phone')
    args=ap.parse_args()

    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- R4.1 STATE-DEPENDENT AFTER-STEP HAZARD ON REAL ARM S5 '+VERSION)
            log('After native F updates h, candidate Bernoulli hazard p=sigmoid(4*h[active_first]) draws PRNG; cannot pre-sample K. C256R32, K<=8.')
            log('State carries across events in each native run. Prefix q uses synthetic epsilon-softmax, not learned W_M. Controlled +/- state pair uses same forced u=0.5.')
            log('F-only MAC budget; NOT complete turn admission or calibrated hazard. Not production learned model, Android Binder, OS actions, real user STOP, auth, hardware power-cut or energy.')
            log('Continuous 2/4 native S5 background workers + touched 32MiB RAM per stage. No zero-load stage. Platform guards unchanged.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            if reason:=safety(args.host_smoke):
                log(f'RESULT SAFETY_SKIP {reason}');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
            bg=background_binary();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_S5 '+str(bg))
            work=root/'uno_a52_r41_s6_afterstep_v12'/stamp
            work.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False,mode=0o700)

            log('SELFTEST '+selftest(exe))
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork');tot=aborts=replays=complete=0
            for name,workers in [('S5x2_RAM32',2),('S5x4_RAM32',4)]:

                    log(f'RESULT SAFETY_ABORT_BEFORE_STAGE stage={name} reason={reason}');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3
                status,n,a,r=stage(log,ctx,exe,bg,name,workers,args.host_smoke)

                    log(f'RESULT {status} complete_stages={complete}/2 native_exec={tot} budget_aborts={aborts}')
                    log('REPORT '+str(report));return 3 if status=='SAFETY_ABORT' else 2
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_AFTER_STEP_HAZARD_LAB stages={complete}/2 native_exec={tot} budget_aborts={aborts} replay_checks={replays}')
            log('LIMIT: post-F hazard is SYNTHETIC and uses a controlled scalar readout, not trained state-dependent neural hazard or energy-validated policy; no Android trust boundary.')
            log('REPORT '+str(report));return 0
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            log('RESULT USER_INTERRUPTED');log('REPORT '+str(report));return 130
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:260]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc().splitlines()[-1]);log('REPORT '+str(report));return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

(F_calls==0&&charged_mac==0) : (F_calls==K)));\n printf("CYCLE mode=%s status=%s q=%d maxK=%d K=%d F_calls=%d F_blocks=%llu MAC_cap=%llu MAC_charged=%llu encoder_MAC=%llu memory_MAC=%llu control_MAC=%llu head_MAC=%llu workspace_cap=%zu allocated_workspace=%d token_cap=%d tokens=%d head_calls=%d beta_unchanged=%s state_unchanged=%s wall_ms=%.3f cpu_ms=%.3f admission=%s contract=%s\\n",\n mode,status,q,maxsteps,K,F_calls,F_blocks,cap,charged_mac,ENCODER_MAC,MEMORY_MAC,CONTROL_MAC,HEAD_MAC,workspace_cap,workspace_allocated,tokens,token_used,head_calls,head_calls==0?"YES":"NO",!state_changed?"YES":"NO",\n (clk(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)-wall0)*1000.,(clk(CLOCK_PROCESS_CPUTIME_ID)-cpu0)*1000.,admitted?"YES":"NO",good?"PASS":"FAIL");\n return good?0:4;\n}\nint main(int argc,char **argv){\n if(argc!=9){fprintf(stderr,"usage fixture MODE q turn mac_cap workspace_cap token_cap deadline_ms seed\\n");return 2;}\n const char *mode=argv[1];int q=atoi(argv[2]),turn=atoi(argv[3]);\n unsigned long long cap=strtoull(argv[4],NULL,10);\n size_t ws=(size_t)strtoull(argv[5],NULL,10);\n int tok=atoi(argv[6]);double dl=atof(argv[7]);uint32_t seed=(uint32_t)strtoul(argv[8],NULL,10);\n if(turn<0||turn>=NB||!seed||dl<0||dl>10000)return 2;\n return cycle(mode,q,turn,cap,ws,tok,dl,seed);\n}\n'

def get_field(line,key):
    m=re.search(r'\b'+re.escape(key)+r'=([^\s]+)',line)
    if not m:raise RuntimeError('MISSING_FIELD '+key+' '+line[:140])
    return m[1]

def parse_int(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (ValueError,OSError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:mem=int(next(x for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):mem=None
    return parse_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),parse_int('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),mem

def safety(smoke):
    if smoke:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'INVALID_TELEMETRY'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def compile_native(where):
    cc=shutil.which('clang')
    if cc is None:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_NOT_FOUND')
    src=where/'v13_resource_cycle.c';exe=where/'v13_resource_cycle';src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    p=subprocess.run([cc,'-O3','-std=c11','-Wall','-Wextra',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=50)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('C_BUILD_FAILED '+p.stderr[-900:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def verify_background():
    exe=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not exe.is_file() or not os.access(exe,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_NOT_FOUND')
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=9)
    if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_NATIVE_NOT_VERIFIED')
    return exe

def s5_worker(exe,stop,ready,results):
    vals=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            p=subprocess.run([str(exe),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=9)
            if p.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',p.stdout):raise RuntimeError('S5_EXEC_FAILED')
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',p.stdout)
            if not m:raise RuntimeError('GMAC_S_NOT_FOUND')
            vals.append(float(m[1]))
            if len(vals)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=3)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:200])
    try:results.put((os.getpid(),len(vals),statistics.mean(vals) if vals else 0.,min(vals) if vals else 0.,errors),timeout=3)
    except BaseException:pass

def hold_ram(mib,stop,ready):
    try:
        buf=bytearray(mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(buf),4096):buf[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=5)
        while not stop.wait(.12):
            for i in range(0,len(buf),262144):buf[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def fixture_case(exe,mode,q,turn,mac,ws,tokens,deadline,seed):
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),mode,str(q),str(turn%8),str(mac),str(ws),str(tokens),str(deadline),str(seed)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_FAILED '+str(p.returncode)+' '+p.stdout[-400:]+' '+p.stderr[-220:])
    line=next((x for x in p.stdout.splitlines() if x.startswith('CYCLE ')),None)
    if not line or get_field(line,'contract')!='PASS':raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_CONTRACT_FAIL '+p.stdout[-400:])
    return line

MODES=('OK','SINGLE_STEP','MAC_ONLY','WORKSPACE','TOKENS','TIME_ZERO','TIME_OVERRUN','INVALID_Q')
EXPECT={'OK':'COMMITTED','SINGLE_STEP':'COMMITTED',
        'MAC_ONLY':'BUDGET_ABORT','WORKSPACE':'BUDGET_ABORT','TOKENS':'BUDGET_ABORT',
        'TIME_ZERO':'BUDGET_ABORT','TIME_OVERRUN':'ABSTAIN_DEADLINE','INVALID_Q':'BUDGET_ABORT'}

def configs(mode,q):
    cap=OTHER_MAC+8*q*BM;ws=WORKSPACE;tokens=1;deadline=5000
    if mode=='SINGLE_STEP':cap=OTHER_MAC+q*BM
    if mode=='MAC_ONLY':cap=q*BM
    if mode=='WORKSPACE':ws=WORKSPACE-1
    if mode=='TOKENS':tokens=0
    if mode=='TIME_ZERO':deadline=0
    if mode=='TIME_OVERRUN':deadline=2
    if mode=='INVALID_Q':q=0
    return q,cap,ws,tokens,deadline

def verify_case(line,mode,q,mac,workspace,tokens):
    state=get_field(line,'status');f_calls=int(get_field(line,'F_calls'))
    steps=int(get_field(line,'K'));charged=int(get_field(line,'MAC_charged'))
    head=int(get_field(line,'head_calls'));maxK=int(get_field(line,'maxK'))
    spent=int(get_field(line,'tokens'));alloc=int(get_field(line,'allocated_workspace'))
    if state!=EXPECT[mode]:raise RuntimeError('UNEXPECTED_STATUS mode='+mode+' '+line)
    if charged>mac or spent>tokens or alloc>workspace:raise RuntimeError('RESOURCE_CAP_OVERFLOW '+line)
    if int(get_field(line,'F_blocks'))!=q*steps or f_calls!=steps:raise RuntimeError('F_BLOCK_LEDGER '+line)
    if mode in ('OK','SINGLE_STEP'):
        if not (1<=steps<=maxK and head==1 and spent==1 and get_field(line,'state_unchanged')=='NO'):
            raise RuntimeError('ADMITTED_NO_COMMIT '+line)
        if mode=='SINGLE_STEP' and steps!=1:raise RuntimeError('UNEXPECTED_SECOND_STEP '+line)
    elif mode=='TIME_OVERRUN':
        if not (steps>=1 and head==0 and get_field(line,'state_unchanged')=='YES'):
            raise RuntimeError('DEADLINE_ABORT_MUST_NOT_COMMIT '+line)
    else:
        if not (f_calls==0 and head==0 and spent==0 and get_field(line,'state_unchanged')=='YES'):
            raise RuntimeError('DENIED_MODE_DID_WORK '+line)
    if charged != (OTHER_MAC-8192)+head*8192 + int(get_field(line,'F_blocks'))*BM and f_calls>0:
        raise RuntimeError('CYCLE_MAC_ACCOUNTING_ERROR '+line)
    return state,steps,float(get_field(line,'wall_ms'))

def single_stage(log,ctx,exe,bg,workers,smoke,inject_abort=False):
    label=f'S5x{workers}_RAM32';count=8 if smoke else 16
    cancel=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();result=ctx.Queue();ram_ready=ctx.Queue()
    sons=[];holder=None;timings=[];completed=0;denied=0;overrun=0;errors=0;reason=None;good=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=hold_ram,args=(32,cancel,ram_ready));holder.start()
        if ram_ready.get(timeout=9) is not True:raise RuntimeError('MEM_HOLDER_FAIL')
        for _ in range(workers):
            proc=ctx.Process(target=s5_worker,args=(bg,cancel,ready,result));proc.start();sons.append(proc)
        if len({ready.get(timeout=25) for _ in sons})!=workers:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_NOT_STARTED')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={label} native_S5_workers={workers} touched_RAM_MiB=32 cases={count}')
        for i in range(count):
            if why:=safety(smoke):reason=why;break
            if inject_abort and i>=3:reason='INJECTED_SAFETY_ABORT_HOST_ONLY';break
            mode=MODES[i%8];q=1+(i%4)
            qq,mac,ws,tok,dl=configs(mode,q)
            line=fixture_case(exe,mode,qq,i,mac,ws,tok,dl,0x119f551+i+workers)
            status,K,wall=verify_case(line,mode,qq,mac,ws,tok)
            completed+=1;timings.append(wall)
            if status=='BUDGET_ABORT':denied+=1
            if status=='ABSTAIN_DEADLINE':overrun+=1
            log(f'CASE stage={label} rep={i//8+1} {line}')
        floor=3 if smoke else (24 if workers==2 else 30)
        while not reason and time.monotonic()-start<floor:
            if why:=safety(smoke):reason=why;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as e:
        errors+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={label} {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:400]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for son in sons:
            son.join(5)
            if son.is_alive():son.kill();son.join(2)
        if holder:
            holder.join(3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(2)
        for _ in sons:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,mn,errs=result.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={label} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean:.5f} min_GMAC_s={mn:.5f} errors={errs}')
                if runs>=1 and not errs:good+=1
                else:errors+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                errors+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={label} MISSING_REPORT')
        for item in (ready,result,ram_ready):item.cancel_join_thread();item.close()
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if reason else ('COMPLETE' if completed==count and errors==0 and good==workers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={label} status={status} cases={completed}/{count} failures={errors} '
        f'budget_denials={denied} deadline_abstains={overrun} workers_ok={good}/{workers} '
        f'wall_median_ms={statistics.median(timings) if timings else None} '
        f'wall_max_ms={max(timings) if timings else None} safety_reason={reason or "-"}')
    log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={label} battery={telemetry()[0]} temp_deciC={telemetry()[1]} MemAvailable_kB={telemetry()[2]}')
    return status,completed,denied,overrun

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='Linux-only quick test, not for A52')
    ap.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true',help='Only with --host-smoke')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    if args.inject_host_abort and not args.host_smoke:raise SystemExit('INJECTION_REQUIRES_HOST_SMOKE')
    root=Path.home();downloads=root/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=root
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f');report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_S6_MULTI_RESOURCE_ADMISSION_V13_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- S6 FOUR RESOURCE GATES + NATIVE F CYCLE UNDER S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('Isolated synthetic cycle: encoder 8192 MAC; memory scan 16384 MAC; control 64 MAC; head 8192 MAC; F 28672 MAC/block/step.')
            log('Resources: reserved aggregate MAC, 65536-byte native workspace segment, synthetic token quota, measured monotonic deadline check.')
            log('Admission checks all resources BEFORE encoder/F. Interrupted turns do not commit h or beta; negative TIME_OVERRUN tests no hard deadline guarantee.')
            log('Only 65536-byte explicit workspace allocation is tracked, NOT peak RSS/Android memory; no DSP, tokenizer, audio, IO, readback or licensed data.')
            log('Real native C256R32 FP32 F + synthetic ancillary kernels under 2/4 native S5 background workers and 32 MiB touched RAM.')
            log('LAB ONLY: no trained weights, production policy, owner authorization, native Android STOP, real OS actions, energy or hard real-time promise.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            if why:=safety(args.host_smoke):log(f'RESULT SAFETY_SKIP {why}\nREPORT {report}');return 3
            bg=verify_background();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_S5 '+str(bg))
            folder=root/'uno_a52_r41_s6_resource_admission_v13'/stamp;folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
            exe,sha=compile_native(folder);log(f'COMPILED_NATIVE_CYCLE {exe} source_sha256={sha}')
            for mode in MODES:
                q=2
                qq,mac,ws,tok,dl=configs(mode,q)
                line=fixture_case(exe,mode,qq,1,mac,ws,tok,dl,6543)
                verify_case(line,mode,qq,mac,ws,tok)
            log('SELFTEST_PASS modes=8 admitted=2 denied=5 deadline_negative=1')
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            a=single_stage(log,ctx,exe,bg,2,args.host_smoke,args.inject_host_abort)
            if a[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {a[0]} stage=S5x2 partial_cases={a[1]}');return 3
            b=single_stage(log,ctx,exe,bg,4,args.host_smoke)
            if b[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {b[0]} stage=S5x4 partial_cases={b[1]}');return 3
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_SYNTHETIC_FULL_CYCLE_RESOURCE_LEDGER stages=2/2 cases={a[1]+b[1]} denied={a[2]+b[2]} deliberate_deadline_abstains={a[3]+b[3]}')
            log('LIMIT: counted MAC excludes uncounted scalar operations and Android costs; 65536-byte workspace != peak RSS; deadline is detection not guaranteed upper latency.')
            log('LIMIT: credit is pre-reserved for permitted K but the synthetic ancillary heads/data are not trained UNO perception or memories.')
            return 0
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:600]}');log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc()[-900:]);return 2
        finally:log('REPORT '+str(report))

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())
