#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 precode isolated exact-memory integrity laboratory, not production.
Real A52 native S5 background computation; synthetic bytes only; no P training.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import json
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import sqlite3
import statistics
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_EXACT_MEMORY_INTEGRITY_V14'
MIB=1048576
MAX_SLOTS=4  # fixture limit; normative full-store upper bound is 4096
MAX_PAYLOAD=4096
MAX_QUERY_BYTES=4096
MAX_CONDITIONS=16
MODEL_A=hashlib.sha256(b'fixture-model-a').digest()
MODEL_B=hashlib.sha256(b'fixture-model-b').digest()
MODES=(
 'EXACT_BYTE_MATCH','NEAR_TEXT_NOT_EXACT','CORRUPT_PAYLOAD_UNAVAILABLE',
 'CORRUPT_HEADER_UNAVAILABLE','MODEL_VERSION_QUARANTINE','BOOT_EPOCH_QUARANTINE','STALE_SLOT_GENERATION',
 'STABLE_PAGE_WALK','CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_MUTATION','CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_CONSENT',
 'CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_QUERY_CHANGE','CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_MODEL_CHANGE',
 'CURSOR_EXPIRED_DEADLINE',
 'TRUNCATED_REQUIRES_FULL_SCAN','ALL_PINNED_DENIED_STORAGE','CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT',
 'INVALID_QUERY_REJECTED','REVOKED_CONSENT_DENIES')

def get_number(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:mem=int(next(x for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):mem=None
    return get_number('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),get_number('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),mem

def safety(smoke):
    if smoke:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_BELOW_400000_KB'
    return None

def native_binary():
    p=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('MISSING_NATIVE_S5_BINARY '+str(p))
    r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_PRECHECK_FAILED '+r.stdout[-220:])
    return p

def s5_worker(binary,stop,ready,out):
    vals=[];errs=[]
    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            r=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10)
            if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
                raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_COMPUTE_FAILED')
            found=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',r.stdout)
            if not found:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_METRIC_MISSING')
            vals.append(float(found[1]))
            if len(vals)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=3)
    except BaseException as e:errs.append(str(e)[:150])
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(vals),statistics.mean(vals) if vals else 0.0,min(vals) if vals else 0.0,errs),timeout=3)
    except BaseException:pass

def ram_holder(stop,ready,mib=32):
    try:
        b=bytearray(mib*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(b),4096):b[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=5)
        while not stop.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(b),262144):b[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def connect(path):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),timeout=7,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000')
    return c

def init_store(path):
    c=connect(path)
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE meta (name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, val TEXT NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE records (slot INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, generation INTEGER NOT NULL, record_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, '
              'payload BLOB NOT NULL, payload_sha BLOB NOT NULL, header BLOB NOT NULL, model_digest BLOB NOT NULL, '
              'kind INTEGER NOT NULL, source_id INTEGER NOT NULL, schema_id INTEGER NOT NULL, source_sequence INTEGER NOT NULL, '
              'time_scope TEXT NOT NULL, pinned INTEGER NOT NULL, boot_epoch INTEGER NOT NULL, consent_epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    for key,val in [('snapshot','1'),('boot','1'),('consent','1'),('consent_granted','1'),('model',MODEL_A.hex())]:
        c.execute('INSERT INTO meta VALUES (?,?)',(key,val))
    return c

def meta(c,key):return c.execute('SELECT val FROM meta WHERE name=?',(key,)).fetchone()[0]
def bump(c,key):
    v=int(meta(c,key))+1
    c.execute('UPDATE meta SET val=? WHERE name=?',(str(v),key))
    return v

def header_bytes(slot,gen,rid,seq):
    buf=bytearray(64)
    struct.pack_into('<Q',buf,0,rid)
    struct.pack_into('<I',buf,8,slot)
    struct.pack_into('<I',buf,12,gen)
    struct.pack_into('<Q',buf,16,80+rid)  # synthetic provenance id, NOT authenticated
    struct.pack_into('<Q',buf,24,1000000000+rid)
    struct.pack_into('<Q',buf,32,2000000000+rid)
    struct.pack_into('<I',buf,40,0)  # manifest model-table index
    struct.pack_into('<H',buf,44,1)  # kind
    struct.pack_into('<H',buf,46,0)
    struct.pack_into('<Q',buf,48,1)
    struct.pack_into('<Q',buf,56,seq)
    return bytes(buf)

def add(c,slot,rid,payload,gen=1,pinned=False,seq=1):
    if not isinstance(payload,bytes) or len(payload)>MAX_PAYLOAD:return 'INVALID_PAYLOAD'
    if not 0<=slot<MAX_SLOTS or gen<1 or gen>=2**32:return 'INVALID_SLOT'
    if c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records').fetchone()[0]>=MAX_SLOTS:return 'DENIED_STORAGE'
    c.execute('INSERT INTO records VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(
        slot,gen,rid,payload,hashlib.sha256(payload).digest(),header_bytes(slot,gen,rid,seq),
        bytes.fromhex(meta(c,'model')),1,3,7,seq,'lab_scope',int(pinned),int(meta(c,'boot')),int(meta(c,'consent'))))
    bump(c,'snapshot')
    return 'INSERTED'

def validate_record(r):
    slot,gen,rid,payload,sha,head,model,kind,source,schema,seq,scope,pinned,boot,consent=r
    if len(head)!=64 or len(payload)>MAX_PAYLOAD or len(sha)!=32 or len(model)!=32:return False
    if hashlib.sha256(payload).digest()!=sha:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<Q',head,0)[0]!=rid or struct.unpack_from('<I',head,8)[0]!=slot:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<I',head,12)[0]!=gen or struct.unpack_from('<Q',head,56)[0]!=seq:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<H',head,44)[0]!=kind:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<Q',head,16)[0]!=80+rid:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<Q',head,24)[0]!=1000000000+rid:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<Q',head,32)[0]!=2000000000+rid:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<I',head,40)[0]!=0:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<H',head,46)[0]!=0:return False
    if struct.unpack_from('<Q',head,48)[0]!=1:return False
    return True

ALLOWED={'record_id','kind','source_id','schema_id','source_sequence','time_scope','raw_literal'}
def validate_query(conditions):
    try:blob=json.dumps(conditions,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False).encode()
    except (ValueError,TypeError):return False
    if not isinstance(conditions,list) or len(conditions)>MAX_CONDITIONS or len(blob)>MAX_QUERY_BYTES:return False
    for d in conditions:
        if not isinstance(d,dict) or set(d)!={'field','op','value'} or d['field'] not in ALLOWED:return False
        fld,op,val=d['field'],d['op'],d['value']
        if fld=='raw_literal':
            if op!='contains' or not isinstance(val,str) or len(val.encode())>256:return False
        elif op=='eq':
            if not isinstance(val,(str,int)) or isinstance(val,bool):return False
        elif op=='in':
            if not isinstance(val,list) or not val or len(val)>64 or any(not isinstance(v,(str,int)) or isinstance(v,bool) for v in val):return False
        elif op=='range':
            if not isinstance(val,list) or len(val)!=2 or any(not isinstance(v,int) or isinstance(v,bool) for v in val):return False
            if val[0]>val[1]:return False
        else:return False
    return True

def cond(fld,op,val):return {'field':fld,'op':op,'value':val}
def row_matches(r,query):
    keys={'record_id':2,'kind':7,'source_id':8,'schema_id':9,'source_sequence':10,'time_scope':11}
    for item in query:
        fld,op,wanted=item['field'],item['op'],item['value']
        if fld=='raw_literal':
            if wanted.encode('utf8') not in r[3]:return False
        else:
            actual=r[keys[fld]]
            if op=='eq' and actual!=wanted:return False
            if op=='in' and actual not in wanted:return False
            if op=='range' and not wanted[0]<=actual<=wanted[1]:return False
    return True

def cursor_stamp(c,q):
    encoded=json.dumps(q,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False).encode()
    return {'query':hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest(),'snapshot':meta(c,'snapshot'),
            'boot':meta(c,'boot'),'consent':meta(c,'consent'),'scope':'lab_scope','model':meta(c,'model')}

def search_exact(c,q,k=MAX_SLOTS,cursor=None):
    if not validate_query(q) or not isinstance(k,int) or k<1 or k>MAX_SLOTS:
        return {'status':'INVALID_QUERY','matches':0,'scanned':0,'items':[],'cursor':None}
    mark=cursor_stamp(c,q)
    if cursor is not None:
        if (not isinstance(cursor,dict) or any(cursor.get(a)!=b for a,b in mark.items())
                or not isinstance(cursor.get('next_slot'),int)
                or not isinstance(cursor.get('deadline_ns'),int)
                or time.monotonic_ns()>cursor['deadline_ns']):
            return {'status':'INVALID_CURSOR','matches':0,'scanned':0,'items':[],'cursor':None}
    if meta(c,'consent_granted')!='1':
        return {'status':'DENIED_CONSENT','matches':0,'scanned':0,'items':[],'cursor':None}
    start_slot=cursor['next_slot'] if cursor else 0
    rows=c.execute('SELECT * FROM records WHERE slot>=? ORDER BY slot,generation',(start_slot,)).fetchall()
    matches=[];in_quarantine=False
    for r in rows:
        if not validate_record(r):
            return {'status':'INTEGRITY_ERROR','matches':0,'scanned':len(rows),'items':[],'cursor':None}
        if not row_matches(r,q):continue
        if r[6]!=bytes.fromhex(meta(c,'model')) or r[13]!=int(meta(c,'boot')) or r[14]!=int(meta(c,'consent')):
            in_quarantine=True;continue
        matches.append({'slot':r[0],'generation':r[1],'record_id':r[2],'payload':r[3]})
    if in_quarantine:
        return {'status':'QUARANTINE','matches':0,'scanned':len(rows),'items':[],'cursor':None}
    ret=matches[:k]
    clipped=len(matches)>k
    nextc={**mark,'next_slot':ret[-1]['slot']+1,
           'deadline_ns':cursor['deadline_ns'] if cursor else time.monotonic_ns()+5_000_000_000} if clipped else None
    return {'status':'TRUNCATED' if clipped else 'COMPLETE','matches':len(matches),'scanned':len(rows),'items':ret,'cursor':nextc}

def read_ref(c,slot,gen):
    r=c.execute('SELECT * FROM records WHERE slot=?',(slot,)).fetchone()
    if not r:return 'NOT_FOUND'
    if r[1]!=gen:return 'STALE_GENERATION'
    if not validate_record(r):return 'INTEGRITY_ERROR'
    if r[6]!=bytes.fromhex(meta(c,'model')) or r[13]!=int(meta(c,'boot')):return 'QUARANTINE'
    if meta(c,'consent_granted')!='1' or r[14]!=int(meta(c,'consent')):return 'DENIED_CONSENT'
    return 'EXACT_BYTES'

def crash_writer(path,ready):
    c=None
    try:
        c=connect(path);c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        result=add(c,0,991,b'uncommitted synthetic payload')
        ready.put(result,timeout=4)
        time.sleep(25)
    finally:
        if c:c.close()

def do_case(mode,case_dir,ctx):
    case_dir.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
    db=case_dir/'exact_store.sqlite3';c=init_store(db)
    q=[cond('schema_id','eq',7)]
    metric='none'
    try:
        if mode=='EXACT_BYTE_MATCH':
            assert add(c,0,100,b'synthetic bolt=17; state=TRUE')=='INSERTED'
            assert add(c,1,101,b'synthetic bolt=18; state=FALSE')=='INSERTED'
            a=search_exact(c,[cond('schema_id','eq',7),cond('raw_literal','contains','bolt=17')])
            ok=a['status']=='COMPLETE' and a['matches']==1 and a['items'][0]['payload']==b'synthetic bolt=17; state=TRUE'
            metric='exact_payload_sha=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='NEAR_TEXT_NOT_EXACT':
            add(c,0,100,b'valve 123 CLOSED')
            a=search_exact(c,[cond('raw_literal','contains','valve 124')]);ok=a['status']=='COMPLETE' and a['matches']==0
            metric='similar_text_did_not_match=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='CORRUPT_PAYLOAD_UNAVAILABLE':
            add(c,0,100,b'authentic synthetic bytes')
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET payload=? WHERE slot=0',(b'corrupt synthetic bytes',))
            a=search_exact(c,q);ok=a['status']=='INTEGRITY_ERROR' and read_ref(c,0,1)=='INTEGRITY_ERROR'
            metric='corrupt_sha_rejected=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='CORRUPT_HEADER_UNAVAILABLE':
            add(c,0,100,b'header checksum still good')
            row=c.execute('SELECT header FROM records WHERE slot=0').fetchone()[0]
            changed=bytearray(row);changed[12]^=1
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET header=? WHERE slot=0',(bytes(changed),))
            a=search_exact(c,q);ok=a['status']=='INTEGRITY_ERROR' and read_ref(c,0,1)=='INTEGRITY_ERROR'
            metric='corrupt_header_rejected=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='MODEL_VERSION_QUARANTINE':
            add(c,0,100,b'model A memory')
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET val=? WHERE name=?',(MODEL_B.hex(),'model'))
            a=search_exact(c,q);ok=a['status']=='QUARANTINE' and read_ref(c,0,1)=='QUARANTINE'
            metric='old_model_quarantined=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='BOOT_EPOCH_QUARANTINE':
            add(c,0,100,b'boot one timestamp')
            bump(c,'boot')
            a=search_exact(c,q);ok=a['status']=='QUARANTINE' and read_ref(c,0,1)=='QUARANTINE'
            metric='cross_boot_time_not_compared=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='STALE_SLOT_GENERATION':
            add(c,0,100,b'generation one',gen=1)
            c.execute('DELETE FROM records WHERE slot=0');bump(c,'snapshot')
            add(c,0,101,b'generation two',gen=2)
            ok=read_ref(c,0,1)=='STALE_GENERATION' and read_ref(c,0,2)=='EXACT_BYTES'
            metric='stale_ref_denied=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='STABLE_PAGE_WALK':
            for i in range(4):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            seen=[];cur=None;steps=0;scanned_total=0
            while True:
                a=search_exact(c,q,k=1,cursor=cur);steps+=1;scanned_total+=a['scanned']
                assert a['status'] in ('COMPLETE','TRUNCATED')
                seen.extend((it['slot'],it['generation']) for it in a['items'])
                cur=a['cursor']
                if cur is None:break
                if steps>5:raise AssertionError('PAGINATION_LOOP')
            ok=seen==[(i,1) for i in range(4)] and steps==4
            metric=f'pages={steps} refs={len(seen)} scanned_total={scanned_total}'
        elif mode=='CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_MUTATION':
            for i in range(2):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            a=search_exact(c,q,k=1);add(c,2,102,b'mutated snapshot')
            b=search_exact(c,q,k=1,cursor=a['cursor'])
            ok=a['status']=='TRUNCATED' and b['status']=='INVALID_CURSOR'
            metric='snapshot_mutation_invalidated_cursor=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_CONSENT':
            for i in range(2):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            a=search_exact(c,q,k=1);bump(c,'consent')
            b=search_exact(c,q,k=1,cursor=a['cursor'])
            ok=a['status']=='TRUNCATED' and b['status']=='INVALID_CURSOR'
            metric='consent_epoch_invalidated_cursor=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_QUERY_CHANGE':
            for i in range(2):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            a=search_exact(c,q,k=1)
            b=search_exact(c,[cond('kind','eq',1)],k=1,cursor=a['cursor'])
            ok=a['status']=='TRUNCATED' and b['status']=='INVALID_CURSOR'
            metric='changed_query_invalidated_cursor=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_MODEL_CHANGE':
            for i in range(2):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            a=search_exact(c,q,k=1)
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET val=? WHERE name=?',(MODEL_B.hex(),'model'))
            b=search_exact(c,q,k=1,cursor=a['cursor'])
            ok=a['status']=='TRUNCATED' and b['status']=='INVALID_CURSOR'
            metric='model_digest_invalidated_cursor=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='CURSOR_EXPIRED_DEADLINE':
            for i in range(2):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            a=search_exact(c,q,k=1);expired=dict(a['cursor']);expired['deadline_ns']=time.monotonic_ns()-1
            b=search_exact(c,q,k=1,cursor=expired)
            ok=a['status']=='TRUNCATED' and b['status']=='INVALID_CURSOR'
            metric='expired_cursor_denied=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='TRUNCATED_REQUIRES_FULL_SCAN':
            for i in range(4):add(c,i,100+i,f'payload-{i}'.encode())
            a=search_exact(c,q,k=1)
            ok=a['status']=='TRUNCATED' and a['matches']==4 and a['scanned']==4 and len(a['items'])==1
            metric=f'k=1 total_matched_lower_bound={a["matches"]} scanned={a["scanned"]}'
        elif mode=='ALL_PINNED_DENIED_STORAGE':
            for i in range(4):assert add(c,i,100+i,f'pinned-{i}'.encode(),pinned=True)=='INSERTED'
            before=int(meta(c,'snapshot'))
            verdict=add(c,0,999,b'eviction forbidden')
            ok=verdict=='DENIED_STORAGE' and int(meta(c,'snapshot'))==before and c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records').fetchone()[0]==4
            metric='all_pinned_fifth_insert=DENIED_STORAGE'
        elif mode=='CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT':
            c.close();ready=ctx.Queue();p=ctx.Process(target=crash_writer,args=(str(db),ready));p.start()
            try:
                status=ready.get(timeout=8)

                p.kill();p.join(timeout=5)
                if p.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('CHILD_NOT_KILLED')
            finally:
                if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(timeout=2)
                ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
            c=connect(db)
            absent=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records').fetchone()[0]==0
            c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');ins=add(c,0,992,b'committed synthetic payload');c.execute('COMMIT')

            ok=absent and ins=='INSERTED' and visible and read_ref(c,0,1)=='EXACT_BYTES'
            metric='precommit_crash_rolled_back=YES committed_visible=YES' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='INVALID_QUERY_REJECTED':
            add(c,0,100,b'benign')
            forms=[q*17,[cond('raw_literal','contains','x'*257)],
                   [cond('not_in_schema','eq',1)],[cond('raw_literal','contains','x'*255),cond('raw_literal','contains','y'*255)]*8]
            ok=all(search_exact(c,x)['status']=='INVALID_QUERY' for x in forms)
            metric='invalid_query_variants=4' if ok else 'bad'
        elif mode=='REVOKED_CONSENT_DENIES':
            add(c,0,100,b'private synthetic string')
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET val=? WHERE name=?',('0','consent_granted'))
            a=search_exact(c,q);ok=a['status']=='DENIED_CONSENT' and read_ref(c,0,1)=='DENIED_CONSENT'
            metric='post_revoke_access=DENIED' if ok else 'bad'
        else:raise RuntimeError('UNKNOWN_MODE')
        integrity=c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
        if integrity!='ok':ok=False
        return bool(ok),metric,integrity
    finally:c.close()

def stage(log,ctx,binary,root,workers,smoke,inject=False):
    tag=f'S5x{workers}_RAM32';cancel=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();out=ctx.Queue();rr=ctx.Queue()
    children=[];holder=None;complete=0;errors=0;worker_ok=0;reason=None;elapsed=[]
    repmax=1 if smoke else 2;expected=len(MODES)*repmax
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=ram_holder,args=(cancel,rr,32));holder.start()
        if rr.get(timeout=9) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(workers):
            p=ctx.Process(target=s5_worker,args=(binary,cancel,ready,out));p.start();children.append(p)
        if len({ready.get(timeout=30) for _ in children})!=workers:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_WORKERS_NOT_READY')
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={tag} processes={workers}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={tag} modes={len(MODES)} repeats={repmax} cases={expected} touched_RAM_MiB=32')
        for rep in range(repmax):
            for j,mode in enumerate(MODES):
                if why:=safety(smoke):reason=why;break
                if inject and complete>=3:reason='INJECTED_HOST_ONLY';break
                t0=time.monotonic()
                ok,detail,integrity=do_case(mode,root/tag/f'{rep+1:02d}_{j+1:02d}_{mode}',ctx)
                ms=(time.monotonic()-t0)*1000;elapsed.append(ms);complete+=1
                if not ok:errors+=1
                log(f'CASE stage={tag} rep={rep+1} mode={mode} status={"PASS" if ok else "FAIL"} '
                    f'fixture={detail} sqlite_integrity={integrity} elapsed_ms={ms:.3f}')
            if reason:break
        floor=2 if smoke else (24 if workers==2 else 30)
        while not reason and time.monotonic()-start<floor:
            if why:=safety(smoke):reason=why;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as e:
        errors+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={tag} {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:280]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for p in children:
            p.join(timeout=4)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(timeout=3)
        if holder:
            holder.join(timeout=3)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(timeout=3)
        for _ in children:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,minimum,errs=out.get(timeout=2)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean:.5f} '
                    f'min_GMAC_s={minimum:.5f} errors={errs}')
                if runs>0 and not errs:worker_ok+=1
                else:errors+=1
            except queue.Empty:
                errors+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} REPORT_MISSING')
        for item in (ready,out,rr):
            item.cancel_join_thread();item.close()
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if reason else ('COMPLETE' if complete==expected and errors==0 and worker_ok==workers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={tag} status={status} cases={complete}/{expected} failures={errors} '
        f'workers_ok={worker_ok}/{workers} case_max_ms={max(elapsed) if elapsed else None} safety_reason={reason or "-"}')
    b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={tag} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return status,complete

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='Linux only; bypass A52 telemetry checks')
    ap.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true',help='Use only with --host-smoke')
    opts=ap.parse_args()
    if opts.inject_host_abort and not opts.host_smoke:raise SystemExit('INJECT_HOST_ABORT_ONLY_WITH_HOST_SMOKE')
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_EXACT_MEMORY_INTEGRITY_V14_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(msg):print(msg,flush=True);f.write(msg+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- EXACT-BYTE MEMORY, GENERATION, QUARANTINE, PAGING AND CRASH LAB '+VERSION)
            log('Synthetic SQLite WAL FULL memory; fixed 64-byte header, SHA-256 payload, model digest, boot and consent epochs.')
            log('Exact query restricted subset; k limits returned refs, not full scan. 4 slots fixture, normative full store up to 4096.')
            log('Native S5 continuous background CPU load: 2/4 real C256R32 workers + 32MiB RAM; no idle stage.')
            log('LIMIT: SHA-256 detects accidental corruption, NOT malicious same-UID forgery. Cursor and consent are synthetic, not cryptographic authentication.')
            log('LIMIT: no real owner private data, actual model memory, embeddings, P training, Android authority, system actions or power-loss test.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE; PRODUCTION_CODE_AUTHORIZED = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            if why:=safety(opts.host_smoke):log(f'RESULT SAFETY_SKIP {why}');return 3
            binary=native_binary();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_S5 '+str(binary))
            root=home/'uno_a52_r41_exact_memory_v14'/stamp
            root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            # Tiny self-test before long-running stages; no external data.
            c=init_store(root/'selftest.sqlite3')
            assert add(c,0,777,b'self-test bytes')=='INSERTED'
            assert search_exact(c,[cond('raw_literal','contains','self-test')])['matches']==1
            c.close();log('SELFTEST_PASS header=64 payload_sha=YES exact_query=YES')
            a=stage(log,ctx,binary,root,2 if not opts.host_smoke else 1,opts.host_smoke,opts.inject_host_abort)
            if a[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {a[0]} first_stage_cases={a[1]}');return 3
            b=stage(log,ctx,binary,root,4 if not opts.host_smoke else 2,opts.host_smoke)
            if b[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {b[0]} second_stage_cases={b[1]}');return 3
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_EXACT_MEMORY_INTEGRITY_LAB cases={a[1]+b[1]} stages=2/2')
            log('LIMIT: scoped exact-byte fixture and cooperative SQLite are not a secure Android memory authority or semantic recall proof.')
            return 0
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:400]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc()[-1000:]);return 2
        finally:log('REPORT '+str(report))

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3

    'MISSING_RNG_RESET','CROSS_BOOT_TIME_QUARANTINE','REVOKED_CONSENT_DENIED',
    'KNOWN_BACKUP_EXACT_BYTES','NO_BACKUP_NO_BYTE_GUESS','SIMILAR_SUMMARY_NOT_EXACT',
    'CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT_ALL_OR_NOTHING','CRASH_AFTER_COMMIT_ALL_VISIBLE',
    'PREPARED_UNVERIFIED_REMAINS_UNKNOWN','DUPLICATE_RECEIPT_DENIED',
    'SAME_UID_FORGED_CHAIN_NEGATIVE',
)
C_SOURCE=r'''
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <math.h>
#define D 256
#define R 32
#define B 32
#define Q 2
#define MAC_PER_BLOCK (2*D*R+B*R)
static float A[8][R][D], C[8][R][D], W[8][B][R];
static uint32_t prng=0x79b43ad1u;
static uint32_t x32(void){prng^=prng<<13; prng^=prng>>17; prng^=prng<<5;return prng;}
static float sample(void){return ((float)(x32()%20001)/10000.f-1.f)*.012f;}
static void init(void){
 for(int j=0;j<8;j++){
  for(int r=0;r<R;r++)for(int i=0;i<D;i++){A[j][r][i]=sample();C[j][r][i]=sample();}
  for(int b=0;b<B;b++)for(int r=0;r<R;r++)W[j][b][r]=sample();
 }
}
static uint64_t fnv_bytes(uint64_t h,const void *data,size_t n){
 const unsigned char *p=(const unsigned char *)data;
 for(size_t i=0;i<n;i++){h^=p[i];h*=1099511628211ull;}
 return h;
}
int main(int argc,char **argv){
 if(argc!=3){fprintf(stderr,"usage: native_fixture event_file seed\n");return 2;}
 char *end=0;unsigned long seed=strtoul(argv[2],&end,10);
 if(!end||*end||seed==0||seed>0xfffffffful)return 2;
 FILE *f=fopen(argv[1],"rb");if(!f){perror("open");return 2;}
 init();float h[D],z[D],next[D],a[R],c[R];
 for(int i=0;i<D;i++)h[i]=.01f*(float)((i%13)-6)+.00001f*(float)(seed%997u);
 unsigned long long mac=0;int n=0;unsigned char buf[8];
 while(fread(buf,1,8,f)==8){
  if(++n>1024){fclose(f);return 4;}
  int32_t x,y;memcpy(&x,buf,4);memcpy(&y,buf+4,4);
  for(int i=0;i<D;i++)z[i]=.001f*(float)((x%211)+(i%11)-(y%101));
  memcpy(next,h,sizeof(h));
  for(int t=0;t<Q;t++){
   int j=(n-1+t)%8;
   for(int r=0;r<R;r++){
    float sa=0,sc=0;
    for(int i=0;i<D;i++){sa+=A[j][r][i]*h[i];sc+=C[j][r][i]*z[i];}
    a[r]=sa;c[r]=sc;
   }
   for(int b=0;b<B;b++){
    float s=0;for(int r=0;r<R;r++)s+=W[j][b][r]*tanhf(a[r]+c[r]);
    next[j*B+b]=.75f*h[j*B+b]+.25f*tanhf(s);
   }
   mac+=MAC_PER_BLOCK;
  }
  memcpy(h,next,sizeof(h));
 }
 int remainder=!feof(f);fclose(f);
 if(remainder||n==0){fprintf(stderr,"INVALID_EVENT_DATA\n");return 4;}
 uint64_t dig=fnv_bytes(1469598103934665603ull,h,sizeof(h));
 printf("NATIVE_REPLAY events=%d blocks=%d actual_F_MAC=%llu state_digest=%016llx status=PASS\n",n,n*Q,mac,(unsigned long long)dig);
 return 0;
}
'''

def number(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try: mem=int(next(line for line in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,ValueError,StopIteration,IndexError):mem=None
    return number('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),number('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),mem

def safety(host):
    if host:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'DEVICE_TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'BATTERY_LT_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'BATTERY_T_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def background_s5():
    p=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_MISSING '+str(p))
    r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('S5_PRECHECK_FAIL '+r.stdout[-180:])
    return p

def s5_worker(binary,cancel,ready,out):
    samples=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not cancel.is_set():
            r=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
            if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):raise RuntimeError('S5_NATIVE_FAILED')
            match=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',r.stdout)
            if not match:raise RuntimeError('S5_METRIC_MISSING')
            samples.append(float(match[1]))
            if len(samples)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=3)
    except BaseException as exc:errors.append(str(exc)[:160])
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(samples),statistics.mean(samples) if samples else 0.,min(samples) if samples else 0.,errors),timeout=3)
    except BaseException:pass

def ram_worker(cancel,ready):
    try:
        b=bytearray(32*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(b),4096):b[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=4)
        while not cancel.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(b),262144):b[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=3)
        except BaseException:pass

def compile_native(root):
    src=root/'v15_replay.c';exe=root/'v15_replay'
    src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    r=subprocess.run(['clang','-std=c11','-O2',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=45)
    if r.returncode:raise RuntimeError('CLANG_FAILED '+r.stderr[-500:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def db_connect(db):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(db),timeout=7,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000')
    return c

def create_db(db):
    c=db_connect(db)
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE metadata (k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE events (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, payload BLOB NOT NULL, payload_sha BLOB NOT NULL, prev BLOB NOT NULL, digest BLOB NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE snapshot (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), state_digest TEXT NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE dispatch (tx TEXT PRIMARY KEY, status TEXT NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE used (tx TEXT PRIMARY KEY)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE receipt (tx TEXT PRIMARY KEY, bytes BLOB NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE memory_ref (tx TEXT PRIMARY KEY, slot INTEGER NOT NULL, generation INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE p_snapshot (tx TEXT PRIMARY KEY, data BLOB NOT NULL)')
    for k,v in [('model',MODEL_A),('dsp',DSP_A),('rng',str(SEED)),('boot','1'),('consent','1'),('tip',bytes(32).hex()),('event_count','0')]:
        c.execute('INSERT INTO metadata VALUES (?,?)',(k,v))
    return c

def meta(c,key):return c.execute('SELECT v FROM metadata WHERE k=?',(key,)).fetchone()
def set_meta(c,key,value):c.execute('UPDATE metadata SET v=? WHERE k=?',(str(value),key))

def append_event(c,seq,payload):
    prev=bytes.fromhex(meta(c,'tip')[0])
    dh=hashlib.sha256(prev+struct.pack('<Q',seq)+payload).digest()
    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
    try:
        c.execute('INSERT INTO events VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)',(seq,payload,hashlib.sha256(payload).digest(),prev,dh))
        set_meta(c,'tip',dh.hex());set_meta(c,'event_count',seq)
        c.execute('COMMIT')
    except BaseException:c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise

def load_events(c):
    rows=c.execute('SELECT seq,payload,payload_sha,prev,digest FROM events ORDER BY seq').fetchall()
    if not rows or len(rows)!=int(meta(c,'event_count')[0]):return None,'MISSING_OR_GAPPED_EVENTS'
    prev=bytes(32)
    for expected,(seq,payload,payload_sha,seen_prev,seen_digest) in enumerate(rows,1):
        if seq!=expected or not isinstance(payload,bytes) or len(payload)!=8 or hashlib.sha256(payload).digest()!=payload_sha:
            return None,'INTEGRITY_OR_SEQUENCE_MISMATCH'
        calc=hashlib.sha256(prev+struct.pack('<Q',seq)+payload).digest()
        if seen_prev!=prev or seen_digest!=calc:return None,'BROKEN_CHAIN'
        prev=calc
    if meta(c,'tip')[0]!=prev.hex():return None,'TIP_MISMATCH'
    return b''.join(row[1] for row in rows),'OK'

def native_replay(exe,case_folder,data,seed):
    source=case_folder/'events.bin'
    source.write_bytes(data)
    r=subprocess.run([str(exe),str(source),str(seed)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if r.returncode:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_REPLAY_ERROR '+r.stderr[-250:]+r.stdout[-250:])
    line=r.stdout.strip()
    if 'status=PASS' not in line or f'events={len(data)//8}' not in line:
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_REPLAY_CONTRACT_FAIL '+line)
    mm=re.search(r'state_digest=([a-f0-9]{16})',line)
    mac=re.search(r'actual_F_MAC=(\d+)',line)
    if not mm or not mac or int(mac[1]) != len(data)//8*2*(2*256*32+32*32):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_REPLAY_MAC_LEDGER_FAIL '+line)
    return mm[1],int(mac[1])

def recover(c,exe,case_folder,expected_model=MODEL_A,expected_dsp=DSP_A,expected_boot='1'):
    if meta(c,'consent')[0]!='1':return 'DENIED_CONSENT',None,0
    if meta(c,'model')[0]!=expected_model or meta(c,'dsp')[0]!=expected_dsp:return 'RESET_UNKNOWN_MANIFEST',None,0
    if meta(c,'boot')[0]!=expected_boot:return 'QUARANTINE_BOOT',None,0
    seedrow=meta(c,'rng')
    if not seedrow:return 'RESET_UNKNOWN_RNG',None,0
    try:seed=int(seedrow[0]);assert 0<seed<2**32
    except (ValueError,AssertionError):return 'RESET_UNKNOWN_RNG',None,0
    data,status=load_events(c)
    if status!='OK':return 'UNKNOWN_LEDGER',None,0
    digest,mac=native_replay(exe,case_folder,data,seed)
    return 'REPLAYED',digest,mac

def populate(c):
    for seq in range(1,N_EVENTS+1):append_event(c,seq,struct.pack('<ii',seq*31-100,seq*17+9))

def child_tx(db,ready,when):
    c=db_connect(db)
    try:
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        c.execute('INSERT INTO used VALUES (?)',('tx-alpha',))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO receipt VALUES (?,?)',('tx-alpha',b'synthetic native outcome'))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO memory_ref VALUES (?,?,?)',('tx-alpha',2,5))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO p_snapshot VALUES (?,?)',('tx-alpha',b'PREBUILT_STATIC_BYTES_NO_LEARNING'))
        if when=='POST':c.execute('COMMIT')
        ready.put('STAGED' if when=='PRE' else 'COMMITTED',timeout=5)
        time.sleep(8) # parent kills process to simulate isolated writer crash
    finally:c.close()

def tx_counts(c):
    return [c.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}').fetchone()[0] for table in ('used','receipt','memory_ref','p_snapshot')]

def case_run(mode,root,exe,ctx):
    root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False);db=root/'events.sqlite3';c=create_db(db)
    negative=False
    try:
        populate(c)
        baseline,digest0,mac0=recover(c,exe,root)
        if baseline!='REPLAYED' or not digest0 or mac0!=N_EVENTS*2*17408:raise RuntimeError('BASELINE_REPLAY_INVALID')
        if mode=='AUTHENTIC_EVENT_REPLAY':
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='REPLAYED' and dig==digest0 and mac==mac0
            detail=f'byte_equal_state_digest={int(dig==digest0)} events={N_EVENTS} native_F_MAC={mac}'
        elif mode=='LOST_SNAPSHOT_REPLAY':
            c.execute('INSERT INTO snapshot VALUES (1,?)',(digest0,));c.execute('DELETE FROM snapshot')
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='REPLAYED' and dig==digest0 and mac==mac0
            detail='snapshot_absent_authentic_replay=YES'
        elif mode=='ALTERED_SNAPSHOT_RECOMPUTED':
            c.execute('INSERT INTO snapshot VALUES (1,?)',('0000000000000000',))
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='REPLAYED' and dig==digest0 and dig!='0000000000000000'
            detail='stale_snapshot_ignored_native_replay=YES'
        elif mode=='EMPTY_HISTORY_UNKNOWN':
            c.execute('DELETE FROM events');set_meta(c,'tip',bytes(32).hex());set_meta(c,'event_count','0')
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='UNKNOWN_LEDGER' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='missing_events_not_invented=YES'
        elif mode=='PAYLOAD_CORRUPT_UNKNOWN':
            c.execute('UPDATE events SET payload=? WHERE seq=2',(b'changed!',))
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='UNKNOWN_LEDGER' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='payload_corruption_rejected_before_F=YES'
        elif mode=='SEQUENCE_GAP_UNKNOWN':
            c.execute('DELETE FROM events WHERE seq=3')
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='UNKNOWN_LEDGER' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='missing_sequence_rejected=YES'
        elif mode=='SEQUENCE_REORDER_UNKNOWN':
            c.execute('UPDATE events SET seq=99 WHERE seq=1')
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='UNKNOWN_LEDGER' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='reordered_sequence_rejected=YES'
        elif mode=='FOREIGN_MODEL_RESET':
            set_meta(c,'model',MODEL_B)
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='RESET_UNKNOWN_MANIFEST' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='different_model_no_old_hidden_state_replay=YES'
        elif mode=='FOREIGN_DSP_RESET':
            set_meta(c,'dsp',DSP_B)
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='RESET_UNKNOWN_MANIFEST' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='different_DSP_no_state_replay=YES'
        elif mode=='MISSING_RNG_RESET':
            c.execute('DELETE FROM metadata WHERE k=?',('rng',))
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='RESET_UNKNOWN_RNG' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='missing_PRNG_seed_no_replay=YES'
        elif mode=='CROSS_BOOT_TIME_QUARANTINE':
            set_meta(c,'boot','2')
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='QUARANTINE_BOOT' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='old_monotonic_time_not_compared=YES'
        elif mode=='REVOKED_CONSENT_DENIED':
            set_meta(c,'consent','0')
            state,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=state=='DENIED_CONSENT' and dig is None and mac==0
            detail='policy_denies_replay=YES'
        elif mode=='KNOWN_BACKUP_EXACT_BYTES':
            original=b'RAW EXACT \x00 \xff private-lab-bytes';bak=root/'known_backing.bin';bak.write_bytes(original)
            expected=hashlib.sha256(original).digest();stored=bytearray(original);stored[3]^=0x18
            recovered=bak.read_bytes() if hashlib.sha256(bak.read_bytes()).digest()==expected else None
            ok=hashlib.sha256(stored).digest()!=expected and recovered==original
            detail='source_backing_bytes_match_original=YES'
        elif mode=='NO_BACKUP_NO_BYTE_GUESS':
            source=b'private only original\x00\x01';hint=b'private only ...';recovered=None
            ok=source!=hint and recovered is None
            detail='lost_bytes_unavailable_not_guessed=YES'
        elif mode=='SIMILAR_SUMMARY_NOT_EXACT':
            source=b'OWNER SAID: schedule 14:00';summary=b'OWNER SAID: schedule around two'
            ok=source!=summary and hashlib.sha256(source).digest()!=hashlib.sha256(summary).digest()
            detail='semantic_summary_not_exact_source=YES'
        elif mode in ('CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT_ALL_OR_NOTHING','CRASH_AFTER_COMMIT_ALL_VISIBLE'):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO dispatch VALUES (?,?)',('tx-alpha','PREPARED'))
            c.close();c=None
            ready=ctx.Queue();when='PRE' if mode=='CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT_ALL_OR_NOTHING' else 'POST'
            p=ctx.Process(target=child_tx,args=(str(db),ready,when));p.start()
            try:
                got=ready.get(timeout=12)
                if got!=('STAGED' if when=='PRE' else 'COMMITTED'):raise RuntimeError('CRASH_CHILD_BAD_MARKER')
                p.kill();p.join(5)
                if p.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('CRASH_CHILD_STILL_ALIVE')
            finally:
                if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
                ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
            c=db_connect(db);counts=tx_counts(c)
            status=c.execute('SELECT status FROM dispatch WHERE tx=?',('tx-alpha',)).fetchone()[0]
            ok=counts==([0,0,0,0] if when=='PRE' else [1,1,1,1]) and status=='PREPARED'
            detail=f'crash_point={when} counts={counts} prepared_status={status}'
        elif mode=='PREPARED_UNVERIFIED_REMAINS_UNKNOWN':
            c.execute('INSERT INTO dispatch VALUES (?,?)',('tx-alpha','PREPARED'))
            row=c.execute('SELECT status FROM dispatch WHERE tx=?',('tx-alpha',)).fetchone()[0]
            counts=tx_counts(c)
            decision='MAY_HAVE_EXECUTED' if row=='PREPARED' and counts==[0,0,0,0] else 'INVALID'
            ok=decision=='MAY_HAVE_EXECUTED'
            detail='no_replay_or_false_FAILURE_status='+decision
        elif mode=='DUPLICATE_RECEIPT_DENIED':
            c.execute('INSERT INTO receipt VALUES (?,?)',('tx-alpha',b'first'))
            duplicate=False
            try:c.execute('INSERT INTO receipt VALUES (?,?)',('tx-alpha',b'second'))
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError:duplicate=True
            raw=c.execute('SELECT bytes FROM receipt WHERE tx=?',('tx-alpha',)).fetchone()[0]
            ok=duplicate and raw==b'first'
            detail='duplicate_receipt_uniqueness=YES'
        elif mode=='SAME_UID_FORGED_CHAIN_NEGATIVE':
            negative=True
            raw=bytearray(c.execute('SELECT payload FROM events WHERE seq=3').fetchone()[0]);raw[0]^=0x35
            c.execute('UPDATE events SET payload=? WHERE seq=3',(bytes(raw),))
            prev=bytes(32)
            for seq,payload in c.execute('SELECT seq,payload FROM events ORDER BY seq').fetchall():
                dh=hashlib.sha256(prev+struct.pack('<Q',seq)+payload).digest()
                c.execute('UPDATE events SET payload_sha=?, prev=?, digest=? WHERE seq=?',
                          (hashlib.sha256(payload).digest(),prev,dh,seq))
                prev=dh
            set_meta(c,'tip',prev.hex())
            status,dig,mac=recover(c,exe,root)
            ok=status=='REPLAYED' and dig!=digest0 and mac==mac0
            detail=f'forged_same_UID_chain_accepted=YES changed_state={int(dig!=digest0)}'
        else:raise RuntimeError('UNKNOWN_MODE')
        integrity=c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
        return bool(ok and integrity=='ok'),detail,integrity,negative
    finally:
        if c is not None:c.close()

def stage(log,ctx,background,exe,root,nworkers,host,inject_abort=False):
    tag=f'S5x{nworkers}_RAM32';cancel=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();res=ctx.Queue();rr=ctx.Queue()
    workers=[];holder=None;failures=0;complete=0;negative_findings=0;worker_ok=0;reason=None;elapsed=[]
    repeats=1 if host else 2;expected=len(MODES)*repeats;start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=ram_worker,args=(cancel,rr));holder.start()
        if rr.get(timeout=12) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_WORKER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            p=ctx.Process(target=s5_worker,args=(background,cancel,ready,res));p.start();workers.append(p)
        if len({ready.get(timeout=30) for _ in workers})!=nworkers:raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_NOT_READY')
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={tag} workers={nworkers}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={tag} modes={len(MODES)} repeats={repeats} cases={expected} RAM_MiB=32')
        for rep in range(repeats):
            for j,mode in enumerate(MODES):
                if reason:=safety(host):break
                if inject_abort and complete>=4:reason='INJECTED_HOST_TEST_ABORT';break
                t0=time.monotonic()
                ok,detail,integrity,negative=case_run(mode,root/tag/f'{rep+1:02d}_{j+1:02d}_{mode}',exe,ctx)
                ms=(time.monotonic()-t0)*1000;elapsed.append(ms);complete+=1
                if not ok:failures+=1
                if negative and ok:negative_findings+=1
                log(f'CASE stage={tag} rep={rep+1} mode={mode} status={"PASS_EXPECTED_NEGATIVE" if ok and negative else "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"} '
                    f'{detail} sqlite_integrity={integrity} elapsed_ms={ms:.3f}')
            if reason:break
        floor=2 if host else (24 if nworkers==2 else 30)
        while not reason and time.monotonic()-start<floor:
            if reason:=safety(host):break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as exc:
        failures+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={tag} {type(exc).__name__} {str(exc)[:250]}')
    finally:
        cancel.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
        if holder:
            holder.join(4)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(3)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,lowest,errs=res.get(timeout=3)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean:.5f} min_GMAC_s={lowest:.5f} errors={errs}')
                if runs>0 and not errs:worker_ok+=1
                else:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:failures+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} REPORT_MISSING')
        for qq in (ready,res,rr):qq.cancel_join_thread();qq.close()
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if reason else ('COMPLETE' if complete==expected and not failures and worker_ok==nworkers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={tag} status={status} cases={complete}/{expected} failures={failures} '
        f'negative_findings={negative_findings} workers_ok={worker_ok}/{nworkers} case_max_ms={max(elapsed) if elapsed else None} safety_reason={reason or "-"}')
    b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={tag} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return status,complete,negative_findings

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='Linux developer mode: sensor guards skipped')
    ap.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true',help='Only with --host-smoke')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    if args.inject_host_abort and not args.host_smoke:raise SystemExit('INJECTION_HOST_ONLY')
    home=Path.home();out=home/'storage'/'downloads'

def number(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:m=int(next(s for s in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):m=None
    return number('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),number('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),m

def safety(host):
    if host:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'SAFETY_TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'SAFETY_MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def native_s5():
    p=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_NOT_FOUND '+str(p))
    r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_PRECHECK_FAILED '+r.stdout[-200:])
    return p

def s5_worker(binary,stop,ready,out):
    values=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            x=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
            if x.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',x.stdout):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_WORKER_FAILED')
            v=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([0-9.]+)',x.stdout)
            if v is None:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_METRICS_MISSING')
            values.append(float(v[1]))
            if len(values)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=4)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:150])
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(values),statistics.mean(values) if values else 0.,min(values) if values else 0.,errors),timeout=5)
    except BaseException:pass

def ram_worker(stop,ready):
    try:
        b=bytearray(32*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(b),4096):b[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=4)
        while not stop.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(b),262144):b[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=3)
        except BaseException:pass

def compile_native(root):
    src=root/'v16_semantic.c';exe=root/'v16_semantic'
    src.write_text(C_SOURCE,encoding='utf-8')
    run=subprocess.run(['clang','-std=c11','-O2',str(src),'-lm','-o',str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=45)
    if run.returncode:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_CLANG_FAILED '+run.stderr[-600:])
    return exe,hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def conn(path):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),timeout=6,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL');c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL');c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000')
    return c

def half_vec(data):return struct.pack('<128e',*data)
def unpack_half(data):return list(struct.unpack('<128e',data))
def vec(first=0.,second=0.):
    out=[0.]*M;out[0]=float(first);out[1]=float(second);return out

def add(c,slot,kind='VERIFIED_COMPLETION',raw=b'verified-source',key=None,val=None,model=MODEL_A,boot=1,consent=1,gen=1,source_gen=1,expiry=10_000,scope='owner',trusted=1):
    if key is None:key=vec(0.)
    if val is None:val=vec(0.5)
    kb=half_vec(key);vb=half_vec(val)
    c.execute('INSERT INTO records VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
       (slot,gen,gen,kind,raw,hashlib.sha256(raw).digest(),kb,vb,model,boot,consent,source_gen,expiry,scope,trusted,17,77,42,1))

def db_init(path):
    c=conn(path)
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE records(slot INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, generation INTEGER NOT NULL, expected_generation INTEGER NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, raw BLOB NOT NULL, raw_sha BLOB NOT NULL, key_fp16 BLOB NOT NULL, value_fp16 BLOB NOT NULL, model TEXT NOT NULL, boot INTEGER NOT NULL, consent INTEGER NOT NULL, source_generation INTEGER NOT NULL, expiry INTEGER NOT NULL, scope TEXT NOT NULL, trusted_source INTEGER NOT NULL, source_id INTEGER NOT NULL, subject_id INTEGER NOT NULL, time_scope INTEGER NOT NULL, raw_available INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    return c

def evaluate_store(c,requested_model=MODEL_A,boot=1,consent=1,scope='owner',now=100):
    # Scan ALL known rows, not top-k; any corrupt accessible row fails closed.
    rows=c.execute('SELECT slot,generation,expected_generation,kind,raw,raw_sha,key_fp16,value_fp16,model,boot,consent,source_generation,expiry,scope,trusted_source,source_id,subject_id,time_scope,raw_available FROM records ORDER BY slot,generation').fetchall()
    if consent!=1:return 'DENIED',[],len(rows),[]
    allow=[];meta=[]
    for row in rows:
        (slot,gen,expected,kind,raw,sha,k,v,model,b,ce,source_gen,expiry,sc,trusted,source_id,subject,t_scope,raw_available)=row
        if sc!=scope or ce!=consent or model!=requested_model or b!=boot or expiry<=now:continue
        if gen!=expected or not isinstance(raw,bytes) or len(raw)>4096 or hashlib.sha256(raw).digest()!=sha or len(k)!=256 or len(v)!=256 or source_gen<=0:
            return 'UNKNOWN',[],len(rows),[]
        try:keys=unpack_half(k);vals=unpack_half(v)
        except (ValueError,struct.error,OverflowError):return 'UNKNOWN',[],len(rows),[]
        if not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in keys+vals):return 'UNKNOWN',[],len(rows),[]
        allow.append((slot,keys,vals));meta.append((slot,kind,raw,trusted,subject,t_scope,raw_available))
    return 'SUMMARY_ONLY',allow,len(rows),meta

def exact_query(c,k,requested_scope='owner',consent=1,now=100):
    # Minimal exact fixture with full scan; this is NOT a protected-source signature verifier.
    if consent!=1:return 'DENIED',[],0
    rows=c.execute('SELECT slot,raw,raw_sha,raw_available,scope,expiry,kind,subject_id,time_scope FROM records ORDER BY slot').fetchall()
    matches=[];claim_values={}
    for slot,raw,sha,available,scope,expiry,kind,subject,t_scope in rows:
        if scope!=requested_scope or expiry<=now:continue
        if not available or not isinstance(raw,bytes) or hashlib.sha256(raw).digest()!=sha:
            return 'UNKNOWN',[],len(rows)
        matches.append((slot,raw))
        if kind=='VERIFIED_COMPLETION':claim_values.setdefault((subject,t_scope),set()).add(raw)
    if len(matches)>k:return 'TRUNCATED',matches[:k],len(rows)
    if any(len(values)>1 for values in claim_values.values()):return 'CONFLICT',matches,len(rows)
    return 'COMPLETE',matches,len(rows)

def python_reference(q,z0,eligible,T=1.0):
    logits=[sum(q[i]*it[1][i] for i in range(M))/(math.sqrt(M)*T) for it in eligible]
    if logits:
        mm=max(logits);weights=[math.exp(x-mm) for x in logits];total=sum(weights)
        rr=[sum((weights[j]/total)*item[2][i] for j,item in enumerate(eligible)) for i in range(M)]
    else:rr=[0.]*M
    return rr,[math.tanh(z0[i]+rr[i]) for i in range(M)]

def native_eval(exe,folder,eligible,q,z0,T=1.0):
    if not 0<len(q)==len(z0)==M or not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in q+z0):raise ValueError('INVALID_QUERY_VECTOR')
    if len(eligible)>16:raise ValueError('OVER_LIMIT')
    file=folder/'semantic_half.bin'
    with file.open('wb') as f:
        f.write(b'S416'+bytes([len(eligible),1,0,0]));f.write(struct.pack('<f',T))
        f.write(struct.pack('<128f',*q));f.write(struct.pack('<128f',*z0))
        for _slot,k,v in eligible:f.write(half_vec(k))
        for _slot,k,v in eligible:f.write(half_vec(v))
    p=subprocess.run([str(exe),str(file)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=15)
    if p.returncode or 'status=PASS' not in p.stdout:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_SEMANTIC_FAILED '+p.stderr[-400:]+p.stdout[-150:])
    lines=p.stdout.strip().splitlines()
    if len(lines)!=2 or not lines[1].startswith('R='):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_OUTPUT_FORMAT')
    got=[float(x) for x in lines[1][2:].split(',')]
    if len(got)!=M:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_OUTPUT_LENGTH')
    ref,_=python_reference(q,z0,eligible,T)
    maximum=max(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(got,ref))
    if maximum>1e-4 or not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in got):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_PYTHON_DIVERGENCE '+str(maximum))
    match=re.search(r'F_MAC=(\d+)\b.*state_digest=([0-9a-f]{16})',lines[0])
    if not match or int(match[1])!=34816:raise RuntimeError('F_MAC_ACCOUNT_MISMATCH '+lines[0])
    return got,match[2],maximum,lines[0]

def case_run(mode,folder,exe):
    folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
    c=db_init(folder/'semantic.sqlite3');negative=False
    q=vec();z0=vec(.25,-.3)
    try:
        if mode in ('C02_OPPOSITE_VALUES_COLLIDE','CONFLICT_NOT_RESOLVED_BY_SUMMARY','RECORD_ORDER_INVARIANCE'):
            # Identical keys; opposite values and independent source identities.
            add(c,2,raw=b'source-A-true',val=vec(1.,.25))
            add(c,1,raw=b'source-B-false',val=vec(-1.,-.25))
        elif mode=='STABLE_LARGE_LOGITS':
            add(c,1,key=vec(100.),val=vec(.75));add(c,2,key=vec(-100.),val=vec(-.75));q=vec(100.)
        elif mode=='K1_EXACT_TRUNCATION':
            for i in range(1,5):add(c,i,raw=('source-%d'%i).encode(),val=vec(i/8.))
        else:add(c,1)
        if mode=='EMPTY_ELIGIBLE_SET_ZERO':c.execute('DELETE FROM records')
        elif mode=='MODEL_MISMATCH_QUARANTINE':c.execute('UPDATE records SET model=?',(MODEL_B,))
        elif mode=='BOOT_MISMATCH_QUARANTINE':c.execute('UPDATE records SET boot=2')
        elif mode=='CONSENT_REVOKE_DENIES':pass
        elif mode=='PAYLOAD_INTEGRITY_UNKNOWN':c.execute('UPDATE records SET raw=? WHERE slot=1',(b'CORRUPTED',))
        elif mode=='DECLARATION_NOT_EVIDENCE':c.execute("UPDATE records SET kind='USER_DECLARATION' WHERE slot=1")
        elif mode=='HYPOTHESIS_NOT_EVIDENCE':c.execute("UPDATE records SET kind='HYPOTHESIS' WHERE slot=1")
        elif mode=='STALE_SLOT_GENERATION_UNKNOWN':c.execute('UPDATE records SET expected_generation=2 WHERE slot=1')
        elif mode=='FP16_PAYLOAD_ROUNDTRIP':c.execute('UPDATE records SET value_fp16=? WHERE slot=1',(half_vec(vec(.3333)),))
        elif mode=='EXACT_RAW_BYTES_SEPARATE':c.execute('UPDATE records SET raw=?,raw_sha=? WHERE slot=1',(b'\x00exact\xffbytes',hashlib.sha256(b'\x00exact\xffbytes').digest()))
        elif mode=='MISSING_RAW_NOT_RECONSTRUCTED':c.execute('UPDATE records SET raw=?,raw_sha=?,raw_available=0 WHERE slot=1',(b'',hashlib.sha256(b'').digest()))
        elif mode=='SAME_UID_SEMANTIC_POISON_NEGATIVE':
            forged=b'forged-same-UID'
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET raw=?,raw_sha=?,value_fp16=? WHERE slot=1',(forged,hashlib.sha256(forged).digest(),half_vec(vec(-.875))))
            negative=True
        status,eligible,scanned,meta=evaluate_store(c,consent=0 if mode=='CONSENT_REVOKE_DENIES' else 1)
        # Never use arbitrary memory as authoritative Evidence, even if its label is VERIFIED_COMPLETION.
        if mode=='CONSENT_REVOKE_DENIES':
            ok=status=='DENIED' and not eligible
            detail='consent_denied_before_native_memory_read=YES'
        elif mode in ('PAYLOAD_INTEGRITY_UNKNOWN','STALE_SLOT_GENERATION_UNKNOWN'):
            ok=status=='UNKNOWN' and not eligible
            detail='corrupt_or_stale_record_unavailable=YES'
        else:
            if status!='SUMMARY_ONLY':raise RuntimeError('SEMANTIC_STATUS_INCORRECT '+status)
            r,digest,err,_header_text=native_eval(exe,folder,eligible,q,z0)
            if mode=='SINGLE_SEMANTIC_CONTEXT':ok=len(eligible)==1 and abs(r[0]-.5)<1e-5
            elif mode=='C02_OPPOSITE_VALUES_COLLIDE':ok=len(eligible)==2 and abs(r[0])<1e-6 and abs(r[1])<1e-6 and len({x[2] for x in meta})==2
            elif mode=='EMPTY_ELIGIBLE_SET_ZERO':
                z_match=re.search(r'\bz0=([-+0-9.eE]+)\b',_header_text)
                ok=len(eligible)==0 and r==[0.]*M and z_match is not None and abs(float(z_match[1])-math.tanh(z0[0]))<1e-6
            elif mode=='STABLE_LARGE_LOGITS':ok=len(eligible)==2 and abs(r[0]-.75)<1e-4
            elif mode=='FP16_PAYLOAD_ROUNDTRIP':
                quantized=unpack_half(half_vec(vec(.3333)))[0]
                ok=abs(r[0]-quantized)<1e-6 and abs(quantized-.3333)>1e-7 and len(half_vec(vec(.3333)))==256
            elif mode in ('MODEL_MISMATCH_QUARANTINE','BOOT_MISMATCH_QUARANTINE'):
                ok=len(eligible)==0 and all(x==0 for x in r)
            elif mode=='DECLARATION_NOT_EVIDENCE':ok=meta[0][1]=='USER_DECLARATION' and not any(kind=='VERIFIED_COMPLETION' and trust for _,kind,_,trust,_,_,_ in meta)
            elif mode=='HYPOTHESIS_NOT_EVIDENCE':ok=meta[0][1]=='HYPOTHESIS' and not any(kind=='VERIFIED_COMPLETION' and trust for _,kind,_,trust,_,_,_ in meta)
            elif mode=='CONFLICT_NOT_RESOLVED_BY_SUMMARY':
                exact_state,exact_refs,full_scan=exact_query(c,k=4)
                ok=len(eligible)==2 and abs(r[0])<1e-6 and status=='SUMMARY_ONLY' and meta[0][4:6]==meta[1][4:6] and exact_state=='CONFLICT' and full_scan==2
            elif mode=='EXACT_RAW_BYTES_SEPARATE':
                exact_state,exact_refs,full_scan=exact_query(c,k=1)
                ok=exact_state=='COMPLETE' and exact_refs==[(1,b'\x00exact\xffbytes')] and status=='SUMMARY_ONLY' and b'\x00exact\xffbytes' not in folder.joinpath('semantic_half.bin').read_bytes()
            elif mode=='MISSING_RAW_NOT_RECONSTRUCTED':
                exact_state,exact_refs,full_scan=exact_query(c,k=1)
                ok=meta[0][2]==b'' and meta[0][-1]==0 and exact_state=='UNKNOWN' and not exact_refs and status=='SUMMARY_ONLY' and abs(r[0]-.5)<1e-5
            elif mode=='K1_EXACT_TRUNCATION':
                exact_state,exact_refs,full_scan=exact_query(c,k=1)
                ok=len(eligible)==4 and scanned==4 and exact_state=='TRUNCATED' and len(exact_refs)==1 and full_scan==4 and status=='SUMMARY_ONLY'
            elif mode=='SAME_UID_SEMANTIC_POISON_NEGATIVE':ok=negative and len(eligible)==1 and abs(r[0]+.875)<1e-5 and meta[0][2]==b'forged-same-UID'
            elif mode=='RECORD_ORDER_INVARIANCE':
                # DB insertion order is 2,1; canonical scan is slot 1,2. Same result after reverse-insertion.
                before=[p[0] for p in eligible]
                temp=folder/'second';temp.mkdir();other=temp/'test.sqlite3';c2=db_init(other)
                try:
                    add(c2,1,raw=b'source-B-false',val=vec(-1.,-.25))
                    add(c2,2,raw=b'source-A-true',val=vec(1.,.25))
                    s2,e2,scan2,m2=evaluate_store(c2)
                    r2,d2,e2err,_hdr2=native_eval(exe,temp,e2,q,z0)
                    ok=s2==status and before==[1,2] and d2==digest and len(e2)==2 and max(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(r,r2))<1e-6
                finally:c2.close()
            else:ok=False
            detail=f'semantic_status={status} eligible={len(eligible)} indexed_scan={scanned} r0={r[0]:.6g} native_F_MAC=34816 python_native_absmax={err:.2g}'
            if mode in ('C02_OPPOSITE_VALUES_COLLIDE','CONFLICT_NOT_RESOLVED_BY_SUMMARY'):detail+=' provenance_not_invertible=YES'
            if mode=='SAME_UID_SEMANTIC_POISON_NEGATIVE':detail+=' same_UID_forged_digest_accepted=YES'
            if mode=='K1_EXACT_TRUNCATION':detail+=' exact_k1_status=TRUNCATED'
        integrity=c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
        return bool(ok and integrity=='ok'),detail,integrity,negative
    finally:c.close()

def stage(log,ctx,binary,exe,root,nworkers,host,inject_abort=False):
    tag=f'S5x{nworkers}_RAM32';expected=len(MODES)*(1 if host else 2)
    stop=ctx.Event();wready=ctx.Queue();wout=ctx.Queue();rready=ctx.Queue()
    workers=[];holder=None;failures=0;completed=0;negative_found=0;worker_ok=0;mslist=[];reason=None
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=ram_worker,args=(stop,rready));holder.start()
        if rready.get(timeout=12) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            w=ctx.Process(target=s5_worker,args=(binary,stop,wready,wout));w.start();workers.append(w)
        if len({wready.get(timeout=25) for _ in workers})!=nworkers:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_BACKGROUND_START_FAILED')
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={tag} workers={nworkers}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={tag} cases={expected} modes={len(MODES)} RAM_MiB=32')
        for rep in range(1,2 if host else 3):
            for idx,mode in enumerate(MODES):
                if why:=safety(host):reason=why;break
                if inject_abort and completed>=4:reason='INJECTED_HOST_ABORT';break
                start_case=time.monotonic()
                ok,detail,integrity,negative=case_run(mode,root/tag/f'{rep:02d}_{idx:02d}_{mode}',exe)
                duration=(time.monotonic()-start_case)*1000
                completed+=1;mslist.append(duration)
                if not ok:failures+=1
                if ok and negative:negative_found+=1
                log(f'CASE stage={tag} rep={rep} mode={mode} status={"PASS_EXPECTED_NEGATIVE" if ok and negative else "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"} '
                    f'{detail} sqlite_integrity={integrity} elapsed_ms={duration:.3f}')
            if reason:break
        floor=2 if host else (24 if nworkers==2 else 30)
        while not reason and time.monotonic()-start<floor:
            if why:=safety(host):reason=why;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as exc:
        failures+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={tag} exception={type(exc).__name__} detail={str(exc)[:300]}')
    finally:
        stop.set()
        for w in workers:
            w.join(5)
            if w.is_alive():w.kill();w.join(3)
        if holder:
            holder.join(4)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(3)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,lowest,errs=wout.get(timeout=3)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean:.6f} min_GMAC_s={lowest:.6f} errors={errs}')
                if runs>0 and not errs:worker_ok+=1
                else:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:failures+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} REPORT_MISSING')
        for q in (wready,wout,rready):q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
    label='SAFETY_ABORT' if reason else ('COMPLETE' if completed==expected and not failures and worker_ok==nworkers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={tag} status={label} cases={completed}/{expected} failures={failures} negative_findings={negative_found} workers_ok={worker_ok}/{nworkers} case_max_ms={max(mslist) if mslist else None} safety_reason={reason or "-"}')
    b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={tag} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return label,completed,negative_found

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='developer Linux mode only')
    ap.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true',help='developer Linux mode only')

            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')

            log('LIMIT: source trust is not enforced by same-UID SQLite; no exact proof from summary, semantic quality or Android trust boundary.')
            return 0
        except BaseException as exc:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:250]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc()[-1200:]);return 2
        finally:log('REPORT '+str(report))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 §11/§12.4 isolated lab: 4096 shared memory slot bound and measured memory.
Synthetic data only. No training, real permissions, OS actions or production files.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue

import shutil
import sqlite3
import statistics
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
