VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_SHARED_4096_MEMORY_CAPACITY_V17'
MIB=1048576
N=4096
M=128
KIND=('MEM','Q','D','L','U')
MODEL_A=hashlib.sha256(b'v17-fixture-model-A').digest()
MODEL_B=hashlib.sha256(b'v17-fixture-model-B').digest()
MODES=(
 'HARD_SHARED_CAP_4096', 'FULL_STREAM_INTEGRITY_4096',
 'EXACT_K1_FULL_SCAN', 'K16_STABLE_PAGING',
 'ALL_PINNED_INSERT_DENIED', 'STALE_GENERATION_REUSED',
 'GENERATION_WRAP_RETIRES_SLOT', 'MODEL_QUARANTINE',
 'MIGRATION_MAX16_PER_TASK', 'CONSENT_REVOKED_NO_ACCESS',
 'CORRUPTED_PAYLOAD_REJECTED', 'CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_MUTATION',
)

def numfile(p):
    try:return int(Path(p).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError, ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:m=int(next(x for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):m=None
    return numfile('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),numfile('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),m

def safety(host):
    if host:return None
    b,t,m=telemetry()
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100 <= t <= 800:return 'SAFETY_TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b < 40:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t >= 375:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m < 400000:return 'SAFETY_MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def pss_kb(pid=None):
    path=Path(f'/proc/{pid if pid is not None else "self"}/smaps_rollup')
    try:
        data=path.read_text()
        return int(re.search(r'^Pss:\s+(\d+)\s+kB$',data,re.M).group(1))
    except (OSError,AttributeError,ValueError):return None

def rss_hwm_kb():
    try:
        data=Path('/proc/self/status').read_text()
        def v(key):
            m=re.search(r'^'+key+r':\s+(\d+)\s+kB$',data,re.M)
            return int(m[1]) if m else None
        return v('VmRSS'),v('VmHWM')
    except OSError:return None,None

def binary_path():
    p=Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_BINARY_MISSING '+str(p))
    r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_PRECHECK_FAILED '+r.stdout[-200:])
    return p

def s5_worker(p,stop,ready,out):
    vals=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=15)
            if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
                raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_NATIVE_F_FAILED')
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',r.stdout)
            if not m:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_F_METRIC_MISSING')
            vals.append(float(m[1]))
            if len(vals)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=5)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:200])
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(vals),statistics.mean(vals) if vals else 0.,min(vals) if vals else 0.,errors),timeout=5)
    except BaseException:pass

def ram_worker(stop,ready):
    try:
        b=bytearray(32*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(b),4096):b[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=5)
        while not stop.wait(.2):
            for i in range(0,len(b),262144):b[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def connect(path):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),isolation_level=None,timeout=8)
    c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=6000')
    c.execute('PRAGMA cache_size=-4096') # cap SQLite page cache for fixture (~4 MiB)
    return c

def header(slot,gen):
    b=bytearray(64)
    struct.pack_into('<QIIQQQIHHQQ',b,0,100000+slot,slot,gen,9999+slot,100000000+slot,200000000+slot,0,1,0,4,slot)
    assert len(b)==64
    return bytes(b)

def bytes_payload(slot):
    return hashlib.blake2s(struct.pack('<I',slot)).digest()*128

def index_hash(head,key,val):
    return hashlib.sha256(head+key+val).digest()

def make_store(path,host,log):
    c=connect(path)
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE meta(k TEXT PRIMARY KEY,v INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('INSERT INTO meta VALUES(?,?)',('snapshot',1))
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE records(slot INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(slot BETWEEN 0 AND 4095),generation INTEGER NOT NULL,kind TEXT NOT NULL,pinned INTEGER NOT NULL,header BLOB NOT NULL,key_fp16 BLOB NOT NULL,value_fp16 BLOB NOT NULL,payload BLOB NOT NULL,payload_sha BLOB NOT NULL,index_sha BLOB NOT NULL,model_digest BLOB NOT NULL,boot INTEGER NOT NULL,consent INTEGER NOT NULL,expiry INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    c.execute("CREATE TRIGGER records_full BEFORE INSERT ON records WHEN (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records) >= 4096 BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'DENIED_STORAGE'); END")
    c.execute("CREATE TRIGGER pinned_protected BEFORE DELETE ON records WHEN OLD.pinned=1 BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'DENIED_PINNED'); END")
    key=struct.pack('<128e',*([0.125]*M))
    val=struct.pack('<128e',*([0.25]*M))
    baseline=hashlib.sha256()
    started=time.monotonic()
    for base in range(0,N,128):
        if why:=safety(host):raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_FILL_'+why)
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        try:
            for i in range(base,min(base+128,N)):
                pay=bytes_payload(i);h=header(i,1);sha=hashlib.sha256(pay).digest();idx=index_hash(h,key,val)
                c.execute('INSERT INTO records VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                        (i,1,KIND[i%len(KIND)],int(i%97==0),h,key,val,pay,sha,idx,MODEL_A,1,1,99999))
                baseline.update(struct.pack('<I',i));baseline.update(sha);baseline.update(idx)
            c.execute('COMMIT')
        except BaseException:
            c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise
    c.execute('PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)') # only fixture WAL
    elapsed=(time.monotonic()-started)*1000
    pages=c.execute('PRAGMA page_count').fetchone()[0]
    size=c.execute('PRAGMA page_size').fetchone()[0]
    dbsize=path.stat().st_size
    log(f'STORE_FILLED slots={N} shared_kinds={len(KIND)} payload_bytes_per_slot=4096 model_fp16_key_bytes=256 model_fp16_value_bytes=256 fixed_header_bytes=64 indexed_hash_bytes=64 expected_raw_payload_bytes={N*4096} sqlite_pages={pages} sqlite_page_size={size} sqlite_bytes={dbsize} fill_wall_ms={elapsed:.1f}')
    return c,baseline.digest()

def snapshot(c):return int(c.execute('SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k="snapshot"').fetchone()[0])

def scoped(c):
    """Savepoint protects all 4096 fixture records; changes never escape case."""
    class S:
        def __enter__(self):
            c.execute('SAVEPOINT case_fixture');return c
        def __exit__(self,typ,val,tb):
            c.execute('ROLLBACK TO case_fixture');c.execute('RELEASE case_fixture')
    return S()

def read_ref(c,slot,generation,model=MODEL_A,boot=1,consent=1):
    if consent!=1:return 'DENIED',None
    row=c.execute('SELECT generation,header,key_fp16,value_fp16,payload,payload_sha,index_sha,model_digest,boot,consent,expiry FROM records WHERE slot=?',(slot,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:return 'UNKNOWN',None
    gen,h,key,val,payload,pay_sha,idx_sha,mod,b,co,expiry=row
    if mod!=model or b!=boot or co!=consent or expiry<=100:return 'QUARANTINED',None
    if gen!=generation:return 'STALE',None
    if len(h)!=64 or len(key)!=256 or len(val)!=256 or len(payload)>4096:return 'CORRUPT',None
    if h!=header(slot,generation):return 'CORRUPT',None
    if hashlib.sha256(payload).digest()!=pay_sha or index_hash(h,key,val)!=idx_sha:return 'CORRUPT',None
    return 'COMPLETE',payload

def bounded_full_scan(c,k=1,model=MODEL_A,consent=1):
    if consent!=1:return 'DENIED',[],0,0
    if not (1<=k<=16):return 'INVALID',[],0,0
    scanned=0;matched=0;selected=[]
    for slot,kind,gen,mod,expiry in c.execute('SELECT slot,kind,generation,model_digest,expiry FROM records ORDER BY slot'):
        scanned+=1
        if kind=='MEM' and mod==model and expiry>100:
            matched+=1
            if len(selected)<k:selected.append((slot,gen))
    return ('TRUNCATED' if matched>k else 'COMPLETE'),selected,scanned,matched

def pagination(c,k=16):
    ver=snapshot(c);last=-1;all_seen=[];scanned=0
    while True:
        if snapshot(c)!=ver:raise RuntimeError('STALE_CURSOR')
        batch=c.execute('SELECT slot FROM records WHERE slot>? ORDER BY slot LIMIT ?',(last,k)).fetchall()
        scanned+=len(batch)
        if not batch:break
        slots=[r[0] for r in batch]
        if slots[0]<=last:raise RuntimeError('BAD_PAGE_ORDER')
        all_seen.extend(slots);last=slots[-1]
    return all_seen,scanned

def next_gen(g):
    return None if g>=0xffffffff else g+1

def case(mode,c,expected_digest):
    # Cases use only synthetic DB; no real application data or state.
    if mode=='HARD_SHARED_CAP_4096':
        counts=dict(c.execute('SELECT kind,COUNT(*) FROM records GROUP BY kind').fetchall())
        attempted=False;denied=False
        with scoped(c):
            try:
                c.execute('INSERT INTO records SELECT 4096,generation,kind,pinned,header,key_fp16,value_fp16,payload,payload_sha,index_sha,model_digest,boot,consent,expiry FROM records WHERE slot=0')
                attempted=True
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError as e:denied='DENIED_STORAGE' in str(e)
            same_count=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records').fetchone()[0]==N
        return (not attempted and denied and same_count and sum(counts.values())==N and set(counts)==set(KIND),f'kinds={counts} cap={N} 4097th_insert=DENIED_STORAGE')
    if mode=='FULL_STREAM_INTEGRITY_4096':
        s=hashlib.sha256();scanned=0
        for slot,gen,h,key,val,pay,sha,idx in c.execute('SELECT slot,generation,header,key_fp16,value_fp16,payload,payload_sha,index_sha FROM records ORDER BY slot'):
            if h!=header(slot,gen) or len(pay)!=4096 or hashlib.sha256(pay).digest()!=sha or index_hash(h,key,val)!=idx:return False,'FULL_RECORD_CORRUPT'
            s.update(struct.pack('<I',slot));s.update(sha);s.update(idx);scanned+=1
        return (scanned==N and s.digest()==expected_digest,f'full_integrity=YES scanned={scanned} baseline_digest_match={s.digest()==expected_digest}')
    if mode=='EXACT_K1_FULL_SCAN':
        status,refs,scanned,matched=bounded_full_scan(c,1)
        return (status=='TRUNCATED' and len(refs)==1 and scanned==N and matched==820 and read_ref(c,*refs[0])[0]=='COMPLETE',f'status={status} k=1 matched={matched} scanned={scanned} refs={len(refs)}')
    if mode=='K16_STABLE_PAGING':
        ids,scanned=pagination(c,16)
        return (ids==list(range(N)) and scanned==N,f'pages={len(ids)//16} returned={len(ids)} scanned={scanned} monotonic=YES')
    if mode=='ALL_PINNED_INSERT_DENIED':
        with scoped(c):
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET pinned=1')
            count=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records WHERE pinned=1').fetchone()[0]
            blocked_insert=False;blocked_delete=False
            try:
                c.execute('INSERT INTO records SELECT 4096,generation,kind,pinned,header,key_fp16,value_fp16,payload,payload_sha,index_sha,model_digest,boot,consent,expiry FROM records WHERE slot=0')
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError as e:blocked_insert='DENIED_STORAGE' in str(e)
            try:c.execute('DELETE FROM records WHERE slot=0')
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError as e:blocked_delete='DENIED_PINNED' in str(e)
            return (count==N and blocked_insert and blocked_delete and c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records').fetchone()[0]==N,f'all_pinned={count} insertion_denied={blocked_insert} protected_eviction_denied={blocked_delete} shared_slots={N}')
    if mode=='STALE_GENERATION_REUSED':
        with scoped(c):
            slot=5;old_status,_=read_ref(c,slot,1)
            pay=bytes_payload(slot);h=header(slot,2)
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET generation=?,header=?,index_sha=? WHERE slot=?',(2,h,index_hash(h,struct.pack('<128e',*([.125]*128)),struct.pack('<128e',*([.25]*128))),slot))
            stale,_=read_ref(c,slot,1);new,_=read_ref(c,slot,2)
            return (old_status=='COMPLETE' and stale=='STALE' and new=='COMPLETE',f'old_status={old_status} stale_status={stale} current_status={new}')
    if mode=='GENERATION_WRAP_RETIRES_SLOT':
        with scoped(c):
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET generation=? WHERE slot=?',(4294967295,9))
            value=c.execute('SELECT generation FROM records WHERE slot=9').fetchone()[0]
            retired=next_gen(value) is None
            return (retired and read_ref(c,9,1)[0]=='STALE',f'generation={value} retired={retired} next_generation={next_gen(value)}')
    if mode=='MODEL_QUARANTINE':
        s,refs,sc,matched=bounded_full_scan(c,16,model=MODEL_B)
        stat,_=read_ref(c,0,1,model=MODEL_B)
        return (s=='COMPLETE' and not refs and matched==0 and sc==N and stat=='QUARANTINED',f'foreign_model_status={stat} eligible={matched} scanned={sc}')
    if mode=='MIGRATION_MAX16_PER_TASK':
        with scoped(c):
            # Reencode 16 synthetic FP16 key/values from exact payload; not learned F.
            selected=c.execute('SELECT slot,payload,header FROM records ORDER BY slot LIMIT 16').fetchall()
            for slot,pay,h in selected:
                d=hashlib.sha256(pay+MODEL_B).digest()
                key=struct.pack('<128e',*[(d[i%32]-127)/256 for i in range(128)])
                val=struct.pack('<128e',*[(d[(i+7)%32]-127)/256 for i in range(128)])
                c.execute('UPDATE records SET key_fp16=?,value_fp16=?,index_sha=?,model_digest=? WHERE slot=?',
                          (key,val,index_hash(h,key,val),MODEL_B,slot))
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=v+1 WHERE k="snapshot"')
            total=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records WHERE model_digest=?',(MODEL_B,)).fetchone()[0]
            rest=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records WHERE model_digest=?',(MODEL_A,)).fetchone()[0]
            current,_=read_ref(c,0,1,model=MODEL_B)
            remaining,_=read_ref(c,16,1,model=MODEL_B)
            # Model-B record header remains original synthetic ABI; no key compatibility claim.
            return (len(selected)==16 and total==16 and rest==N-16 and current=='COMPLETE' and remaining=='QUARANTINED',f'migrated_this_task={total} excluded_remaining={rest} max_per_task=16')
    if mode=='CONSENT_REVOKED_NO_ACCESS':
        state,refs,sc,matched=bounded_full_scan(c,1,consent=0)
        status,_=read_ref(c,0,1,consent=0)
        return (state=='DENIED' and not refs and sc==0 and status=='DENIED',f'exact_status={state} reference_status={status} data_scanned={sc}')
    if mode=='CORRUPTED_PAYLOAD_REJECTED':
        with scoped(c):
            c.execute('UPDATE records SET payload=? WHERE slot=0',(b'corrupted-same-uid',))
            stat,_=read_ref(c,0,1)
            good,_=read_ref(c,1,1)
            return (stat=='CORRUPT' and good=='COMPLETE',f'corrupted_status={stat} unaffected_slot={good}')
    if mode=='CURSOR_INVALID_AFTER_MUTATION':
        with scoped(c):
            old=snapshot(c)
            first=c.execute('SELECT slot FROM records WHERE slot>-1 ORDER BY slot LIMIT 16').fetchall()
            c.execute("UPDATE records SET kind='MEM' WHERE slot=1")
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=v+1 WHERE k="snapshot"')
            rejected=snapshot(c)!=old
            return (len(first)==16 and rejected,f'page_count={len(first)} old_snapshot={old} current_snapshot={snapshot(c)} old_cursor=STALE')
    raise RuntimeError('UNKNOWN_MODE_'+mode)

def stage(log,ctx,bg,root,nworkers,host,inject):
    tag=f'S5x{nworkers}_RAM32';expected=len(MODES)*(1 if host else 2)
    stop=ctx.Event();wr=ctx.Queue();wo=ctx.Queue();rr=ctx.Queue();workers=[];holder=None
    completed=0;failures=0;worker_ok=0;reason=None;durations=[];conn=None
    stage_start=time.monotonic()
    ps_before=pss_kb();rss_before,hwm_before=rss_hwm_kb()
    try:
        holder=ctx.Process(target=ram_worker,args=(stop,rr));holder.start()
        if rr.get(timeout=15) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            w=ctx.Process(target=s5_worker,args=(bg,stop,wr,wo));w.start();workers.append(w)
        if len({wr.get(timeout=28) for _ in workers})!=nworkers:raise RuntimeError('S5_NOT_ALL_ACTIVE')
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={tag} workers={nworkers}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={tag} expected={expected} slots={N} kinds={len(KIND)} RAM_MiB=32')
        stage_path=root/tag;stage_path.mkdir()
        conn,baseline=make_store(stage_path/'shared_store.sqlite3',host,log)
        log(f'ACTUAL_MEMORY stage={tag} phase=after_fill own_PSS_kB={pss_kb()} own_RSS_kB={rss_hwm_kb()[0]} own_HWM_kB={rss_hwm_kb()[1]} NOTE=not_Android_app_peak')
        for rep in range(1,2 if host else 3):
            for mode in MODES:
                if why:=safety(host):reason=why;break
                if inject and completed>=3:reason='INJECTED_HOST_SAFETY_ABORT';break
                t=time.monotonic()
                ok,detail=case(mode,conn,baseline)
                ms=(time.monotonic()-t)*1000;durations.append(ms);completed+=1
                if not ok:failures+=1
                log(f'CASE stage={tag} rep={rep} mode={mode} status={"PASS" if ok else "FAIL"} {detail} elapsed_ms={ms:.3f}')
            if reason:break
        if conn:
            integrity=conn.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
            if integrity!='ok':failures+=1
            log(f'STORAGE_CHECK stage={tag} sqlite_integrity={integrity} count={conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records").fetchone()[0]}')
        floor=2 if host else (24 if nworkers==2 else 30)
        while not reason and time.monotonic()-stage_start<floor:
            if why:=safety(host):reason=why;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as exc:
        failures+=1
        if str(exc).startswith('SAFETY_ABORT_FILL_'):reason=str(exc)
        else:log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={tag} error={type(exc).__name__} detail={str(exc)[:200]}')
    finally:
        if conn:conn.close()
        stop.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
        if holder:
            holder.join(4)
            if holder.is_alive():holder.kill();holder.join(3)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,minval,errors=wo.get(timeout=3)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean:.6f} min_GMAC_s={minval:.6f} errors={errors}')
                if runs>0 and not errors:worker_ok+=1
                else:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:failures+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} REPORT_MISSING')
        for q in (wr,wo,rr):q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
    ps_after=pss_kb();rss,hwm=rss_hwm_kb()
    label='SAFETY_ABORT' if reason else ('COMPLETE' if completed==expected and failures==0 and worker_ok==nworkers else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={tag} status={label} cases={completed}/{expected} failures={failures} workers_ok={worker_ok}/{nworkers} max_case_ms={max(durations) if durations else None} safety_reason={reason or "-"}')
    log(f'MEMORY_MEASURE stage={tag} own_PSS_before_kB={ps_before} own_PSS_after_kB={ps_after} own_RSS_before_kB={rss_before} own_RSS_after_kB={rss} own_HWM_after_kB={hwm} NOTE=single_python_process_not_total_peak')
    b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={tag} battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
    return label,completed

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='developer-only local Linux smoke')
    ap.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true',help='developer-only simulation')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    if args.inject_host_abort and not args.host_smoke:raise SystemExit('HOST_ONLY_INJECTION')
    home=Path.home();out=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not out.is_dir() or not os.access(out,os.W_OK):out=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=out/f'UNO_A52_R41_SHARED_4096_MEMORY_CAPACITY_V17_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        def log(x):print(x,flush=True);fp.write(x+'\n');fp.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- SHARED 4096-SLOT MEMORY CAPACITY / REAL SQLITE + PSS UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('R4.1 §11, §12.4: 4096 shared slots across MEM/Q/D/L/U, FP16 m128 key/value, 64B header, max 4096B payload, 64B indexed hashes; synthetic fixture.')
            log('4096 records are materialized in SQLite WAL FULL. Real process PSS is measured where /proc/self/smaps_rollup permits; not total Android peak.')
            log('Two/four continuously computing native C256R32 S5 background workers plus 32MiB held RAM. No idle stage.')
            log('NO neural model training, P updates, Android permission/STOP/OS dispatch, secure provenance, personal data, power loss, or production code.')
            log('SAFETY battery>=40%, battery temperature<37.5C, MemAvailable>=400000kB. SAFETY_ABORT is never COMPLETE.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE; PRODUCTION_CODE_AUTHORIZED = FALSE')
            b,t,m=telemetry();log(f'PRECHECK battery={b} temp_deciC={t} MemAvailable_kB={m}')
            if why:=safety(args.host_smoke):log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+why);return 3
            base=home/'uno_a52_r41_shared_capacity_v17'
            base.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            if not args.host_smoke and shutil.disk_usage(base).free<128*MIB:
                log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP STORAGE_FREE_BELOW_128_MiB');return 3
            native=binary_path();log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_NATIVE_S5 '+str(native))
            root=base/stamp;root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            a=stage(log,ctx,native,root,1 if args.host_smoke else 2,args.host_smoke,args.inject_host_abort)
            if a[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {a[0]} cases={a[1]}');return 3
            b=stage(log,ctx,native,root,2 if args.host_smoke else 4,args.host_smoke,False)
            if b[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {b[0]} cases={b[1]}');return 3
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_SHARED_4096_MEMORY_CAPACITY_LAB stages=2/2 cases={a[1]+b[1]} slots_per_stage=4096')
            log('LIMIT: fixture key/value are synthetic; SSD durability, protected provenance, semantic quality, actual full Android app peak PSS, real migration and energy are untested.')
            return 0
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:250]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc()[-1400:]);return 2
        finally:log('REPORT '+str(report))
if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 isolated V18: 4096 slot WAL concurrent readers/writer stress on A52.
Not a secure Android boundary, trained model, physical power-cut or production code.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import shutil
import signal
import sqlite3
import statistics
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_WAL_CONCURRENT_MEMORY_STRESS_V18'
N=4096
MIB=1048576
KINDS=('MEM','Q','D','L','U')
MODES=('READER_PINNED_OLD_SNAPSHOT','WAL_CHECKPOINT_PINNED_THEN_RELEASED',
       'PAGING_CURSOR_STALE_AFTER_WRITER','CONCURRENT_READERS_SINGLE_WRITER',
       'WRITER_CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT','SQLITE_BUSY_NO_IMPLICIT_RETRY',
       'CONSENT_FRESH_READ_DENIED','CONSENT_TOCTOU_NEGATIVE',
       'FULL_4096_INTEGRITY_AFTER_CHURN')

def readnum(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:mem=int(next(x for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,StopIteration,ValueError,IndexError):mem=None
    return dict(battery=readnum('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),
                temp_deciC=readnum('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),
                MemAvailable_kB=mem,
                charge_status=(Path('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/status').read_text().strip() if Path('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/status').exists() else 'UNAVAILABLE'))

def guard(host):
    if host:return None
    x=telemetry();b=x['battery'];t=x['temp_deciC'];m=x['MemAvailable_kB']
    if None in (b,t,m) or not (100<=t<=800):return 'SAFETY_TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'SAFETY_MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def smaps(pid=None):
    p=Path(f'/proc/{pid if pid is not None else "self"}/smaps_rollup')
    try:
        d=p.read_text();q=re.search(r'^Pss:\s+(\d+)\s+kB$',d,re.M)
        return int(q[1]) if q else None
    except OSError:return None

def proc_rss():
    try:
        d=Path('/proc/self/status').read_text();m=re.search(r'^VmRSS:\s+(\d+)\s+kB$',d,re.M)
        return int(m[1]) if m else None
    except OSError:return None

def native_binary(host,override=None):
    p=Path(override) if host and override else Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_BINARY_MISSING '+str(p))
    r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=12)
    if r.returncode!=0 or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_OPERATOR_CONTRACT_FAIL '+r.stdout[-220:])
    return p

def s5worker(binary,stop,ready,out):
    vals=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            r=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=15)
            if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
                raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_CONTRACT_FAIL')
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',r.stdout)
            if not m:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_THROUGHPUT_MISSING')
            vals.append(float(m[1]))
            if len(vals)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=5)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:160])
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(vals),statistics.mean(vals) if vals else 0.,min(vals) if vals else 0.,errors),timeout=4)
    except BaseException:pass

def ramholder(stop,ready):
    try:
        b=bytearray(32*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(b),4096):b[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=5)
        while not stop.wait(.15):
            for i in range(0,len(b),262144):b[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def dbconnect(path,busy=1200):
    c=sqlite3.connect(str(path),timeout=busy/1000,isolation_level=None)
    c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    c.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    c.execute(f'PRAGMA busy_timeout={busy}')
    c.execute('PRAGMA cache_size=-4096')
    return c

def pay(slot,gen):
    h=hashlib.blake2s(struct.pack('<II',slot,gen)).digest()
    return h*128

def sha(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).digest()

def setup(path,host,log):
    c=dbconnect(path)
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE meta(k TEXT PRIMARY KEY,v INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    c.executemany('INSERT INTO meta VALUES(?,?)', [('version',0),('consent',1),('consent_epoch',1)])
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE records(slot INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(slot BETWEEN 0 AND 4095),kind TEXT NOT NULL,generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(generation>=1),payload BLOB NOT NULL,payload_sha BLOB NOT NULL,pinned INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)')
    c.execute('CREATE TABLE audit(version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,slot INTEGER NOT NULL,generation INTEGER NOT NULL,payload_sha BLOB NOT NULL)')
    c.execute('CREATE TRIGGER cap BEFORE INSERT ON records WHEN (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records)>=4096 BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, "DENIED_STORAGE"); END')
    begin=time.monotonic()
    for start in range(0,N,128):
        if reason:=guard(host):raise RuntimeError('SAFETY_ABORT_FILL_'+reason)
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        try:
            c.executemany('INSERT INTO records(slot,kind,generation,payload,payload_sha,pinned) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                          [(s,KINDS[s%5],1,pay(s,1),sha(pay(s,1)),int(s%97==0)) for s in range(start,start+128)])
            c.execute('COMMIT')
        except BaseException:c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise
    c.execute('PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)')
    size=path.stat().st_size
    log(f'STORE_FILLED slots={N} sqlite_bytes={size} fill_wall_ms={(time.monotonic()-begin)*1000:.1f} own_PSS_kB={smaps()}')
    return c

def meta(c,k):return int(c.execute('SELECT v FROM meta WHERE k=?',(k,)).fetchone()[0])

def checked_payload(c,slot=0):
    row=c.execute('SELECT generation,payload,payload_sha FROM records WHERE slot=?',(slot,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:raise AssertionError('MISSING_SLOT')
    gen,b,d=row
    if len(b)!=4096 or sha(b)!=d or b!=pay(slot,gen):raise AssertionError('BAD_PAYLOAD')
    return gen,b

def commit_update(c,slot=0):
    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
    try:
        old=meta(c,'version');gen=int(c.execute('SELECT generation FROM records WHERE slot=?',(slot,)).fetchone()[0])+1
        payload=pay(slot,gen);digest=sha(payload)
        c.execute('UPDATE records SET generation=?,payload=?,payload_sha=? WHERE slot=?',(gen,payload,digest,slot))
        c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=? WHERE k="version"',(old+1,))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO audit VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(old+1,slot,gen,digest))
        c.execute('COMMIT')
        return old+1,gen
    except BaseException:
        c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise

def update_child(path,n,out):
    try:
        c=dbconnect(path)
        for j in range(n):commit_update(c)
        c.close();out.put(('OK',n),timeout=3)
    except BaseException as e:
        try:out.put(('FAIL',str(e)[:180]),timeout=3)
        except BaseException:pass

def crash_child(path,ready):
    try:
        c=dbconnect(path)
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        old=meta(c,'version');gen=checked_payload(c)[0]+1
        b=pay(0,gen)
        c.execute('UPDATE records SET generation=?,payload=?,payload_sha=? WHERE slot=0',(gen,b,sha(b)))
        c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=? WHERE k="version"',(old+1,))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO audit VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(old+1,0,gen,sha(b)))
        ready.put(('OPEN_UNCOMMITTED',old),timeout=4)
        time.sleep(25)
    except BaseException as e:
        try:ready.put(('ERROR',str(e)[:180]),timeout=2)
        except BaseException:pass

def busy_child(path,out):
    c=None
    try:
        c=dbconnect(path,busy=150)
        try:
            c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            c.execute('ROLLBACK');out.put('UNEXPECTED_ACQUIRED',timeout=3)
        except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:
            if 'locked' in str(e).lower():out.put('SQLITE_BUSY',timeout=3)
            else:out.put('OTHER_'+str(e)[:100],timeout=3)
    except BaseException as e:
        try:out.put('ERROR_'+str(e)[:100],timeout=3)
        except BaseException:pass
    finally:
        if c:c.close()

def scan_reader(path,stop,ready,out):
    c=None;count=0;errors=[];last=-1
    try:
        c=dbconnect(path)
        ready.put(True,timeout=4)
        while not stop.is_set():
            c.execute('BEGIN')
            try:
                v=meta(c,'version');gen,_=checked_payload(c,0)
                n=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records').fetchone()[0]
                a=int(c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit').fetchone()[0])
                if gen!=v+1 or a!=v or n!=N:raise AssertionError(f'TORN_SNAPSHOT v={v} gen={gen} audit={a} n={n}')
                count+=1;last=v
                c.execute('COMMIT')
            except BaseException:
                c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise
            if count%8==0:time.sleep(.001)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:180])
    finally:
        if c:c.close()
        try:out.put((count,last,errors),timeout=4)
        except BaseException:pass

def child_wait(proc,host,limit=12):
    end=time.monotonic()+limit
    while proc.is_alive() and time.monotonic()<end:
        if why:=guard(host):raise RuntimeError(why)
        proc.join(.07)
    if proc.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('CHILD_TIMEOUT')
    if proc.exitcode!=0:raise RuntimeError('CHILD_EXIT_'+str(proc.exitcode))

def query_page(c,cursor=None,k=16):
    # Illustrates cooperative *fresh* permission check, NOT linearizable OS authority.
    if meta(c,'consent')!=1:return 'DENIED',[],None,0
    v=meta(c,'version');e=meta(c,'consent_epoch')
    if cursor and cursor[:2]!=(v,e):return 'STALE_CURSOR',[],None,0
    last=-1 if cursor is None else cursor[2]
    rows=[r[0] for r in c.execute('SELECT slot FROM records WHERE slot>? ORDER BY slot LIMIT ?',(last,k)).fetchall()]
    next_cursor=(v,e,rows[-1]) if rows else None
    return 'COMPLETE',rows,next_cursor,len(rows)

def mode_case(mode,c,path,ctx,host,log):
    if mode in ('READER_PINNED_OLD_SNAPSHOT','WAL_CHECKPOINT_PINNED_THEN_RELEASED'):
        reader=dbconnect(path)
        try:
            reader.execute('BEGIN')
            v0=meta(reader,'version');g0,_=checked_payload(reader)
            out=ctx.Queue();w=ctx.Process(target=update_child,args=(path,8 if host else 24,out));w.start()
            try:
                child_wait(w,host,16)
                report=out.get(timeout=3)
                assert report[0]=='OK',report
            finally:
                if w.is_alive():w.kill();w.join(3)
                out.cancel_join_thread();out.close()
            v_old=meta(reader,'version');g_old,_=checked_payload(reader)
            v_new=meta(c,'version');g_new,_=checked_payload(c)
            wal=Path(str(path)+'-wal')
            wal_before=wal.stat().st_size if wal.exists() else 0
            if mode=='WAL_CHECKPOINT_PINNED_THEN_RELEASED':
                # PASSIVE checkpoint is nonblocking. Reader has an old snapshot, thus cannot advance to tip.
                busy,logframes,checkpointed=tuple(c.execute('PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)').fetchone())
                incomplete=logframes>checkpointed
                reader.execute('COMMIT');reader.close();reader=None
                done=tuple(c.execute('PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)').fetchone())
                wal_after=wal.stat().st_size if wal.exists() else 0
                ok=(v_old==v0 and g_old==g0 and v_new>v0 and g_new>g0 and incomplete and done[0]==0 and wal_after==0)
                return ok,f'old_version={v_old} new_version={v_new} pinned_WAL_bytes={wal_before} passive_frames={logframes} passive_checkpointed={checkpointed} full_checkpoint={done} WAL_after_bytes={wal_after}'
            ok=(v_old==v0 and g_old==g0 and v_new==v0+(8 if host else 24) and g_new==g0+(8 if host else 24))
            return ok,f'pinned_old={v_old}/{g_old} fresh={v_new}/{g_new} snapshot_consistent={ok} wal_bytes={wal_before}'
        finally:
            if reader:
                try:reader.execute('COMMIT')
                except sqlite3.Error:pass
                reader.close()
    if mode=='PAGING_CURSOR_STALE_AFTER_WRITER':
        first,rows,cur,sc=query_page(c,k=16)
        commit_update(c)
        stale,after,_,sc_after=query_page(c,cur,k=16)
        fresh,fr,_,sc_fr=query_page(c,k=16)
        ok=(first=='COMPLETE' and len(rows)==16 and stale=='STALE_CURSOR' and not after and sc_after==0 and fresh=='COMPLETE' and len(fr)==16)
        return ok,f'old_cursor={stale} old_cursor_data_scan={sc_after} fresh_count={len(fr)}'
    if mode=='CONCURRENT_READERS_SINGLE_WRITER':
        stop=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();out=ctx.Queue();wo=ctx.Queue();readers=[];writer=None
        try:
            for _ in range(2):
                p=ctx.Process(target=scan_reader,args=(path,stop,ready,out));p.start();readers.append(p)
            for _ in readers:
                assert ready.get(timeout=6) is True
            base=meta(c,'version')
            n=24 if host else 64
            writer=ctx.Process(target=update_child,args=(path,n,wo));writer.start()
            child_wait(writer,host,24)
            wreport=wo.get(timeout=4)
            assert wreport==('OK',n),wreport
            # Keep readers active until they observe the committed endpoint.
            end=time.monotonic()+(.1 if host else .25)
            while time.monotonic()<end:
                if why:=guard(host):raise RuntimeError(why)
                time.sleep(.04)
            stop.set()
            for p in readers:child_wait(p,host,5)
            infos=[out.get(timeout=3) for _ in readers]
            v=meta(c,'version');g,_=checked_payload(c)
            ok=(v==base+n and g==v+1 and all(i[0]>2 and not i[2] for i in infos))
            return ok,f'commits={n} version={v} readers_checks={[i[0] for i in infos]} reader_errors={[i[2] for i in infos]} snapshot_atomic={ok}'
        finally:
            stop.set()
            if writer and writer.is_alive():writer.kill();writer.join(3)
            for p in readers:
                if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
            for q in (ready,out,wo):q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
    if mode=='WRITER_CRASH_BEFORE_COMMIT':
        before=meta(c,'version');g0,_=checked_payload(c)
        ready=ctx.Queue();p=ctx.Process(target=crash_child,args=(path,ready));p.start()
        try:
            received=ready.get(timeout=8)
            if received!=('OPEN_UNCOMMITTED',before):raise RuntimeError('WRITER_NOT_PREPARED_'+str(received))
            os.kill(p.pid,signal.SIGKILL);p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('KILLED_CHILD_STILL_ALIVE')
        finally:
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
            ready.cancel_join_thread();ready.close()
        check=dbconnect(path)
        try:
            after=meta(check,'version');g1,_=checked_payload(check)
            audit_count=check.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit').fetchone()[0]
            integrity=check.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
        finally:check.close()
        ok=(after==before and g1==g0 and audit_count==after and integrity=='ok')
        return ok,f'killed_uncommitted_writer=YES pre_version={before} post_version={after} pre_gen={g0} post_gen={g1} audit={audit_count} integrity={integrity}'
    if mode=='SQLITE_BUSY_NO_IMPLICIT_RETRY':
        v0=meta(c,'version');q=ctx.Queue();p=None
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        try:
            p=ctx.Process(target=busy_child,args=(path,q));p.start()
            child_wait(p,host,5)
            result=q.get(timeout=3)
        finally:
            c.execute('ROLLBACK')
            if p and p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
            q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
        no_partial=(meta(c,'version')==v0)
        v1,g1=commit_update(c)
        ok=(result=='SQLITE_BUSY' and no_partial and v1==v0+1)
        return ok,f'contending_writer={result} no_partial={no_partial} next_explicit_commit={v1} generation={g1}'
    if mode=='CONSENT_FRESH_READ_DENIED':
        a=meta(c,'consent');e=meta(c,'consent_epoch')
        first,rows,cur,sc=query_page(c,k=16)
        assert first=='COMPLETE' and len(rows)==16 and cur
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        try:
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=0 WHERE k="consent"')
            c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=v+1 WHERE k="consent_epoch"')
            c.execute('COMMIT')
        except BaseException:c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise
        state,refs,_,scan=query_page(c,cur,k=16)
        check=meta(c,'consent_epoch')
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=1 WHERE k="consent"')
        c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=v+1 WHERE k="consent_epoch"')
        c.execute('COMMIT')
        # A cursor minted before consent revoke never gets to continue, even after re-grant.
        old,_,_,scanold=query_page(c,cur,k=16)
        ok=(a==1 and check==e+1 and state=='DENIED' and not refs and scan==0 and old=='STALE_CURSOR' and scanold==0)
        return ok,f'fresh_read={state} scanned={scan} regrant_old_cursor={old} regrant_scan={scanold}'
    if mode=='CONSENT_TOCTOU_NEGATIVE':
        # A direct reader that checked consent before revocation can still access raw data.
        pre=meta(c,'consent');assert pre==1
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=0 WHERE k="consent"');c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=v+1 WHERE k="consent_epoch"');c.execute('COMMIT')
        raw=c.execute('SELECT payload FROM records WHERE slot=0').fetchone()[0]
        scoped,refs,_,scans=query_page(c,k=16)
        c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=1 WHERE k="consent"');c.execute('UPDATE meta SET v=v+1 WHERE k="consent_epoch"');c.execute('COMMIT')
        vulnerable=(len(raw)==4096 and scoped=='DENIED' and not refs and scans==0)
        return vulnerable,f'NEGATIVE_FINDING precheck_was_allowed={pre} revoked_raw_still_accessible={len(raw)==4096} fresh_cooperative_check={scoped} (not_OS_isolation)'
    if mode=='FULL_4096_INTEGRITY_AFTER_CHURN':
        c.execute('BEGIN')
        try:
            n=0
            for s,g,b,d in c.execute('SELECT slot,generation,payload,payload_sha FROM records ORDER BY slot'):
                if len(b)!=4096 or sha(b)!=d or b!=pay(s,g):raise AssertionError(f'BAD_SLOT {s}')
                n+=1
            ver=meta(c,'version');a=c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit').fetchone()[0]
            c.execute('COMMIT')
        except BaseException:c.execute('ROLLBACK');raise
        check=c.execute('PRAGMA quick_check').fetchone()[0]
        ok=(n==N and a==ver and check=='ok')
        return ok,f'full_scan={n} audit_rows={a} version={ver} sqlite_quick_check={check}'
    raise RuntimeError('UNKNOWN_MODE '+mode)

def runstage(log,ctx,binary,root,nworkers,host,inject_abort):
    tag=f'S5x{nworkers}_RAM32';expected=len(MODES)*2
    stage_start=time.monotonic();stop=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();out=ctx.Queue();ram_ready=ctx.Queue()
    workers=[];ram=None;conn=None;cases=0;failed=0;negative=0;why=None;metrics=[];workers_good=0
    prepss=smaps();pre_rss=proc_rss()
    try:
        ram=ctx.Process(target=ramholder,args=(stop,ram_ready));ram.start()
        if ram_ready.get(timeout=10) is not True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAILED')
        for _ in range(nworkers):
            p=ctx.Process(target=s5worker,args=(binary,stop,ready,out));p.start();workers.append(p)
        if len(set(ready.get(timeout=25) for _ in workers))!=nworkers:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_NOT_ALL_ACTIVE')
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={tag} workers={nworkers}')
        stage_dir=root/tag;stage_dir.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False);db=stage_dir/'v18_concurrent.sqlite3'
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={tag} expected={expected} slots={N} background_S5={nworkers} held_RAM_MiB=32')
        conn=setup(db,host,log)
        for rep in range(1,3):
            for mode in MODES:
                if reason:=guard(host):why=reason;break
                if inject_abort and cases>=3:why='INJECTED_HOST_SAFETY_ABORT';break
                start=time.monotonic()
                try:
                    ok,detail=mode_case(mode,conn,db,ctx,host,log)
                except BaseException as e:
                    if str(e).startswith('SAFETY_'):why=str(e);break
                    ok=False;detail=f'{type(e).__name__} {str(e)[:240]}'
                ms=(time.monotonic()-start)*1000;metrics.append(ms);cases+=1
                if not ok:failed+=1
                if mode=='CONSENT_TOCTOU_NEGATIVE' and ok:negative+=1
                status=('PASS_EXPECTED_NEGATIVE' if mode=='CONSENT_TOCTOU_NEGATIVE' and ok else 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
                log(f'CASE stage={tag} rep={rep} mode={mode} status={status} {detail} elapsed_ms={ms:.3f}')
            if why:break
        if conn:
            integrity=conn.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
            if integrity!='ok':failed+=1
            log(f'STORAGE_CHECK stage={tag} integrity={integrity} rows={conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM records").fetchone()[0]} audit={conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit").fetchone()[0]}')
            wal=Path(str(db)+'-wal');walsize=wal.stat().st_size if wal.exists() else 0
            log(f'WAL_MEASURE stage={tag} before_final_checkpoint_bytes={walsize} sqlite_db_bytes={db.stat().st_size} own_PSS_kB={smaps()} own_RSS_kB={proc_rss()}')
            conn.execute('PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)')
            log(f'WAL_CHECKPOINT_END stage={tag} after_bytes={wal.stat().st_size if wal.exists() else 0}')
        floor=1.2 if host else 24 if nworkers==2 else 30
        while not why and time.monotonic()-stage_start<floor:
            if reason:=guard(host):why=reason;break
            time.sleep(.15)
    except BaseException as e:
        if str(e).startswith('SAFETY_'):why=str(e)
        else:
            failed+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={tag} error={type(e).__name__} detail={str(e)[:260]}')
    finally:
        if conn:conn.close()
        stop.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(4)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
        if ram:
            ram.join(4)
            if ram.is_alive():ram.kill();ram.join(3)
        for _ in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,minval,errors=out.get(timeout=3)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} pid={pid} native_runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean:.5f} min_GMAC_s={minval:.5f} errors={errors}')
                if runs>0 and not errors:workers_good+=1
                else:failed+=1
            except queue.Empty:failed+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={tag} RESULT_MISSING')
        for q in (ready,out,ram_ready):q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
    state='SAFETY_ABORT' if why else 'COMPLETE' if cases==expected and failed==0 and workers_good==nworkers and negative==2 else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL'
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={tag} status={state} cases={cases}/{expected} failures={failed} expected_negative_findings={negative} workers_ok={workers_good}/{nworkers} max_case_ms={max(metrics) if metrics else None} safety_reason={why or "-"}')
    log(f'PROCESS_MEMORY stage={tag} own_PSS_before_kB={prepss} own_PSS_after_kB={smaps()} own_RSS_before_kB={pre_rss} own_RSS_after_kB={proc_rss()} NOTE=single_process_not_total_app_peak')
    log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={tag} {telemetry()}')
    return state,cases,negative

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true',help='developer Linux-only smoke')
    p.add_argument('--native-binary',help='developer smoke only; not allowed on phone')
    p.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true',help='developer smoke only')
    a=p.parse_args()
    if (a.native_binary or a.inject_host_abort) and not a.host_smoke:raise SystemExit('HOST_ONLY_OPTIONS')
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_WAL_CONCURRENT_MEMORY_V18_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- CONCURRENT 4096-SLOT SQLITE WAL / CURSOR / REVOCATION LAB UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('One cooperative SQLite writer, concurrent snapshot readers, 4096 actual 4096B records. WAL FULL, real PSS and WAL size measurement. No real private data.')
            log('Charging supported WITH unchanged hardware guards; battery status is observed, not proof of charger thermal safety.')
            log('NEGATIVE consent check-before-read race is NOT fixed by cooperative SQLite; Android trust boundary and physical STOP remain untested.')
            log('No weight training, P updates, production code, Binder, OS dispatch, real microphone, readback or power cut.')
            log('SAFETY battery>=40% temp<37.5C MemAvailable>=400000kB. SAFETY_ABORT != COMPLETE.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE; PRODUCTION_CODE_AUTHORIZED = FALSE')
            log(f'PRECHECK {telemetry()}')
            if reason:=guard(a.host_smoke):log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+reason);return 3
            root_parent=home/'uno_a52_r41_wal_concurrent_v18'
            root_parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            if not a.host_smoke and shutil.disk_usage(root_parent).free<192*MIB:
                log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP DISK_FREE_BELOW_192_MiB');return 3
            binary=native_binary(a.host_smoke,a.native_binary);log('VERIFIED_BACKGROUND_NATIVE_S5 '+str(binary))
            root=root_parent/stamp;root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            one=runstage(log,ctx,binary,root,1 if a.host_smoke else 2,a.host_smoke,a.inject_host_abort)
            if one[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {one[0]} completed={one[1]}');return 3
            two=runstage(log,ctx,binary,root,2 if a.host_smoke else 4,a.host_smoke,False)
            if two[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {two[0]} completed={one[1]+two[1]}');return 3
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_CONCURRENT_WAL_LAB stages=2/2 cases={one[1]+two[1]} expected_negative_findings={one[2]+two[2]}')
            log('LIMIT: local SQLite snapshot guarantees != Android permission isolation or atomic permission/read operation; no hard real-time deadline, energy, power cut or full Android RSS proof.')
            return 0
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:260]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc()[-1500:]);return 2
        finally:log('REPORT '+str(report))
if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""UNO R4.1 V19 isolated causal clock / timer / one-shot outbox lab.

Virtual event time is used for deterministic S2/S3 ordering; actual Android
monotonic timer jitter is measured separately under continuously active native S5.
No production agent, Android action or authority is implemented.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import datetime as dt
import heapq
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import traceback

VERSION='UNO_R41_A52_NATIVE_S5_CAUSAL_CLOCK_TIMER_RACE_V19'
MIB=1048576
MODES=(
 'TIMER_BEFORE_EXTERNAL', 'NEW_EARLIER_TIMER', 'STOP_PRECEDES_DUE_TIMER',
 'REVOKE_PRECEDES_OWNER', 'DEATH_PRECEDES_SENSOR', 'DEADLINE_PRECEDES_OUTBOX',
 'READBACK_PRECEDES_OWNER', 'EQUAL_PRIORITY_SERIAL', 'IMMEDIATE_TIMER_MICROSTEP',
 'REVOKE_PENDING_OUTBOX', 'OUTBOX_SINGLE_CONSUME', 'STOP_AFTER_ISSUE',
 'NO_EVENTS_NO_NEURAL_TICKS', 'BOOT_INVALIDATES_OLD_TIMER',
)
PRIORITY={'STOP':0,'REVOKE':0,'DEATH':0,'BOOT':0,'DEADLINE':1,
          'READBACK':2,'OWNER':3,'ARM_TIMER':3,'SENSOR':4,'TIMER':5}
CONTROL={'STOP','REVOKE','DEATH','BOOT','DEADLINE'}


def readnum(path):
    try:return int(Path(path).read_text().strip())
    except (OSError,ValueError):return None

def telemetry():
    try:mem=int(next(x for x in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('MemAvailable:')).split()[1])
    except (OSError,ValueError,StopIteration,IndexError):mem=None
    try:charge=Path('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/status').read_text().strip()
    except OSError:charge='UNAVAILABLE'
    return {'battery':readnum('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'),
            'battery_temp_deciC':readnum('/sys/class/power_supply/battery/temp'),
            'MemAvailable_kB':mem,'charge':charge}

def guard(host):
    if host:return None
    a=telemetry();b=a['battery'];t=a['battery_temp_deciC'];m=a['MemAvailable_kB']
    if None in (b,t,m) or not 100<=t<=800:return 'SAFETY_TELEMETRY_UNAVAILABLE'
    if b<40:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_BELOW_40_PERCENT'
    if t>=375:return 'SAFETY_BATTERY_TEMP_GE_37_5C'
    if m<400000:return 'SAFETY_MEMAVAILABLE_LT_400000_KB'
    return None

def native_binary(host,override):
    p=Path(override) if host and override else Path.home()/'uno_a52_r41_operator_lab'/'uno_r41_s5_operator'
    if not p.is_file() or not os.access(p,os.X_OK):raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_MISSING '+str(p))
    r=subprocess.run([str(p),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=15)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_CONTRACT_NOT_PASS '+r.stdout[-180:])
    return p

def s5worker(binary,stop,ready,out):
    values=[];errors=[]
    try:
        while not stop.is_set():
            r=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','full','0.70'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=18)
            if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
                raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_WORKER_FAILED')
            m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',r.stdout)
            if not m:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_S5_THROUGHPUT_MISSING')
            values.append(float(m[1]))
            if len(values)==1:ready.put(os.getpid(),timeout=4)
    except BaseException as e:errors.append(str(e)[:160])
    try:out.put((os.getpid(),len(values),statistics.mean(values) if values else None,
                 min(values) if values else None,errors),timeout=3)
    except BaseException:pass

def ramholder(stop,ready):
    try:
        a=bytearray(32*MIB)
        for i in range(0,len(a),4096):a[i]=1
        ready.put(True,timeout=4)
        while not stop.wait(0.15):
            for i in range(0,len(a),262144):a[i]^=1
    except BaseException:
        try:ready.put(False,timeout=3)
        except BaseException:pass

# Event = tuple(time_monotonic_virtual_ms, priority, source_serial, insertion_id, type, payload)
class Scheduler:
    def __init__(self):
        self.heap=[];self.counter=0;self.now=0;self.stop=False;self.revoke=False
        self.dead=False;self.deadline=False;self.boot=1;self.outbox=None
        self.traces=[];self.neural=[];self.issue=[];self.consumed=0
        self.denied=0;self.quarantine=0;self.micro=0;self.times=[]
    def push(self,t,kind,serial=0,payload=None):
        if not isinstance(t,int) or t<self.now or kind not in PRIORITY:raise ValueError('BAD_EVENT')
        self.counter+=1
        heapq.heappush(self.heap,(t,PRIORITY[kind],serial,self.counter,kind,payload))
    def run(self):
        processed=0
        while self.heap:
            if processed>=64:raise RuntimeError('EVENT_LIMIT')
            t=self.heap[0][0]
            if t<self.now:raise AssertionError('CAUSAL_TIME_WENT_BACKWARD')
            if t==self.now and self.times:self.micro+=1
            self.now=t;self.times.append(t)
            # Drain same-time external/control/due events before consuming the *previous* outbox.
            # A newly scheduled timer at current time becomes a zero-duration microstep.
            batch=[]
            while self.heap and self.heap[0][0]==t:
                batch.append(heapq.heappop(self.heap))
            batch.sort()
            controls=[e for e in batch if e[4] in CONTROL]
            others=[e for e in batch if e[4] not in CONTROL]
            for e in controls:
                _,_,serial,_,kind,payload=e
                self.traces.append(f'{kind}@{t}#{serial}')
                if kind=='STOP':self.stop=True
                elif kind=='REVOKE':self.revoke=True
                elif kind=='DEATH':self.dead=True
                elif kind=='DEADLINE':self.deadline=True
                elif kind=='BOOT':self.boot+=1
                processed+=1
            if self.outbox is not None:
                self.consumed+=1
                if not (self.stop or self.revoke or self.dead or self.deadline):
                    self.issue.append((t,self.outbox))  # synthetic request, NOT OS effect
                else:self.denied+=1
                self.outbox=None
            for e in others:
                _,_,serial,_,kind,payload=e
                self.traces.append(f'{kind}@{t}#{serial}')
                if kind=='ARM_TIMER':
                    # (due_time, boot_generation) is an internal timer, not an OS permission
                    due=payload if payload is not None else t
                    self.push(due,'TIMER',serial+1,self.boot)
                elif kind=='OWNER':
                    if self.stop or self.revoke or self.dead or self.deadline:self.denied+=1
                    elif self.outbox is None:self.outbox=f'outbox_from_{t}_{serial}'
                    else:raise AssertionError('UNCONSUMED_OUTBOX_OVERWRITE')
                elif kind in ('SENSOR','TIMER'):
                    if kind=='TIMER' and payload!=self.boot:self.quarantine+=1
                    elif not (self.stop or self.revoke or self.dead or self.deadline):
                        self.neural.append((t,kind,serial))
                elif kind=='READBACK':pass
                processed+=1
        # Crucial: idle does NOT consume newly created outbox or generate a neural tick.
        return self

def scenario(mode):
    s=Scheduler()
    if mode=='TIMER_BEFORE_EXTERNAL':
        s.push(5,'TIMER',1,1);s.push(10,'SENSOR',2)
        expected=['TIMER@5#1','SENSOR@10#2'];nf=2;ni=0
    elif mode=='NEW_EARLIER_TIMER':
        s.push(10,'TIMER',1,1);s.push(2,'ARM_TIMER',2,4)
        expected=['ARM_TIMER@2#2','TIMER@4#3','TIMER@10#1'];nf=2;ni=0
    elif mode=='STOP_PRECEDES_DUE_TIMER':
        s.push(5,'TIMER',2,1);s.push(5,'STOP',1)
        expected=['STOP@5#1','TIMER@5#2'];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='REVOKE_PRECEDES_OWNER':
        s.push(5,'OWNER',2);s.push(5,'REVOKE',1)
        expected=['REVOKE@5#1','OWNER@5#2'];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='DEATH_PRECEDES_SENSOR':
        s.push(5,'SENSOR',2);s.push(5,'DEATH',1)
        expected=['DEATH@5#1','SENSOR@5#2'];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='DEADLINE_PRECEDES_OUTBOX':
        s.push(1,'OWNER',1);s.push(2,'SENSOR',2);s.push(2,'DEADLINE',1)
        expected=['OWNER@1#1','DEADLINE@2#1','SENSOR@2#2'];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='READBACK_PRECEDES_OWNER':
        s.push(5,'OWNER',2);s.push(5,'READBACK',1)
        expected=['READBACK@5#1','OWNER@5#2'];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='EQUAL_PRIORITY_SERIAL':
        s.push(5,'SENSOR',20);s.push(5,'SENSOR',3)
        expected=['SENSOR@5#3','SENSOR@5#20'];nf=2;ni=0
    elif mode=='IMMEDIATE_TIMER_MICROSTEP':
        s.push(4,'ARM_TIMER',1,4);s.push(8,'SENSOR',3)
        expected=['ARM_TIMER@4#1','TIMER@4#2','SENSOR@8#3'];nf=2;ni=0
    elif mode=='REVOKE_PENDING_OUTBOX':
        s.push(1,'OWNER',1);s.push(2,'REVOKE',1);s.push(3,'SENSOR',1)
        expected=['OWNER@1#1','REVOKE@2#1','SENSOR@3#1'];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='OUTBOX_SINGLE_CONSUME':
        s.push(1,'OWNER',1);s.push(2,'SENSOR',1);s.push(3,'SENSOR',2)
        expected=['OWNER@1#1','SENSOR@2#1','SENSOR@3#2'];nf=2;ni=1
    elif mode=='STOP_AFTER_ISSUE':
        s.push(1,'OWNER',1);s.push(2,'SENSOR',1);s.push(3,'STOP',1)
        expected=['OWNER@1#1','SENSOR@2#1','STOP@3#1'];nf=1;ni=1
    elif mode=='NO_EVENTS_NO_NEURAL_TICKS':
        expected=[];nf=0;ni=0
    elif mode=='BOOT_INVALIDATES_OLD_TIMER':
        s.push(2,'BOOT',1);s.push(5,'TIMER',1,1);s.push(6,'SENSOR',1)
        expected=['BOOT@2#1','TIMER@5#1','SENSOR@6#1'];nf=1;ni=0
    else:raise ValueError('UNKNOWN_SCENARIO')
    s.run()
    ok=(s.traces==expected and len(s.neural)==nf and len(s.issue)==ni
        and s.consumed==(1 if mode in ('DEADLINE_PRECEDES_OUTBOX','REVOKE_PENDING_OUTBOX',
                                      'OUTBOX_SINGLE_CONSUME','STOP_AFTER_ISSUE') else 0)
        and (mode!='BOOT_INVALIDATES_OLD_TIMER' or s.quarantine==1)
        and (mode!='NO_EVENTS_NO_NEURAL_TICKS' or s.times==[])
        and (mode!='IMMEDIATE_TIMER_MICROSTEP' or s.micro>=1))
    # An intentionally WRONG external-only clock would jump to the next external arrival,
    # skipping d_R=5 before external SENSOR at t=10, i.e. it violates R4.1 C01.
    naive_skip=(mode=='TIMER_BEFORE_EXTERNAL' and s.times[0]==5 or
                mode=='NEW_EARLIER_TIMER' and s.times[:3]==[2,4,10])
    return ok,s,naive_skip

class TimerJitter:
    def __init__(self):self.data=[];self.stop=threading.Event();self.thread=None
    def start(self):
        def f():
            # Real monotonic scheduling, NOT a hard-realtime OS guarantee.
            base=time.monotonic_ns()+10_000_000
            for n in range(18):
                if self.stop.is_set():break
                target=base+n*20_000_000
                remaining=(target-time.monotonic_ns())/1e9
                if remaining>0:self.stop.wait(remaining)
                if self.stop.is_set():break
                self.data.append((time.monotonic_ns()-target)/1e6)
        self.thread=threading.Thread(target=f,daemon=True);self.thread.start()
    def finish(self):
        if self.thread:
            self.thread.join(3)
            if self.thread.is_alive():self.stop.set();self.thread.join(1)
        return list(self.data)

def native_pulse(binary):
    r=subprocess.run([str(binary),'rank','prefix2','0.30'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=15)
    if r.returncode or not re.search(r'FIXED_MASK_CONTRACT[^\n]*status=PASS',r.stdout):
        raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_F_PULSE_NOT_PASS')
    m=re.search(r'estimated_F_GMAC_s=([\d.]+)',r.stdout)
    if not m:raise RuntimeError('NATIVE_F_PULSE_MISSING_THROUGHPUT')
    return float(m[1])

def stage(log,ctx,binary,nworker,host,inject):
    label=f'S5x{nworker}_RAM32';expect=len(MODES)*2
    stop=ctx.Event();ready=ctx.Queue();out=ctx.Queue();ram_ready=ctx.Queue()
    workers=[];ram=None;jitter=TimerJitter();start=time.monotonic()
    cases=0;failures=0;negative=0;native_cases=0;workers_good=0;why=None;last_details=[]
    try:
        ram=ctx.Process(target=ramholder,args=(stop,ram_ready));ram.start()
        if ram_ready.get(timeout=10)!=True:raise RuntimeError('RAM_HOLDER_FAIL')
        for i in range(nworker):
            p=ctx.Process(target=s5worker,args=(binary,stop,ready,out));p.start();workers.append(p)
        if len(set(ready.get(timeout=25) for p in workers))!=nworker:raise RuntimeError('BACKGROUND_S5_NOT_ACTIVE')
        log(f'NATIVE_S5_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE stage={label} workers={nworker}')
        log(f'STAGE_BEGIN stage={label} scenarios={expect} background_S5={nworker} held_RAM_MiB=32')
        jitter.start()
        for rep in (1,2):
            for mode in MODES:
                if reason:=guard(host):why=reason;break
                if inject and cases>=3:why='INJECTED_HOST_SAFETY_ABORT';break
                t0=time.monotonic()
                try:
                    ok,s,neg=scenario(mode)
                    # The data/timer paths invoke the existing native S5 operator in an
                    # isolated process. This is NOT the production controller executing F.
                    throughput=native_pulse(binary) if s.neural else None
                    if s.neural:native_cases+=1
                except BaseException as e:
                    if 'SAFETY_' in str(e):why=str(e);break
                    ok=False;s=None;neg=False;throughput=None
                    detail=f'ERROR={type(e).__name__}:{str(e)[:100]}'
                else:
                    detail=(f'events={len(s.traces)} virtual_neural={len(s.neural)} '
                            f'outbox_issues={len(s.issue)} outbox_consumed={s.consumed} '
                            f'quarantined_old_timer={s.quarantine} microsteps={s.micro} '
                            f'last_virtual_ms={s.now} native_F_GMAC_s={throughput} '
                            f'causal_order={"OK" if ok else "FAIL"}')
                cases+=1;negative+=int(neg);failures+=int(not ok)
                status='FAIL' if not ok else 'PASS_C01_NEGATIVE_CONTROL' if neg else 'PASS'
                log(f'CASE stage={label} rep={rep} mode={mode} status={status} {detail} elapsed_ms={(time.monotonic()-t0)*1000:.3f}')
                last_details.append((mode,ok))
            if why:break
        jitter_values=jitter.finish()
        if reason:=guard(host):why=reason
        log(f'CLOCK_JITTER stage={label} samples={len(jitter_values)} '
            f'max_late_ms={round(max(jitter_values),3) if jitter_values else None} '
            f'median_late_ms={round(statistics.median(jitter_values),3) if jitter_values else None} '
            f'min_late_ms={round(min(jitter_values),3) if jitter_values else None} '
            f'NOTE=real_clock_not_hard_realtime')
        if not why and len(jitter_values)!=18:failures+=1
        floor=1 if host else (24 if nworker==2 else 30)
        while not why and time.monotonic()-start<floor:
            if reason:=guard(host):why=reason;break
            time.sleep(0.15)
    except BaseException as e:
        if str(e).startswith('SAFETY_'):why=str(e)
        else:failures+=1;log(f'STAGE_ERROR stage={label} type={type(e).__name__} detail={str(e)[:200]}')
    finally:
        jitter.stop.set();jitter.finish();stop.set()
        for p in workers:
            p.join(5)
            if p.is_alive():p.kill();p.join(3)
        if ram:
            ram.join(4)
            if ram.is_alive():ram.kill();ram.join(2)
        for p in workers:
            try:
                pid,runs,mean,minimum,errors=out.get(timeout=3)
                log(f'S5_WORKER stage={label} pid={pid} runs={runs} mean_GMAC_s={mean} min_GMAC_s={minimum} errors={errors}')
                if runs>0 and not errors:workers_good+=1
                else:failures+=1
            except queue.Empty:failures+=1;log(f'S5_WORKER stage={label} RESULT_MISSING')
        for q in (ready,out,ram_ready):q.cancel_join_thread();q.close()
    status='SAFETY_ABORT' if why else ('COMPLETE' if cases==expect and failures==0 and negative==4
                                and workers_good==nworker else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL')
    log(f'STAGE_SUMMARY stage={label} status={status} cases={cases}/{expect} failures={failures} '
        f'c01_naive_skip_negative_findings={negative} native_F_case_invocations={native_cases} '
        f'background_workers_ok={workers_good}/{nworker} safety_reason={why or "-"}')
    log(f'STAGE_TELEMETRY stage={label} {telemetry()}')
    return status,cases,negative

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument('--host-smoke',action='store_true')
    p.add_argument('--native-binary')
    p.add_argument('--inject-host-abort',action='store_true')
    a=p.parse_args()
    if (a.native_binary or a.inject_host_abort) and not a.host_smoke:raise SystemExit('HOST_ONLY_OPTIONS')
    home=Path.home();downloads=home/'storage'/'downloads'
    if not downloads.is_dir() or not os.access(downloads,os.W_OK):downloads=home
    stamp=dt.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
    report=downloads/f'UNO_A52_R41_CAUSAL_CLOCK_TIMER_V19_{stamp}.txt'
    with report.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        def log(s):print(s,flush=True);f.write(s+'\n');f.flush()
        try:
            log('UNO / UNOVYRA -- CAUSAL CLOCK, DUE TIMERS, PRIORITY CONTROL & ONE-SHOT OUTBOX UNDER NATIVE S5 LOAD '+VERSION)
            log('R4.1 S2/S3/C01: virtual monotonic causal event scheduler, due timers, simultaneous control priority and one-shot outbox; real monotonic Android timer jitter measured separately.')
            log('Synthetic controller and request issuance, NOT OS action/owner authentication/Android Binder, physical STOP, hard deadline, model training, production runtime.')
            log('Actual native ARM S5 F invoked in neural-event cases while 2/4 additional S5 processes run continuously plus 32MiB RAM. No idle load stage.')
            log('Battery charger allowed but 40% battery, 37.5C battery temperature and 400000kB free memory guards unchanged.')
            log('OWNER_APPROVED_PRECODE = FALSE; PRODUCTION_CODE_AUTHORIZED = FALSE')
            log(f'PRECHECK {telemetry()}')
            if reason:=guard(a.host_smoke):log('RESULT SAFETY_SKIP '+reason);return 3
            binary=native_binary(a.host_smoke,a.native_binary);log('VERIFIED_NATIVE_S5 '+str(binary))
            ctx=mp.get_context('fork')
            first=stage(log,ctx,binary,1 if a.host_smoke else 2,a.host_smoke,a.inject_host_abort)
            if first[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {first[0]} partial_cases={first[1]}');return 3
            second=stage(log,ctx,binary,2 if a.host_smoke else 4,a.host_smoke,False)
            if second[0]!='COMPLETE':log(f'RESULT {second[0]} partial_cases={first[1]+second[1]}');return 3
            log(f'RESULT COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CLOCK_TIMER_LAB cases={first[1]+second[1]} '
                f'naive_timer_skip_negatives={first[2]+second[2]} stages=2/2')
            log('LIMIT: virtual event schedule is deterministic fixture; real timer jitter measurements have no guaranteed bound. Synthetic issued request is NOT OS effect.')
            return 0
        except BaseException as e:
            log(f'RESULT ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:240]}')
            log('TRACE '+traceback.format_exc()[-1500:]);return 2
        finally:log('REPORT '+str(report))
if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

 'STOP_DURING_NATIVE_F','REVOKE_DURING_NATIVE_F','BOOT_DURING_NATIVE_F',
 'DEADLINE_DURING_NATIVE_F','DUE_TIMER_DURING_NATIVE_F',
